(11.1.1’) agne jāyasvāditir nāthiteyaṃ brahmaudanaṃ pacati putrakāmā |
(11.1.1.c) saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛtas te tvā manthantu prajayā saheha ‖1‖
(11.1.2’) kṛṇuta dhūmaṃ vṛṣaṇaḥ sakhāyo 'droghāvitā vācam acha |
(11.1.2.c) ayam agniḥ pṛtanāṣāt suvīro yena devā asahanta dasyūn ‖2‖
(11.1.3’) agne 'janiṣṭhā mahate vīryāya brahmaudanāya paktave jātavedaḥ |
(11.1.3.c) saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛtas te tvājījanann asyai rayiṃ sarvavīraṃ ni yacha ‖3‖
(11.1.4’) samiddho agne samidhā sam idhyasva vidvān devān yajñiyā;m eha vakṣaḥ |
(11.1.4.c) tebhyo haviḥ śrapayaṃ jātaveda uttamaṃ nākam adhi rohayemam ‖4‖
(11.1.5’) tredhā bhāgo nihito yaḥ purā vo devānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ martyānām |
(11.1.5.c) aṃśān jānīdhvaṃ vi bhajāmi tān vo yo devānāṃ sa imāṃ pārayāti ‖5‖
(11.1.6’) agne sahasvān abhibhūr abhīd asi nīco ny ubja dviṣataḥ sapatnān |
(11.1.6.c) iyaṃ mātrā mīyamānā mitā ca sajātāṃs te balihṛtaḥ kṛṇotu  ‖6‖
(11.1.7’) sākaṃ sajātaiḥ payasā sahaidhy ud ubjaināṃ mahate vīryāya |
(11.1.7.c) ūrdhvo nākasyādhi roha viṣṭapaṃ svargo loka iti yaṃ vadanti	‖7‖
(11.1.8’) iyaṃ mahī prati gṛhṇātu carma pṛthivī devī sumanasyamānā |
(11.1.8.c) atha gachema sukṛtasya lokam  ‖8‖
(11.1.9’) etau grāvāṇau sayujā yuṅgdhi carmaṇi nirbindhy aṃśūn yajamānāya sādhu |
(11.1.9.c) nir avaghnatī ni jahi ya imāṃ pṛtanyava ūrdhvaṃ prajām udbharanty ud ūha  ‖9‖
(11.1.10’) gṛhāṇa grāvāṇau sakṛtau vīra hasta ā te devā yajñiyā yajñam aguḥ |
(11.1.10.c) trayo varā yatamāṃs tvaṃ vṛṇīṣe tās te samṛddhīr iha rādhayāmi ‖10‖ {1}
(11.1.11’) iyaṃ te dhītir idam u te janitraṃ gṛhṇātu tvām aditiḥ śūraputrā |
(11.1.11.c) parā punīhi ya imāṃ pṛtanyavo 'syai rayiṃ sarvavīraṃ ni yacha ‖11‖
(11.1.12’) upaśvase druvaye sīdatā yūyaṃ vi vicyadhvaṃ yajñiyāsas tuṣaiḥ |
(11.1.12.c) śriyā samānān ati sarvānt syāmādhaspadaṃ dviṣatas pādayāmi ‖12‖
(11.1.13’) parehi nāri punar ehi kṣipram apāṃ tvā goṣṭho 'dhy arukṣad bharāya |
(11.1.13.c) tāsāṃ gṛhṇītād yatamā yajñiyā asan vibhājya dhīrītarā jahītāt  ‖13‖
(11.1.14’) emā agur yoṣitaḥ śumbhamānā ut tiṣṭha nāri tavasaṃ rabhasva |
(11.1.14.c) supatnī patyā prajayā prajāvaty ā tvāgan yajñaḥ prati kumbhaṃ gṛbhāya  ‖14‖
(11.1.15’) ūrjo bhāgo nihito yaḥ purā va ṛṣipraśiṣṭāpa ā bharaitāḥ  |
(11.1.15.c) ayaṃ yajo gātuvin nāthavit prajāvid ugraḥ paśuvid vīravid vo astu  ‖15‖
(11.1.16’) agne carur yajñiyas tvādhy arukṣac chucis tapiṣṭhas tapasā tapainam |
(11.1.16.c) ārṣeyā daivā abhisaṃgatya bhāgam emaṃ tapiṣṭhā ṛtubhis tapantu ‖16‖
(11.1.17’) śuddhāḥ putā yoṣito yajñiyā imā āpaś carum ava sarpantu śubhrāḥ |
(11.1.17.c) aduḥ prajāṃ bahulān paśūn naḥ paktaudanasya sukṛtām etu lokam ‖17‖
(11.1.18’) brahmaṇā śuddhā uta pūtā ghṛtena somasyāṃśavas taṇḍulā yajñiyā ime |
(11.1.18.c) apaḥ pra viśata prati gṛhṇātu vaś carur imaṃ paktvā sukṛtām eta lokam ‖18‖
(11.1.19’) uruḥ prathasva mahatā mahimnā sahasrapṛṣṭhaḥ sukṛtasya loke |
(11.1.19.c) pitāmahāḥ pitaraḥ prajopajāhaṃ paktā pañcadaśas te asmi ‖19‖
(11.1.20’) sahasrapṛṣṭhaḥ śatadhāro akṣito brahmaudano devayānaḥ svargaḥ |
(11.1.20.c) amūṃs ta ā dadhāmi prajayā reṣayainān balihārāya mṛḍatān mahyam eva ‖20‖ {2}
(11.1.21’) udehi vediṃ prajayā vardhayaināṃ nudasva rakṣaḥ prataraṃ dhehy enām |
(11.1.21.c) śriyā samānān ati sarvānt syāmādhaspadaṃ dviṣatas pādayāmi ‖21‖
(11.1.22’) abhyāvartasva paśubhiḥ sahaināṃ pratyaṅ enāṃ devatābhiḥ sahaidhi |
(11.1.22.c) mā tvā prāpac chapatho mābhicāraḥ sve kṣetre anamīvā vi rāja ‖22‖
(11.1.23’) ṛtena taṣṭā manasā hitaiṣā brahmaudanasya vihitā vedir agre |
(11.1.23.c) aṃsadrīṃ śuddhām upa dhehi nāri tatraudanaṃ sādaya daivānām ‖23‖
(11.1.24’) aditer hastāṃ srucam etāṃ dvitīyāṃ saptaṛṣayo bhūtakṛto yām akṛṇvan |
(11.1.24.c) sā gātrāṇi viduṣy odanasya darvir vedyām adhy enaṃ cinotu ‖24‖
(11.1.25’) śṛtaṃ tvā havyam upa sīdantu daivā niḥsṛpyāgneḥ punar enān pra sīda |
(11.1.25.c) somena pūto jathare sīda brahmaṇām ārṣeyās te mā riṣan prāśitāraḥ ‖25‖
(11.1.26’) soma rājant saṃjñānam ā vapaibhyaḥ subrāhmaṇā yatame tvopasīdān |
(11.1.26.c) ṛṣīn ārṣeyāṃs tapaso 'dhi jātān brahmaudane suhavā johavīmi ‖26‖
(11.1.27’) śuddhāḥ pūtā yosito yajñiyā imā brahmaṇāṃ hasteṣu prapṛthaḥ sādayāmi |
(11.1.27.c) yatkāma idam abhiṣiñcāmi vo 'ham indro marutvānt sa dadāt idam me ‖27‖
(11.1.28’) idaṃ me jyotir amṛtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ pakvaṃ kṣetrāt kāmadughā ma eṣā |
(11.1.28.c) idaṃ dhanaṃ ni dadhe brāhmaṇeṣu kṛṇve panthāṃ pitṛṣu yaḥ svargaḥ ‖28‖
(11.1.29’) agnau tuṣān ā vapa jātavedasi paraḥ kambūkā;m apa mṛḍḍhi dūram |
(11.1.29.c) etaṃ śuśruma gṛharājasya bhāgam atho vidma nirṛter bhāgadheyam ‖29‖
(11.1.30’) śrāmyataḥ pacato viddhi sunvataḥ panthāṃ svargam adhi rohayainam |
(11.1.30.c) yena rohāt param āpadya yad vaya uttamaṃ nākaṃ paramaṃ vyoma ‖30‖ {3}
(11.1.31’) babhrer adhvaryo mukham etad vi mṛḍḍhy ājyāya lokaṃ kṛṇuhi pravidvān |
(11.1.31.c) ghṛtena gātrānu sarvā vi mṛḍḍhi kṛṇve panthāṃ pitṛṣu yaḥ svargaḥ ‖31‖
(11.1.32’) babhre rakṣaḥ samadam ā vapaibhyo 'brāhmaṇā yatame tvopasīdān |
(11.1.32.c) purīṣiṇaḥ prathamānāḥ purastād ārṣeyās te mā riṣan prāśitāraḥ ‖32‖
(11.1.33’) ārṣeyeṣu ni dadha odana tvā nānārṣeyāṇām apy asty atra |
(11.1.33.c) agnir me goptā marutaś ca sarve viśve devā abhi rakṣantu pakvam ‖33‖
(11.1.34’) yajñaṃ duhānaṃ sadam it prapīnaṃ pumāṃsaṃ dhenuṃ sadanaṃ rayīṇām |
(11.1.34.c) prajāmṛtatvam uta dīrgham āyu rāyaś ca poṣair upa tvā sadema ‖34‖
(11.1.35’) vṛṣabho 'si svarga ṛṣīn ārṣeyān gacha |
(11.1.35.c) sukṛtāṃ loke sīda tatra nau saṃskṛtam ‖35‖
(11.1.36’) samācinuṣvānusamprayāhy agne pathaḥ kalpaya devayānān |
(11.1.36.c) etaiḥ sukṛtair anu gachema yajñaṃ nāke tiṣṭhantam adhi saptaraśmau ‖36‖
(11.1.37’) yena devā jyotiṣā dyām udāyan brahmaudanaṃ paktvā sukṛtasya lokam |
(11.1.37.c) tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokaṃ svar ārohanto abhi nākam uttamam ‖37‖ {4}

(11.2.1’) bhavāśarvau mṛḍataṃ mābhi yātaṃ bhūtapatī paśupatī namo vām |
(11.2.1.c) pratihitām āyatāṃ mā vi srāṣṭam mā no hiṃsiṣṭaṃ dvipado mā catuṣpadaḥ ‖1‖
(11.2.2’) śune kroṣṭre mā śarīrāṇi kartam aliklavebhyo gṛdhrebhyo ye ca kṛṣṇā aviṣyavaḥ |
(11.2.2.c) makṣikās te paśupate vayāṃsi te vighase mā vidanta ‖2‖
(11.2.3’) krandāya te prāṇāya yāś ca te bhava ropayaḥ |
(11.2.3.c) namas te rudra kṛṇmaḥ sahasrākṣāyāmartya ‖3‖
(11.2.4’) purastāt te namaḥ kṛṇma uttarād adharād uta |
(11.2.4.c) abhīvargād divas pary antarikṣāya te namaḥ ‖4‖
(11.2.5’) mukhāya te paśupate yāni cakṣūṃṣi te bhava |
(11.2.5.c) tvace rūpāya saṃdṛśe pratīcīnāya te namaḥ ‖5‖
(11.2.6’) aṅgebhyas ta udarāya jihvāyā āsyāya te |
(11.2.6.c) dadbhyo gandhāya te namaḥ ‖6‖
(11.2.7’) astrā nīlaśikhaṇḍena sahasrākṣeṇa vājinā |
(11.2.7.c) rudreṇārdhakaghātinā tena mā sam arāmahi ‖7‖
(11.2.8’) sa no bhavaḥ pari vṛṇaktu viśvata āpa ivāgniḥ pari vṛṇaktu no bhavaḥ |
(11.2.8.c) mā no 'bhi māṃsta namo astv asmai ‖8‖
(11.2.9’) catur namo aṣṭakṛtvo bhavāya daśa kṛtvaḥ paśupate namas te |
(11.2.9.c) taveme pañca paśavo vibhaktā gāvo aśvāḥ puruṣā ajāvayaḥ ‖9‖
(11.2.10’) tava catasraḥ pradiśas tava dyaus tava pṛthivī tavedam ugrorv antarikṣam |
(11.2.10.c) tavedaṃ sarvam ātmanvad yat prāṇat pṛthivīm anu ‖10‖ {5}
(11.2.11’) uruḥ kośo vasudhānas tavāyaṃ yasminn imā viśvā bhuvanāny antaḥ |
(11.2.11.c) sa no mṛḍa paśupate namas te paraḥ kroṣṭāro abhibhāḥ śvānaḥ paro yantv agharudo vikeśyaḥ ‖11‖
(11.2.12’) dhanur bibharṣi haritaṃ hiraṇyayaṃ sahasraghni śatavadhaṃ śikhaṇḍin |
(11.2.12.c) rudrasyeṣuś carati devahetis tasyai namo yatamasyāṃ diśītaḥ ‖12‖
(11.2.13’) yo 'bhiyāto nilayate tvāṃ rudra nicikīrṣati |
(11.2.13.c) paścād anuprayuṅkṣe taṃ viddhasya padanīr iva ‖13‖
(11.2.14’) bhavārudrau sayujā saṃvidānāv ubhāv ugrau carato vīryāya |
(11.2.14.c) tabhyāṃ namo yatamasyām diśītaḥ ‖14‖
(11.2.15’) namas te 'stv āyate namo astu parāyate |
(11.2.15.c) namas te rudra tiṣṭhata āsīnāyota te namaḥ ‖15‖
(11.2.16’) namaḥ sāyaṃ namaḥ prātar namo rātryā namo divā |
(11.2.16.c) bhavāya ca śarvāya cobhābhyām akaraṃ namaḥ ‖16‖
(11.2.17’) sahasrākṣam atipaśyaṃ purastād rudram asyantaṃ bahudhā vipaścitam |
(11.2.17.c) mopārāma jihvayeyamānam ‖17‖
(11.2.18’) śyāvāśvaṃ kṛṣṇam asitaṃ mṛṇantaṃ bhīmaṃ rathaṃ keśinaḥ pādayantam |
(11.2.18.c) pūrve pratīmo namo astv asmai ‖18‖
(11.2.19’) mā no 'bhi srā matyaṃ devahetiṃ mā naḥ krudhaḥ paśupate namas te |
(11.2.19.c) anyatrāsmad divyāṃ śākhāṃ vi dhūnu ‖19‖
(11.2.20’) mā no hiṃsīr adhi no brūhi pari ṇo vṛṅgdhi mā krudhaḥ |
(11.2.20.c) mā tvayā sam arāmahi ‖20‖ {6}
(11.2.21’) mā no goṣu puruṣeṣu mā gṛdho no ajāviṣu |
(11.2.21.c) anyatrogra vi vartaya piyārūṇāṃ prajāṃ jahi ‖21‖
(11.2.22’) yasya takmā kāsikā hetir ekam aśvasyeva vṛṣaṇaḥ kranda eti |
(11.2.22.c) abhipūrvaṃ nirṇayate namo astv asmai ‖22‖
(11.2.23’) yo 'ntarikṣe tiṣṭhati viṣṭabhito 'yajvanaḥ pramṛṇan devapīyūn |
(11.2.23.c) tasmai namo daśabhiḥ śakvarībhiḥ ‖23‖
(11.2.24’) tubhyam āraṇyāḥ paśavo mṛgā vane hitā haṃsāḥ suparṇāḥ śakunā vayāṃsi |
(11.2.24.c) tava yakṣaṃ paśupate apsv antas tubhyaṃ kṣaranti divyā āpo vṛdhe ‖24‖
(11.2.25’) śiśumārā ajagarāḥ purīkayā jaṣā matsyā rajasā yebhyo asyasi |
(11.2.25.c) na te dūraṃ na pariṣṭhāsti te bhava sadyaḥ sarvān pari paśyasi bhūmiṃ pūrvasmād dhaṃsy uttarasmin samudr;e ‖25‖
(11.2.26’) mā no rudra takmanā mā viseṇa mā naḥ saṃ srā divyenāgninā |
(11.2.26.c) anyatrāsmad vidyutaṃ pātayaitām ‖26‖
(11.2.27’) bhavo divo bhava īśe pṛthivyā bhava ā papra urv antarikṣam |
(11.2.27.c) tasmai namo yatamasyāṃ diśītaḥ ‖27‖
(11.2.28’) bhava rajan yajamānāya mṛḍa paśūnāṃ hi paśupatir babhūtha |
(11.2.28.c) yaḥ śraddadhāti santi devā iti catuṣpade dvipade 'sya mṛḍa ‖28‖
(11.2.29’) mā no mahāntam uta mā no arbhakaṃ mā no vahantam uta mā no vakṣyataḥ |
(11.2.29.c) mā no hiṃsīḥ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca svāṃ tanvaṃ rudra mā rīriso naḥ ‖29‖
(11.2.30’) rudrasyailabakārebhyo 'saṃsūktagilebhyaḥ |
(11.2.30.c) idaṃ mahāsyebhyaḥ śvabhyo akaraṃ namaḥ ‖30‖
(11.2.31’) namas te ghoṣiṇībhyo namas te keśinībhyaḥ |
(11.2.31.c) namo namaskṛtābhyo namaḥ saṃbhuñjatībhyaḥ |
(11.2.31.e) namas te deva senābhyaḥ svasti no abhayaṃ ca naḥ ‖31‖ {7}

(11.3.1’) tasyaudanasya bṛhaspatiḥ śiro brahma mukham ‖1‖
(11.3.2’) dyāvāpṛthivī śrotre sūryācandramasāv akṣiṇī saptaṛṣayaḥ prāṇāpānāḥ ‖2‖
(11.3.3’) cakṣur musalaṃ kāma ulūkhalam ‖3‖
(11.3.4’) ditiḥ śūrpam aditiḥ śūrpagrāhī vāto 'pāvinak ‖4‖
(11.3.5’) aśvāḥ kaṇā gāvas taṇḍulā maśakās tuṣāḥ ‖5‖
(11.3.6’) kabru phalīkaraṇāḥ śaro 'bhram ‖6‖
(11.3.7’) śyāmam ayo 'sya māṃsāni lohitam asya lohitam ‖7‖
(11.3.8’) trapu bhasma haritaṃ varṇaḥ puṣkaram asya gandhaḥ ‖8‖
(11.3.9’) khalaḥ pātraṃ sphyāv aṃsāv īṣe anūkye ‖9‖
(11.3.10’) āntrāṇi jatravo gudā varatrāḥ ‖10‖
(11.3.11’) iyam eva pṛthivī kumbhī bhavati rādhyamānasyaudanasya dyaur apidhānam ‖11‖
(11.3.12’) sītāḥ parśavaḥ sikatā ūbadhyam ‖12‖
(11.3.13’) ṛtaṃ hastāvanejanaṃ kulyopasecanam ‖13‖
(11.3.14’) ṛcā kumbhy adhihitārtvijyena preṣitā ‖14‖
(11.3.15’) brahmaṇā parigṛhītā sāmnā paryūḍhā ‖15‖
(11.3.16’) bṛhad āyavanaṃ rathantaraṃ darviḥ ‖16‖
(11.3.17’) ṛtavaḥ paktāra ārtavāḥ sam indhate ‖17‖
(11.3.18’) caruṃ pañcabilam ukhaṃ gharmo 'bhīndhe ‖18‖
(11.3.19’) odanena yajñavacaḥ sarve lokāḥ samāpyāḥ ‖19‖
(11.3.20’) yasmint samudro dyaur bhūmis trayo 'varaparaṃ śritāḥ ‖20‖
(11.3.21’) yasya devā akalpantocchiṣṭe ṣaḍ aśītayaḥ ‖21‖
(11.3.22’) taṃ tvaudanasya pṛchāmi yo asya mahimā mahān ‖22‖
(11.3.23’) taṃ tvaudanasya mahimānaṃ vidyāt ‖23‖
(11.3.24’) nālpa iti brūyān nānupasecana iti nedaṃ ca kiṃ ceti ‖24‖
(11.3.25’) yāvad dātābhimanasyeta tan nāti vadet ‖25‖
(11.3.26’) brahmavādino vadanti parāñcam odanaṃ prāśī3ḥ pratyañcā3m iti ‖26‖
(11.3.27’) tvam odanaṃ prāśī3s tvām odanā3 iti ‖27‖
(11.3.28’) parāñcaṃ cainam prāśīḥ prāṇās tvā hāsyantīty enam āha ‖28‖
(11.3.29’) pratyañcaṃ cainaṃ prāśīr apānās tvā hāsyantīty enam āha ‖29‖
(11.3.30’) naivāham odanaṃ na mām odanaḥ ‖30‖
(11.3.31’) odana evaudanaṃ prāśīt ‖31‖ {8}
(11.3.31’) tatas cainam anyena śīrṣṇā prāśīr yena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.31.c) jyeṣṭhatas te prajā mariṣyatīty enam āha |
(11.3.31.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.33’) tataś cainam anyābhyāṃ śrotrābhyaṃ prāśīr yābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.33.c) badhiro bhaviṣyasīty enam āha |
(11.3.33.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.34’) tataś cainam anyābhyām akṣībhyāṃ prāśīr yābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.34.c) andho bhaviṣyasīty enam āha |
(11.3.34.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.35’) tataś cainam anyena mukhena prāśīr yena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.35.c) mukhatas te prajā mariṣyatīty enam āha |
(11.3.35.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.36’) tataś cainam anyayā jihvayā prāśīr yayā caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.36.c) jihvā te mariṣyatīty enam āha |
(11.3.36.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.37’) tataś cainam anyair dantaiḥ prāśīr yaiś caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.37.c) dantās te śatsyantīty enam āha |
(11.3.37.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.38’) tataś cainam anyaiḥ prāṇāpānaiḥ prāśīr yaiś caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.38.c) prāṇāpānās tvā hāsyantīty enam āha |
(11.3.38.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.39’) tataś cainam anyena vyacasā prāśīr yena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.39.c) rājayakṣmas tvā haniṣyatīty enam āha |
(11.3.39.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.40’) tataś cainam anyena pṛṣṭhena prāśīr yena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.40.c) vidyut tvā haniṣyatīty enam āha |
(11.3.40.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.41’) tataś cainam anyenorasā prāśīr yena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.41.c) kṛṣyā na rātsyasīty enam āha |
(11.3.41.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.42’) tataś cainam anyenodareṇa prāśīr yena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.42.c) udaradāras tvā haniṣyatīty enam āha |
(11.3.42.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.43’) tataś cainam anyena vastinā prāśīr yena caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.43.c) apsu mariṣyasīty enam āha |
(11.3.43.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.44’) tataś cainam anyābhyām ūrubhyāṃ prāśīr yābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.44.c) ūrū te mariṣyata ity enam āha |
(11.3.44.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.45’) tataś cainam anyābhyām aṣṭhīvadbhyāṃ prāśīr yābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.45.c) srāmo bhaviṣyasīti enam āha |
(11.3.45.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.46’) tataś cainam anyābhyāṃ pādābhyāṃ prāśīr yābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.46.c) bahucārī bhaviṣyasīty enam āha |
(11.3.46.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.47’) tataś cainam anyābhyāṃ prapadābhyāṃ prāśīr yābhyāṃ caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.47.c) sarpas tvā haniṣyatīty enam āha |
(11.3.47.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.48’) tataś cainam anyābhyām hastābhyāṃ prāśīr yābhyāṃ caitam pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.48.c) brāhmaṇaṃ haniṣyasīty enam āha |
(11.3.48.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.49’) tataś cainam anyayā pratiṣṭhayā prāśīr yayā caitaṃ pūrva ṛṣayaḥ prāśnan |
(11.3.49.c) apratiṣṭhāno 'nāyatano mariṣyasīty enam āha |
(11.3.49.e) taṃ vā ahaṃ nārvāñcaṃ na parāñcaṃ na pratyañcam |
(11.3.49’) etad vai bradhnasya viṣṭapaṃ yad odanaḥ ‖50‖
(11.3.51’) bradhnaloko bhavati bradhnasya viṣṭapi śrayate ya evaṃ veda ‖51‖
(11.3.52’) etasmād vā odanāt trayastriṃśataṃ lokān nir amimīta prajāpatiḥ ‖52‖
(11.3.53’) teṣāṃ prajñānāya yajñam asṛjata ‖53‖
(11.3.54’) sa ya evaṃ viduṣa upadraṣṭā bhavati prāṇaṃ ruṇaddhi ‖54‖
(11.3.55’) na ca prānaṃ ruṇaddhi sarvajyāniṃ jīyate ‖55‖
(11.3.56’) na ca sarvajyāniṃ jīyate purainaṃ jarasaḥ prāṇo jahāti ‖56‖ {10}
(11.3.1’) prāṇāya namo yasya sarvam idaṃ vaśe |
(11.3.1.c) yo bhūtaḥ sarvasyeśvaro yasmint sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ‖1‖
(11.3.2’) namas te prāṇa krandāya namas te stanayitnave |
(11.3.2.c) namas te prāṇa vidyute namas te prāṇa varṣate ‖2‖
(11.3.3’) yat prāṇa stanayitnunābhikrandaty oṣadhīḥ |
(11.3.3.c) pra vīyante garbhān dadhate 'tho bahvīr vi jāyante ‖3‖
(11.3.4’) yat prāṇa ṛtāv āgate 'bhikrandaty oṣadhīḥ |
(11.3.4.c) sarvaṃ tadā pra modate yat kiṃ ca bhūmyām adhi ‖4‖
(11.3.5’) yadā prāṇo abhyavarṣīd varṣeṇa pṛthivīṃ mahīm |
(11.3.5.c) paśavas tat pra modante maho vai no bhaviṣyati ‖5‖
(11.3.6’) abhivṛṣṭā oṣadhayaḥ prāṇena sam avādiran |
(11.3.6.c) āyur vai naḥ prātītaraḥ sarvā naḥ surabhīr akaḥ ‖6‖
(11.3.7’) namas te astv āyate namo astu parāyate |
(11.3.7.c) namas te prāṇa tiṣṭhata āsīnāyota te namaḥ ‖7‖
(11.3.8’) namas te prāṇa prāṇate namo astv apānate |
(11.3.8.c) parācīnāya te namaḥ pratīcīnāya te namaḥ sarvasmai ta idaṃ namaḥ ‖8‖
(11.3.9’) yā te prāṇa priyā tanūr yo te prāṇa preyasī |
(11.3.9.c) atho yad bheṣajaṃ tava tasya no dhehi jīvase ‖9‖
(11.3.10’) prāṇaḥ prajā anu vaste pitā putram iva priyam |
(11.3.10.c) prāṇo ha sarvasyeśvaro yac ca prāṇati yac ca na ‖10‖ {11}
(11.3.11’) prāṇo mṛtyuḥ prāṇas takmā prāṇaṃ devā upāsate |
(11.3.11.c) prāṇo ha satyavādinam uttame loka ā dadhat ‖11‖
(11.3.12’) prāṇo virāṭ prāṇo deṣṭrī prānaṃ sarva upāsate |
(11.3.12.c) prāṇo ha sūryaś candramāḥ prāṇam āhuḥ prajāpatim ‖12‖
(11.3.13’) prāṇāpānau vrīhiyavāv anaḍvān prāṇa ucyate |
(11.3.13.c) yave ha prāṇa āhito 'pāno vrīhir ucyate ‖13‖
(11.3.14’) apānati prāṇati puruṣo garbhe antarā |
(11.3.14.c) yadā tvaṃ prāṇa jinvasy atha sa jāyate punaḥ ‖14‖
(11.3.15’) prāṇaṃ āhur mātariśvānaṃ vāto ha prāṇa ucyate |
(11.3.15.c) prāṇe ha bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ca prāṇe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ‖15‖
(11.3.16’) ātharvaṇīr āṅgirasīr daivīr manuṣyajā uta |
(11.3.16.c) oṣadhayaḥ pra jāyante yadā tvaṃ prāṇa jinvasi ‖16‖
(11.3.17’) yadā prāṇo abhyavarṣīd varṣeṇa pṛthivīm mahīm |
(11.3.17.c) oṣadhayaḥ pra jāyante 'tho yāḥ kāś ca vīrudhaḥ ‖17‖
(11.3.18’) yas te prāṇedaṃ veda yasmiṃś cāsi pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
(11.3.18.c) sarve tasmai baliṃ harān amuṣmiṃl loka uttame ‖18‖
(11.3.19’) yathā prāṇa balihṛtas tubhyaṃ sarvāḥ prajā imāḥ |
(11.3.19.c) evā tasmai baliṃ harān yas tvā śṛṇavat suśravaḥ ‖19‖
(11.3.20’) antar garbhaś carati devatāsv ābhūto bhūtaḥ sa u jāyate punaḥ |
(11.3.20.c) sa bhūto bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyat pitā putraṃ pra viveśā śacībhiḥ ‖20‖ {12}
(11.3.21’) ekaṃ pādaṃ not khidati salilād dhaṃsa uccaran |
(11.3.21.c) yad aṅga sa tam utkhiden naivādya na śvaḥ syāt |
(11.3.21.e) na rātrī nāhaḥ syān na vy uchet kadā cana ‖21‖
(11.3.22’) aṣṭācakraṃ vartata ekanemi sahasrākṣaraṃ pra puro ni paścā |
(11.3.22.c) ardhena viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ jajāna yad asyārdhaṃ katamaḥ sa ketuḥ ‖22‖
(11.3.23’) yo asya viśvajanmana īśe viśvasya ceṣṭataḥ |
(11.3.23.c) anyeṣu kṣipradhanvane tasmai prāṇa namo 'stu te ‖23‖
(11.3.24’) yo asya sarvajanmana īśe sarvasya ceṣṭataḥ |
(11.3.24.c) atandro brahmaṇā dhīraḥ prāṇo mānu tiṣṭhātu ‖24‖
(11.3.25’) ūrdhvaḥ supteṣu jāgāra nanu tiryaṅ ni padyate |
(11.3.25.c) na suptam asya supteṣv anu śuśrāva kaś cana ‖25‖
(11.3.26’) prāṇa mā mat paryāvṛto na mad anyo bhaviṣyasi |
(11.3.26.c) apāṃ garbham iva jīvase prāṇa badhnāmi tvā mayi ‖26‖ {13}
(11.3.1’) brahmacārīṣṇaṃś carati rodasī ubhe tasmin devāḥ saṃmanaso bhavanti |
(11.3.1.c) sa dādhāra pṛthivīṃ divaṃ ca sa ācāryaṃ tapasā piparti ‖1‖
(11.3.2’) brahmacāriṇaṃ pitaro devajanāḥ pṛthag devā anusaṃyanti sarve |
(11.3.2.c) gandharvā enam anv āyan trayastriṃśat triśatāh ṣaṭsahasrāḥ sarvānt sa devāṃs tapasā piparti ‖2‖
(11.3.3’) ācārya upanayamāno brahmacāriṇaṃ kṛṇute garbham antaḥ |
(11.3.3.c) tam rātrīs tisra udare bibharti taṃ jātaṃ draṣṭum abhisaṃyanti devāḥ ‖3‖
(11.3.4’) iyaṃ samit pṛthivī dyaur dvitīyotāntarikṣaṃ samidhā pṛṇāti |
(11.3.4.c) brahmacārī samidhā mekhalayā śrameṇa lokāṃs tapasā piparti ‖4‖
(11.3.5’) pūrvo jāto brahmaṇo brahmacārī gharmaṃ vasānas tapasod atiṣṭhat |
(11.3.5.c) tasmāj jātaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brahma jyeṣṭhaṃ devāś ca sarve amṛtena sākam ‖5‖
(11.3.6’) brahmacāry eti samidhā samiddhaḥ kārṣṇaṃ vasāno dīkṣito dīrghaśmaśruḥ |
(11.3.6.c) sa sadya eti pūrvasmād uttaraṃ samudraṃ lokānt samgṛbhya muhur ācarikrat ‖6‖
(11.3.7’) brahmacārī janayan brahmāpo lokaṃ prajāpatiṃ parameṣṭhinam virājam |
(11.3.7.c) garbho bhūtvāmṛtasya yonāv indro ha bhūtvāsurāṃs tatarha ‖7‖
(11.3.8’) ācāryas tatakṣa nabhasī ubhe ime urvī gambhīre pṛthivīṃ divaṃ ca |
(11.3.8.c) te rakṣati tapasā brahmacārī tasmin devāḥ saṃmanaso bhavanti ‖8‖
(11.3.9’) imāṃ bhūmiṃ pṛthivīṃ brahmacārī bhikṣām ā jabhāra prathamo divaṃ ca |
(11.3.9.c) te kṛtvā samidhāv upāste tayor ārpitā bhuvanāni viśvā ‖9‖
(11.3.10’) arvāg anyaḥ paro anyo divas pṛṣṭhād guhā nidhī nihitau brāhmaṇasya |
(11.3.10.c) tau rakṣati tapasā brahmacārī tat kevalaṃ kṛṇute brahma vidvān ‖10‖ {14}
(11.3.11’) arvāg anya ito anyaḥ pṛthivyā agnī sameto nabhasī antareme |
(11.3.11.c) tayoḥ śrayante raśmayo 'dhi dṛḍhās tān ā tiṣṭhati tapasā brahmacārī ‖11‖
(11.3.12’) abhikrandan stanayann aruṇaḥ śitiṅgo bṛhac chepo 'nu bhūmau jabhāra |
(11.3.12.c) brahmacārī siñcati sānau retaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tena jīvanti pradiśaś catasraḥ ‖12‖
(11.3.13’) agnau sūrye candramasi mātariśvan brahmacāry apsu samidham ā dadhāti |
(11.3.13.c) tāsām arcīṣi pṛthag abhre caranti tāsām ājyaṃ puruṣo varṣam āpaḥ ‖13‖
(11.3.14’) ācāryo mṛtyur varuṇaḥ somo oṣadhayaḥ payaḥ |
(11.3.14.c) jībhūtā āsant satvānas tair idaṃ svar ābhṛtam ‖14‖
(11.3.15’) amā ghṛtaṃ kṛṇute kevalam ācāryo bhūtvā varuṇo yadyad aichat prajāpatau |
(11.3.15.c) tad brahmacārī prāyachat svān mitro adhy ātmanaḥ ‖15‖
(11.3.16’) ācāryo brahmacārī brahmacarī prajāpatiḥ |
(11.3.16.c) prajāpatir vi rājati virāḍ indro 'bhavad vaśī ‖16‖
(11.3.17’) brahmacaryeṇa tapasā rājā rāṣṭraṃ vi rakṣati |
(11.3.17.c) ācāryo brahmacaryeṇa brahmacāriṇam ichate ‖17‖
(11.3.18’) brahmacaryeṇa kanyā yuvānam vindate patim |
(11.3.18.c) anaḍvān brahmacaryeṇāśvo ghāsaṃ jigīrṣati ‖18‖
(11.3.19’) brahmacaryeṇa tapasā devā mṛtyum apāghnata |
(11.3.19.c) indro ha brahmacaryeṇa devebhyaḥ svar ābharat ‖19‖
(11.3.20’) oṣadhayo bhūtabhavyam ahorātre vanaspatiḥ |
(11.3.20.c) saṃvatsaraḥ saha rtubhis te jātā brahmacāriṇaḥ ‖20‖ {15}
(11.3.21’) pārthivā divyāḥ paśava āraṇyā grāmyāś ca ye |
(11.3.21.c) apakṣāḥ pakṣiṇaś ca ye te jātā brahmacāriṇaḥ ‖21‖
(11.3.22’) pṛthak sarve prājāpatyāḥ prāṇān ātmasu bibhrati |
(11.3.22.c) tānt sarvān brahma rakṣati brahmacāriṇy ābhṛtam ‖22‖
(11.3.23’) devānām etat pariṣūtam anabhyārūḍhaṃ carati rocamānam |
(11.3.23.c) tasmāj jātaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brahma jyeṣṭhaṃ devāś ca sarve amṛtena sākam ‖23‖
(11.3.24’) brahmacārī brahma bhrājad bibharti tasmin devā adhi viśve samotāḥ |
(11.3.24.c) prāṇāpānau janayann ād vyānaṃ vācaṃ mano hṛdayaṃ brahma medhām ‖24‖
(11.3.25’) cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ yaśo asmāsu dhehy annaṃ reto lohitam udaram ‖25‖
(11.3.26’) tāni kalpan brahmacārī salilasya pṛṣṭhe tapo 'tiṣṭhat tapyamānaḥ samudre |
(11.3.26.c) sa snāto babhruḥ piṅgalaḥ pṛthivyāṃ bahu rocate ‖26‖ {16}

(11.6.1’) agnim brūmo vanaspatīn oṣadhīr uta vīrudhaḥ |
(11.6.1.c) indraṃ bṛhaspatiṃ sūryaṃ te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(11.6.2’) brūmo rājānaṃ varuṇaṃ mitraṃ viṣṇum atho bhagam |
(11.6.2.c) aṃśaṃ vivasvantaṃ brūmas te no muñcantv aṃhasah ‖2‖
(11.6.3’) brūmo devaṃ savitāraṃ dhātāram uta pūṣaṇam |
(11.6.3.c) tvaṣṭāram agriyaṃ brūmas te no muñcantv aṃhasah ‖3‖
(11.6.4’) gandharvāpsaraso brūmo aśvinā brahmaṇas patim |
(11.6.4.c) aryamā nāma yo devas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖4‖
(11.6.5’) ahorātre idaṃ brūmaḥ sūryācandramasāv ubhā |
(11.6.5.c) viśvān ādityān brūmas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖5‖
(11.6.6’) vātaṃ brūmaḥ parjanyam antarikṣam atho diśaḥ |
(11.6.6.c) āśāś ca sarvā brūmas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖6‖
(11.6.7’) muñcantu mā śapathyād ahorātre atho uṣāḥ |
(11.6.7.c) somo mā devo muñcatu yam āhuś candramā iti ‖7‖
(11.6.8’) pārthivā divyaḥ paśava āraṇyā uta ye mṛgāḥ |
(11.6.8.c) śakuntān pakṣino brūmas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖8‖
(11.6.9’) bhavāśarvāv idaṃ brūmo rudraṃ paśupatiś ca yaḥ |
(11.6.9.c) iṣūr yā eṣāṃ saṃvidma tā naḥ santu sadā śivāḥ ‖9‖
(11.6.10’) divaṃ brūmo nakṣatrāṇi bhūmiṃ yakṣāṇi parvatān |
(11.6.10.c) samudrā nadyo veśantās te no muñcantv amhasaḥ ‖10‖ {17}
(11.6.11’) saptarṣīn vā idaṃ brūmo 'po devīḥ prajāpatim |
(11.6.11.c) pitṝn yamaśreṣṭhān brūmas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ |11‖
(11.6.12’) ye devā diviṣado antarikṣasadaś ca ye |
(11.6.12.c) pṛthivyāṃ śakrā ye śritās te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖12‖
(11.6.13’) ādityā rudrā vasavo divi devā atharvānaḥ |
(11.6.13.c) aṅgiraso manīṣiṇas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖13‖
(11.6.14’) yajñaṃ brūmo yajamānam ṛcaḥ sāmāni bheṣajā |
(11.6.14.c) yajūṃṣi hotrā brūmas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖14‖
(11.6.15’) pañca rājyāni vīrudhāṃ somaśreṣṭhāni brūmaḥ |
(11.6.15.c) darbho bhaṅgo yavaḥ sahas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖15‖
(11.6.16’) arāyān brūmo rakṣāṃsi sarpān puṇyajanān pitṝn |
(11.6.16.c) mṛtyūn ekaśataṃ brūmas te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖16‖
(11.6.17’) ṛtūn brūma ṛtupatīn ārtavān uta hāyanān |
(11.6.17.c) samāḥ saṃvatsarān māsāṃs te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖17‖
(11.6.18’) eta devā dakṣiṇataḥ paścāt prāñca udeta |
(11.6.18.c) purastād uttarāc chakrā viśve devāḥ sametya te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖18‖
(11.6.19’) viśvān devān idaṃ brūmaḥ satyasaṃdhān ṛtavṛdhaḥ |
(11.6.19.c) viśvābhiḥ patnībhiḥ saha te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖19‖
(11.6.20’) sarvān devān idaṃ brūmaḥ satyasaṃdhān ṛtāvṛdhaḥ |
(11.6.20.c) sarvābhiḥ patnībhiḥ saha te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖20‖
(11.6.21’) bhūtaṃ brūmo bhūtapatiṃ bhūtānām uta yo vaśī |
(11.6.21.c) bhūtāni sarvā saṃgatya te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖21‖
(11.6.22’) yā devīḥ pañca pradiśo ye devā dvādaśa rtavah |
(11.6.22.c) saṃvatsarasya ye daṃṣṭrās te naḥ santu sadā śivāḥ ‖22‖
(11.6.23’) yan mātalī rathakrītam amṛtaṃ veda bheṣajam |
(11.6.23.c) tad indro apsu prāveśayat tad āpo datta bheṣajam ‖23‖ {18}
(11.6.1’) ucchiṣṭe nāma rūpaṃ cocchiṣṭe loka āhitaḥ |
(11.6.1.c) ucchiṣṭa indraś cāgniś ca viśvam antaḥ samāhitam ‖1‖
(11.6.2’) ucchiṣṭe dyāvāpṛthivī viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ samāhitam |
(11.6.2.c) āpaḥ samudra ucchiṣṭe candramā vāta āhitaḥ ‖2‖
(11.6.3’) sann ucchiṣṭe asaṃś ca ubhau mṛtyur vājaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
(11.6.3.c) laikyā ucchiṣṭa āyattā vraś ca draś cāpi śrīr mayi ‖3‖
(11.6.4’) dṛḍho dṛṃhasthiro nyo brahma viśvasṛjaḥ daśa |
(11.6.4.c) nābhim iva sarvataḥ cakram ucchiṣṭe devatās śritāḥ ‖4‖
(11.6.5’) ṛk sāma yajur ucchiṣṭa udgīthaḥ prastutam stutam |
(11.6.5.c) hiṅkāra ucchiṣṭe svaraḥ sāmno mediś ca tan mayi ‖5‖
(11.6.6’) aindrāgnaṃ pāvamānaṃ mahānāmnīr mahāvratam |
(11.6.6.c) ucchiṣṭe yajñasyāṅgāny antar garbha iva mātari ‖6‖
(11.6.7’) rājasūyaṃ vājapeyam agniṣṭomaḥ tad adhvaraḥ |
(11.6.7.c) arkāśvamedhāv ucchiṣṭe jīvabarhir madintamaḥ ‖7‖
(11.6.8’) agnyādheyam atho dīkṣā kāmapraś chandasā saha |
(11.6.8.c) utsannā yajñāḥ satrāṇy ucchiṣṭe 'dhi samāhitāḥ ‖8‖
(11.6.9’) agnihotraṃ ca śraddhā ca vaṣaṭkāro vrataṃ tapaḥ |
(11.6.9.c) dakṣiṇeṣṭaṃ pūrtaṃ cocchiṣṭe 'dhi samāhitāḥ ‖9‖
(11.6.10’) ekarātro dvirātraḥ sadyaḥkrīḥ prakrīr ukthyaḥ |
(11.6.10.c) otaṃ nihitam ucchiṣṭe yajñasyāṇūni vidyayā ‖10‖ {19}
(11.6.11’) catūrātraḥ pañcarātraḥ ṣaḍrātraś cobhayaḥ saha |
(11.6.11.c) ṣoḍaśī saptarātraś cocchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve ye yajñā amṛte hitāḥ ‖11‖
(11.6.12’) pratīhāro nidhanaṃ viśvajic cābhijic ca yaḥ |
(11.6.12.c) sāhnātirātrāv ucchiṣṭe dvādaśāho 'pi tan mayi ‖12‖
(11.6.13’) sūnṛtā samnatiḥ kṣemaḥ svadhorjāmṛtaṃ sahaḥ |
(11.6.13.c) ucchiṣṭe sarve pratyañcaḥ kāmāḥ kāmena tatṛpuh ‖13‖
(11.6.14’) nava bhūmīḥ samudrā ucchisṭe adhi śritā divaḥ |
(11.6.14.c) ā sūryo bhāty ucchiṣṭe 'horātre api tan mayi ‖14‖
(11.6.15’) upahavyaṃ viṣūvantaṃ ye ca yajñā guhā hitāḥ |
(11.6.15.c) bibharti bhartā viśvasyocchiṣṭo janituḥ pitā ‖15‖
(11.6.16’) pitā janitur ucchisṭo 'soḥ pautraḥ pitāmahaḥ |
(11.6.16.c) sa kṣiyati viśvasyeśāno vṛṣā bhūmyām atighnyaḥ ‖16‖
(11.6.17’) ṛtaṃ satyaṃ tapo rāsṭraṃ śramo dharmaś ca karma ca |
(11.6.17.c) bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyad ucchiṣṭe vīryaṃ lakṣmīr balaṃ bale ‖17‖
(11.6.18’) samṛddhir oja ākutiḥ kṣatraṃ rāṣṭraṃ ṣaḍ urvyaḥ |
(11.6.18.c) saṃvatsaro 'dhy ucchista iḍā praiṣā grahā haviḥ ‖18‖
(11.6.19’) caturhotāra āpriyaś cāturmāsyāni nīvidaḥ |
(11.6.19.c) ucchiṣṭe yajñāḥ hotrāḥ paśubandhās tad iṣṭayaḥ ‖19‖
(11.6.20’) ardhamāsāś ca māsāś cārtavā ṛtubhiḥ saha |
(11.6.20.c) ucchiṣṭe ghoṣiṇīr āpaḥ stanayitnuḥ śrutir mahī ‖20‖ {20}
(11.6.21’) śarkarāḥ sikatā aśmāna oṣadhayo vīrudhas tṛṇā |
(11.6.21.c) abhrāṇi vidyuto varṣam ucchiṣṭe saṃśritā śritā ‖21‖
(11.6.22’) rāddhiḥ prāptiḥ samāptir vyāptir maha edhatuḥ |
(11.6.22.c) atyāptir ucchiṣṭe bhūtiś cāhitā nihitā hitā ‖22‖
(11.6.23’) yac ca prāṇati prāṇena yac ca paśyati cakṣuṣā |
(11.6.23.c) ucchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ ‖23‖

(11.6.24.c) ucchiṣṭāj jajñire sarve divi devā diviśritaḥ ‖24‖

(11.6.26’) ānandā modāḥ pramudo 'bhimodamudaś ca ye |

(11.6.1’) yan manyur jāyām āvahat saṃkalpasya gṛhād adhi |

(11.6.2’) tapaś caivāstāṃ karma cāntar mahaty arṇave |

(11.6.3’) daśa sākam ajāyanta devā devebhyaḥ purā |

(11.6.4’) prāṇāpānau cakṣuḥ śrotram akṣitiś ca kṣitiś ca yā |
(11.6.4.c) vyānodānau vāṅ manas te vā ākūtim āvahan ‖4‖
(11.6.5’) ajātā āsann ṛtavo 'tho dhātā bṛhaspatiḥ |
(11.6.5.c) indrāgnī aśvinā tarhi kaṃ te jyeṣṭham upāsata ‖5‖
(11.6.6’) tapaś caivāstāṃ karma cāntar mahaty arṇave |
(11.6.6.c) tapo ha jajñe karmaṇas tat te jyeṣṭham upāsata ‖6‖
(11.6.7’) yeta āsīd bhūmiḥ pūrvā yām addhātaya id viduḥ |
(11.6.7.c) yaḥ vai tāṃ vidyān nāmathā sa manyeta purāṇavit ‖7‖
(11.6.8’) kuta indraḥ kutaḥ somaḥ kuto agnir ajāyata |
(11.6.8.c) kutas tvaṣṭā sam abhavat kuto dhātājāyata ‖8‖
(11.6.9’) indrād indraḥ somāt somo agner agnir ajāyata |

(11.6.10’) ye ta āsan daśa jātā devā devebhyaḥ purā |

(11.6.12’) kutaḥ keśān kutaḥ snāva kuto asthīny ābharat |

(11.6.13’) saṃsico nāma te devā ye saṃbhārānt samabharan |

(11.6.28’) āsteyīś ca vāsteyīś ca tvaraṇāḥ kṛpaṇāś ca yāḥ |

(11.6.29’) asthi kṛtvā ṣamidhaṃ tad aṣṭa āpo asādayan |

(11.6.30’) yā āpo yāś ca devatā yā virāṭ brahmaṇā saha |

(11.6.1’) ye bāhavo yā iṣavo dhanvanāṃ vīryāṇi ca |

(11.6.2’) ut tiṣṭhata saṃ nahyadhvaṃ mitrā devajanā yūyam |

(11.6.3’) ut tiṣṭhatam ā rabhetām ādānasaṃdānābhyām |

(11.6.7’) pratighnānāśrumukhī kṛdhukarṇī ca krośatu |

(11.6.13’) muhyantv eṣāṃ bāhavaś cittākūtaṃ ca yad dhṛdi |

(11.10.1’) ut tiṣṭhata saṃ nahyadhvam udārāḥ ketubhiḥ saha |
(11.10.1.c) sarpā itarajanā rakṣāṃsy amitrān anu dhāvata ‖1‖
(11.10.2’) īśāṃ vo veda rājyaṃ triṣandhe aruṇaiḥ ketubhiḥ saha |
(11.10.2.c) ye antarikṣe ye divi pṛthivyāṃ ye ca manavāḥ |
(11.10.2.e) triṣandhes te cetasi durṇāmāna upāsatām ‖2‖
(11.10.3’) ayomukhāḥ sūcīmukhā atho vikaṅkatīmukhāḥ |
(11.10.3.c) kravyādo vātaraṃhasaḥ ā sajantv amitrān vajreṇa triṣandhinā ‖3‖
(11.10.4’) antar dhehi jātaveda āditya kuṇapam bahu |
(11.10.4.c) trisandher iyaṃ senā suhitāstu me vaśe ‖4‖
(11.10.5’) ut tiṣṭha tvaṃ devajanārbude senayā saha |
(11.10.5.c) ayaṃ balir va āhutas triṣandher āhutiḥ priyā ‖5‖
(11.10.6’) śitipadī saṃ dyatu śaravyeyaṃ catuṣpadī |
(11.10.6.c) kṛtye 'mitrebhyo bhava triṣandheḥ saha senayā ‖6‖
(11.10.7’) dhūmākṣī saṃ patatu kṛdhukarnī ca krośatu |
(11.10.7.c) triṣandheḥ senayā jite aruṇāḥ santu ketavaḥ ‖7‖
(11.10.8’) avāyantāṃ pakṣiṇo ye vayāṃsy antarikṣe divi ye caranti |
(11.10.8.c) śvāpado makṣikāḥ saṃ rabhantām āmādo gṛdhrāḥ kuṇape radantām ‖8‖
(11.10.9’) yām indreṇa saṃdhāṃ samadhatthā brahmaṇā ca bṛhaspate |
(11.10.9.c) tayāham indrasaṃdhayā sarvān devān iha huva ito jayata māmutaḥ ‖9‖
(11.10.10’) bṛhaspatir āṅgirasa ṛṣayo brahmasaṃśitāḥ |
(11.10.10.c) asurakṣayaṇaṃ vadhaṃ triṣandhim divy āsrayan ‖10‖ {28}
(11.10.11’) yenāsau gupta āditya ubhāv indraś ca tiṣṭhataḥ |
(11.10.11.c) triṣandhiṃ devā abhajantaujase ca balāya ca ‖11‖
(11.10.12’) sarvāṃl lokānt sam ajayan devā āhutyānayā |
(11.10.12.c) bṛhaspatir āṅgiraso vajraṃ yam asiñcatāsurakṣayaṇaṃ vadham ‖12‖
(11.10.13’) bṛhaspatir āṅgiraso vajraṃ yam asiñcatāsurakṣayaṇaṃ vadham |
(11.10.13.c) tenāham amūṃ senāṃ ni limpāmi bṛhaspate 'mitrān hanmy ojasā ‖13‖
(11.10.14’) sarve devā atyāyanti ye aśnanti vaṣaṭkṛtam |
(11.10.14.c) imāṃ juṣadhvam āhutim ito jayata māmutaḥ ‖14‖
(11.10.15’) sarve devā atyāyantu triṣandher āhutiḥ priyā |
(11.10.15.c) saṃdhāṃ mahatīṃ rakṣata yayāgre asurā jitāh ‖15‖
(11.10.16’) vāyur amitrāṇām iṣvagrāṇy āñcatu |
(11.10.16.c) indra eṣāṃ bahūn prati bhanaktu mā śakan pratidhām iṣum |
(11.10.16.e) āditya eṣām astraṃ vi nāśayatu candramā yutām agatasya panthām ‖16‖
(11.10.17’) yadi preyur devapurā brahma varmāṇi cakrire |
(11.10.17.c) tanūpānaṃ paripāṇaṃ kṛṇvānā yad upocire sarvaṃ tad arasaṃ kṛdhi ‖17‖
(11.10.18’) kravyādānuvartayan mṛtyunā ca purohitam |
(11.10.18.c) triṣandhe prehi senayā jayāmitrān pra padyasva ‖18‖
(11.10.19’) triṣandhe tamasā tvam amitrān pari vāraya |
(11.10.19.c) pṛṣadājyapraṇuttānāṃ māmīṣāṃ moci kaś cana ‖19‖
(11.10.20’) śitipadī saṃ patatv amitrānām amūḥ śicaḥ |
(11.10.20.c) mahyantv adyāmūḥ senāḥ amitrānāṃ nyarbude ‖20‖ {29}
(11.10.21’) mūḍhā amitrā nyarbude jahy eṣāṃ varaṃvaram |
(11.10.21.c) anayā jahi senayā ‖21‖
(11.10.22’) yaś ca kavacī yaś cākavaco 'mitro yaś cājmani |
(11.10.22.c) jyāpāśaiḥ kavacapāśair ajmanābhihataḥ śayām ‖22‖
(11.10.23’) ye varmiṇo ye 'varmāṇo amitrā ye ca varmiṇaḥ |
(11.10.23.c) sarvāṃs tā;m arbude hatāṃ chvāno 'dantu bhūmyām ‖23‖
(11.10.24’) ye rathino ye arathā asādā ye ca sādinaḥ |
(11.10.24.c) sarvān adantu tān hatān gṛdhrāḥ śyenāḥ patatriṇaḥ ‖24‖
(11.10.25’) sahasrakuṇapā śetām āmitrī senā samare vadhānām |
(11.10.25.c) vividdhā kakajākṛtā ‖25‖
(11.10.26’) marmāvidhaṃ roruvataṃ suparṇair adantu duścitaṃ mṛditam śayānam |
(11.10.26.c) ya imāṃ pratīcīm āhutim amitro no yuyutsati ‖26‖
(11.10.27’) yāṃ devā anutisṭhanti yasyā nāsti virādhanam |
(11.10.27.c) tayendro hantu vṛtrahā vajreṇa triṣaṃdhinā ‖27‖ {30}

(12.1.1’) satyaṃ bṛhad ṛtam ugraṃ dīkṣā tapo brahma yajñaḥ pṛthivīṃ dhārayanti |
(12.1.1.c) sā no bhūtasya bhavyasya patny uruṃ lokaṃ pṛthivī naḥ kṛṇotu ‖1‖
(12.1.2’) asaṃbādhaṃ madhyato mānavānāṃ yasyā udvataḥ pravataḥ samaṃ bahu |
(12.1.2.c) nānāvīryā oṣadhīr yā bibharti pṛthivī naḥ prathatāṃ rādhyatāṃ naḥ  ‖2‖
(12.1.3’) yasyāṃ samudra uta sindhur āpo yasyām annaṃ kṛṣṭayaḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ  |
(12.1.3.c) yasyām idaṃ jinvati prāṇad ejat sā no bhūmiḥ pūrvapeye dadhātu  ‖3‖
(12.1.4’) yasyāś catasraḥ pradiśaḥ pṛthivyā yasyām annam kṛṣṭayaḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ  |
(12.1.4.c) yā bibharti bahudhā prāṇad ejat sā no bhūmir goṣv apy anne dadhātu ‖4‖
(12.1.5’) yasyāṃ pūrve pūrvajanā vicakrire yasyāṃ devā asurān abhyavartayan  |
(12.1.5.c) gavām aśvānāṃ vayasaś ca viṣṭhā bhagaṃ varcaḥ pṛthivī no dadhātu  ‖5‖
(12.1.6’) viśvaṃbharā vasudhānī pratiṣṭhā hiraṇyavakṣā jagato niveśanī |
(12.1.6.c) vaiśvānaraṃ bibhratī bhūmir agnim indraṛṣabhā draviṇe no dadhātu ‖6‖
(12.1.7’) yāṃ rakṣanty asvapnā viśvadānīṃ devā bhūmiṃ pṛthivīm apramādam  |
(12.1.7.c) sā no madhu priyaṃ duhām atho ukṣatu varcasā  ‖7‖
(12.1.8’) yārṇave 'dhi salilam agra āsīt yāṃ māyābhir anvacaran manīṣiṇaḥ  |
(12.1.8.c) yasyā hṛdayaṃ parame vyomant satyenāvṛtam amṛtaṃ pṛthivyāḥ  |
(12.1.8.e) sā no bhūmis tviṣiṃ balaṃ rāṣṭre dadhātūttame  ‖8‖
(12.1.9’) yasyām āpaḥ paricarāḥ samānīr ahorātre apramādaṃ kṣaranti	|
(12.1.9.c) sā no bhūmir bhūridhārā payo duhām atho ukṣatu varcasā  ‖9‖
(12.1.10’) yām aśvināv amimātāṃ viṣṇur yasyāṃ vicakrame  |
(12.1.10.c) indro yāṃ cakra ātmane 'namitrāṃ śacīpatiḥ |
(12.1.10.e) sā no bhūmir vi sṛjatāṃ mātā putrāya me payaḥ  ‖10‖ {1}
(12.1.11’) girayas te parvatā himavanto 'raṇyaṃ te pṛthivi syonam astu  |
(12.1.11.c) babhruṃ kṛṣṇām rohiṇīṃ viśvarūpāṃ dhruvāṃ bhūmiṃ pṛthivīm indraguptām  |
(12.1.11.e) ajīto 'hato akṣato 'dhy aṣṭhām pṛthivīm aham  ‖11‖
(12.1.12’) yat te madhyaṃ pṛthivi yac ca nabhyaṃ yās ta ūrjas tanvaḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ  |
(12.1.12.c) tāsu no dhehy abhi naḥ pavasva mātā bhūmiḥ putro ahaṃ pṛthivyāḥ parjanyaḥ pitā sa u naḥ pipartu  ‖12‖
(12.1.13’) yasyāṃ vediṃ parigṛhṇanti bhūmyāṃ yasyāṃ yajñaṃ tanvate viśvakarmāṇaḥ  |
(12.1.13.c) yasyāṃ mīyante svaravaḥ pṛthivyām ūrdhvāḥ śukrā āhutyāḥ purastāt  |
(12.1.13.e) sā no bhūmir vardhayad vardhamānā  ‖13‖
(12.1.14’) yo no dveṣat pṛthivi yaḥ pṛtanyād yo 'bhidāsān manasā yo vadhena  |
(12.1.14.c) taṃ no bhūme randhaya pūrvakṛtvari  ‖14‖
(12.1.15’) tvaj jātās tvayi caranti martyās tvaṃ bibharṣi dvipadas tvaṃ catuṣpadaḥ  |
(12.1.15.c) taveme pṛthivi pañca mānavā yebhyo jyotir amṛtaṃ martyebhya udyant sūryo raśmibhir ātanoti  ‖15‖
(12.1.16’) tā naḥ prajāḥ saṃ duhratāṃ samagrā vāco madhu pṛthivi dhehi mahyam  ‖16‖
(12.1.17’) viśvasvaṃ mātaram oṣadhīnāṃ dhruvāṃ bhūmiṃ pṛthivīṃ dharmaṇā dhṛtām  |
(12.1.17.c) śivāṃ syonām anu carema viśvahā  ‖17‖
(12.1.18’) mahat sadhasthaṃ mahatī babhūvitha mahān vega ejathur vepathuṣ ṭe  |
(12.1.18.c) mahāṃs tvendro rakṣaty apramādam  |
(12.1.18.e) sā no bhūme pra rocaya hiraṇyasyeva saṃdṛśi mā no dvikṣata kaś cana  ‖18‖
(12.1.19’) agnir bhūmyām oṣadhīṣv agnim āpo bibhraty agnir aśmasu  |
(12.1.19.c) agnir antaḥ puruṣeṣu goṣv aśveṣv agnayaḥ  ‖19‖
(12.1.20’) agnir diva ā tapaty agner devasyorv antarikṣam  |
(12.1.20.c) agniṃ martāsa indhate havyavāhaṃ ghṛtapriyam  ‖20‖ {2}
(12.1.21’) agnivāsāḥ pṛthivy asitajñūs tviṣīmantaṃ saṃśitaṃ mā kṛṇotu  ‖21‖
(12.1.22’) bhūmyāṃ devebhyo dadati yajñaṃ havyam araṃkṛtam |
(12.1.22.c) bhūmyāṃ manuṣyā jīvanti svadhayānnena martyāḥ |
(12.1.22.e) sā no bhūmiḥ prāṇam āyur dadhātu jaradaṣṭiṃ mā pṛthivī kṛṇotu	‖22‖
(12.1.23’) yas te gandhaḥ pṛthivi saṃbabhūva yaṃ bibhraty oṣadhayo yam āpaḥ |
(12.1.23.c) yaṃ gandharvā apsarasaś ca bhejire tena mā surabhiṃ kṛṇu mā no dvikṣata kaś cana  ‖23‖
(12.1.24’) yas te gandhaḥ puṣkaram āviveśa yaṃ saṃjabhruḥ sūryāyā vivāhe |
(12.1.24.c) amartyāḥ pṛthivi gandham agre tena mā surabhiṃ kṛṇu mā no dvikṣata kaś cana  ‖24‖
(12.1.25’) yas te gandhaḥ puruṣeṣu strīṣu puṃsu bhago ruciḥ |
(12.1.25.c) yo aśveṣu vīreṣu yo mṛgeṣūta hastiṣu |
(12.1.25.e) kanyāyāṃ varco yad bhūme tenāsmā;m api saṃ sṛja mā no dvikṣata kaś cana  ‖25‖
(12.1.26’) śilā bhūmir aśmā pāṃsuḥ sā bhūmiḥ saṃdhṛtā dhṛtā |
(12.1.26.c) tasyai hiraṇyavakṣase pṛthivyā akaraṃ namaḥ  ‖26‖
(12.1.27’) yasyāṃ vṛkṣā vānaspatyā dhruvās tiṣṭhanti viśvahā |
(12.1.27.c) pṛthivīṃ viśvadhāyasaṃ dhṛtām achāvadāmasi  ‖27‖
(12.1.28’) udīrāṇā utāsīnās tiṣṭhantaḥ prakrāmantaḥ |
(12.1.28.c) padbhyāṃ dakṣiṇasavyābhyāṃ mā vyathiṣmahi bhūmyām  ‖28‖
(12.1.29’) vimṛgvarīṃ pṛthivīm ā vadāmi kṣamāṃ bhūmiṃ brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānām |
(12.1.29.c) ūrjaṃ puṣṭaṃ bibhratīm annabhāgaṃ ghṛtaṃ tvābhi ni ṣīdema bhūme  ‖29‖
(12.1.30’) śuddhā na āpas tanve kṣarantu yo naḥ sedur apriye taṃ ni dadhmaḥ  |
(12.1.30.c) pavitreṇa pṛthivi mot punāmi  ‖30‖ {3}
(12.1.31’) yās te prācīḥ pradiśo yā udīcīr yās te bhūme adharād yāś ca paścāt	|
(12.1.31.c) syonās tā mahyaṃ carate bhavantu mā ni paptaṃ bhuvane śiśriyāṇaḥ	‖31‖
(12.1.32’) mā naḥ paścān mā purastān nudiṣṭhā mottarād adharād uta |
(12.1.32.c) svasti bhūme no bhava mā vidan paripanthino varīyo yāvayā vadham	‖32‖
(12.1.33’) yāvat te 'bhi vipaśyāmi bhūme sūryeṇa medinā |
(12.1.33.c) tāvan me cakṣur mā meṣṭottarāmuttarāṃ samām  ‖33‖
(12.1.34’) yac chayānaḥ paryāvarte dakṣiṇaṃ sakhyam abhi bhūme pārśvam uttānās tvā pratīcīṃ yat pṛṣṭībhir adhiśemahe |
(12.1.34.c) mā hiṃsīs tatra no bhūme sarvasya pratiśīvari  ‖34‖
(12.1.35’) yat te bhūme vikhanāmi kṣipraṃ tad api rohatu  |
(12.1.35.c) mā te marma vimṛgvari mā te hṛdayam arpipam  ‖35‖
(12.1.36’) grīṣmas te bhūme varṣāṇi śarad dhemantaḥ śiśiro vasantaḥ  |
(12.1.36.c) ṛtavas te vihitā hāyanīr ahorātre pṛthivi no duhātām  ‖36‖
(12.1.37’) yāpa sarpaṃ vijamānā vimṛgvarī yasyām āsann agnayo ye apsv antaḥ  |
(12.1.37.c) parā dasyūn dadatī devapīyūn indraṃ vṛṇānā pṛthivī na vṛtram śakrāya dadhre vṛṣabhāya vṛṣṇe  ‖37‖
(12.1.38’) yasyāṃ sadohavirdhāne yūpo yasyāṃ nimīyate |
(12.1.38.c) brahmāṇo yasyām arcanty ṛgbhiḥ sāmnā yajurvidaḥ yujyante yasyām ṛtvijaḥ somam indrāya pātave  ‖38‖
(12.1.39’) yasyāṃ pūrve bhūtakṛta ṛṣayo gā udānṛcuḥ |
(12.1.39.c) sapta satreṇa vedhaso yajñena tapasā saha	‖39‖
(12.1.40’) sā no bhūmir ā diśatu yad dhanaṃ kāmayāmahe |
(12.1.40.c) bhago anuprayuṅktām indra etu purogavaḥ  ‖40‖ {4}
(12.1.41’) yasyāṃ gāyanti nṛtyanti bhūmyāṃ martyā vyailabāḥ |
(12.1.41.c) yudhyante yasyām ākrando yasyām vadati dundubhiḥ |
(12.1.41.e) sā no bhūmiḥ pra ṇudatāṃ sapatnān asapatnaṃ mā pṛthivī kṛṇotu  ‖41‖
(12.1.42’) yasyām annaṃ vrīhiyavau yasyā imāḥ pañca kṛṣṭayaḥ |
(12.1.42.c) bhūmyai parjanyapatnyai namo 'stu varṣamedase  ‖42‖
(12.1.43’) yasyāḥ puro devakṛtāḥ kṣetre yasyā vikurvate |
(12.1.43.c) prajāpatiḥ pṛthivīṃ viśvagarbhām āśāmāśāṃ raṇyāṃ naḥ kṛṇotu  ‖43‖
(12.1.44’) nidhiṃ bibhratī bahudhā guhā vasu maṇiṃ hiraṇyaṃ pṛthivī dadātu me |
(12.1.44.c) vasūni no vasudā rāsamānā devī dadhātu sumanasyamānā	‖44‖
(12.1.45’) janaṃ bibhratī bahudhā vivācasaṃ nānādharmāṇaṃ pṛthivī yathaukasam |
(12.1.45.c) sahasraṃ dhārā draviṇasya me duhāṃ dhruveva dhenur anapasphurantī  ‖45‖
(12.1.46’) yas te sarpo vṛścikas tṛṣṭadaṃśmā hemantajabdho bhṛmalo guhā śaye |
(12.1.46.c) krimir jinvat pṛthivi yadyad ejati prāvṛṣi tan naḥ sarpan mopa sṛpad yac chivaṃ tena no mṛḍa  ‖46‖
(12.1.47’) ye te panthāno bahavo janāyanā rathasya vartmānasaś ca yātave  |
(12.1.47.c) yaiḥ saṃcaranty ubhaye bhadrapāpās taṃ panthānaṃ jayemānamitram ataskaraṃ yac chivaṃ tena no mṛḍa  ‖47‖
(12.1.48’) malvaṃ bibhratī gurubhṛd bhadrapāpasya nidhanaṃ titikṣuḥ |
(12.1.48.c) varāheṇa pṛthivī saṃvidānā sūkarāya vi jihīte mṛgāya  ‖48‖
(12.1.49’) ye ta āraṇyāḥ paśavo mṛgā vane hitāḥ siṃhā vyāghrāḥ puruṣādaś caranti  |
(12.1.49.c) ulaṃ vṛkaṃ pṛthivi duchunām ita ṛkṣīkāṃ rakṣo apa bādhayāsmat	‖49‖
(12.1.50’) ye gandharvā apsaraso ye cārāyāḥ kimīdinaḥ |
(12.1.50.c) piśācānt sarvā rakṣāṃsi tān asmad bhūme yāvaya  ‖50‖ {5}
(12.1.51’) yāṃ dvipādaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ saṃpatanti haṃsāḥ suparṇāḥ śakunā vayāṃsi  |
(12.1.51.c) yasyāṃ vāto mātariśveyate rajāṃsi kṛṇvaṃś cyāvayaṃś ca vṛkṣān |
(12.1.51.e) vātasya pravām upavām anu vāty arciḥ  ‖51‖
(12.1.52’) yasyāṃ kṛṣṇam aruṇaṃ ca saṃhite ahorātre vihite bhūmyām adhi |
(12.1.52.c) varṣeṇa bhūmiḥ pṛthivī vṛtāvṛtā sā no dadhātu bhadrayā priye dhāmanidhāmani	‖52‖
(12.1.53’) dyauś ca ma idaṃ pṛthivī cāntarikṣaṃ ca me vyacaḥ |
(12.1.53.c) agniḥ sūrya āpo medhāṃ viśve devāś ca saṃ daduḥ  ‖53‖
(12.1.54’) aham asmi sahamāna uttaro nāma bhūmyām |
(12.1.54.c) abhīṣāḍ asmi viśvāṣāḍ āśāmāśāṃ viṣāsahiḥ  ‖54‖
(12.1.55’) ado yad devi prathamānā purastād devair uktā vyasarpo mahitvam |
(12.1.55.c) ā tvā subhūtam aviśat tadānīm akalpayathāḥ pradiśaś catasraḥ  ‖55‖
(12.1.56’) ye grāmā yad araṇyaṃ yāḥ sabhā adhi bhūmyām |
(12.1.56.c) ye saṃgrāmāḥ samitayas teṣu cāru vadema te  ‖56‖
(12.1.57’) aśva iva rajo dudhuve vi tān janān ya ākṣiyan pṛthivīṃ yād ajāyata |
(12.1.57.c) mandrāgretvarī bhuvanasya gopā vanaspatīnāṃ gṛbhir oṣadhīnām  ‖57‖
(12.1.58’) yad vadāmi madhumat tad vadāmi yad īkṣe tad vananti mā |
(12.1.58.c) tviṣīmān asmi jūtimān avānyān hanmi dodhataḥ  ‖58‖
(12.1.59’) śantivā surabhiḥ syonā kīlālodhnī payasvatī |
(12.1.59.c) bhūmir adhi bravītu me pṛthivī payasā saha ‖59‖
(12.1.60’) yām anvaichad dhaviṣā viśvakarmāntar arṇave rajasi praviṣṭām |
(12.1.60.c) bhujiṣyaṃ pātraṃ nihitaṃ guhā yad āvir bhoge abhavan mātṛmadbhyaḥ ‖60‖
(12.1.61’) tvam asy āvapanī janānām aditiḥ kāmadughā paprathānā |
(12.1.61.c) yat ta ūnaṃ tat ta ā pūrayāti prajāpatiḥ prathamajā ṛtasya ‖61‖
(12.1.62’) upasthās te anamīvā ayakṣmā asmabhyaṃ santu pṛthivi prasūtāḥ |
(12.1.62.c) dīrghaṃ na āyuḥ pratibudhyamānā vayaṃ tubhyaṃ balihṛtaḥ syāma ‖62‖
(12.1.63’) bhūme mātar ni dhehi mā bhadrayā supratiṣṭhitam |
(12.1.63.c) saṃvidānā divā kave śriyāṃ mā dhehi bhūtyām ‖63‖ {6}

(12.2.1’) naḍam ā roha na te atra loka idaṃ sīsaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ ta ehi |
(12.2.1.c) yo goṣu yakṣmaḥ puruṣeṣu yakṣmas tena tvaṃ sākam adharāṅ parehi ‖1‖
(12.2.2’) aghaśaṃsaduḥśaṃsābhyāṃ kareṇānukareṇa ca |
(12.2.2.c) yakṣmaṃ ca sarvaṃ teneto mṛtyuṃ ca nir ajāmasi ‖2‖
(12.2.3’) nir ito mṛtyuṃ nirṛtiṃ nir arātim ajāmasi |
(12.2.3.c) yo no dveṣṭi tam addhy agne akravyād yam u dviṣmas tam u te pra suvāmasi ‖3‖
(12.2.4’) yady agniḥ kravyād yadi vā vyāghra imaṃ goṣṭhaṃ praviveśānyokāḥ |
(12.2.4.c) taṃ māṣājyaṃ kṛtvā pra hiṇomi dūraṃ sa gachatv apsuṣado 'py agnīn ‖4‖
(12.2.5’) yat tvā kruddhāḥ pracakrur manyunā puruṣe mṛte |
(12.2.5.c) sukalpam agne tat tvayā punas tvod dīpayāmasi ‖5‖
(12.2.6’) punas tvādityā rudrā vasavaḥ punar brahmā vasunītir agne |
(12.2.6.c) punas tvā brahmaṇas patir ādhād dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ‖6‖
(12.2.7’) yo agniḥ kravyāt praviveśa no gṛham imaṃ paśyann itaraṃ jātavedasam |
(12.2.7.c) taṃ harāmi pitṛyajñāya dūraṃ sa gharmam indhāṃ parame sadhasthe ‖7‖
(12.2.8’) kravyādam agniṃ pra hiṇomi dūram yamarājño gachatu ripravāhaḥ |
(12.2.8.c) ihāyam itaro jātavedā devo devebhyo havyaṃ vahatu prajānan ‖8‖
(12.2.9’) kravyādam agnim iṣito harāmi janān dṛṃhantaṃ vajreṇa mṛtyum |
(12.2.9.c) ni taṃ śāsmi gārhapatyena vidvān pitṝṇāṃ loke 'pi bhāgo astu ‖9‖
(12.2.10’) kravyādam agniṃ śaśamānam ukthyaṃ pra hiṇomi pathibhiḥ pitṛyāṇaiḥ |
(12.2.10.c) mā devayānaiḥ punar ā gā atraivaidhi pitṛṣu jāgṛhi tvam ‖10‖ {7}
(12.2.11’) sam indhate saṃkasukaṃ svastaye śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ |
(12.2.11.c) jahāti ripram aty ena eti samiddho agniḥ supunā punāti ‖11‖
(12.2.12’) devo agniḥ saṃkasuko divas pṛṣṭhāny āruhat |
(12.2.12.c) mucyamāno nir eṇaso 'mog asmā;m aśastyāḥ ‖12‖
(12.2.13’) asmin vayaṃ saṃkasuke agnau riprāṇi mṛjmahe |
(12.2.13.c) abhūma yajñiyāḥ śuddhāḥ pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat ‖13‖
(12.2.14’) saṃkasuko vikasuko nirṛtho yaś ca nisvaraḥ |
(12.2.14.c) te te yakṣmaṃ savedaso dūrād dūram anīnaśan ‖14‖
(12.2.15’) yo no aśveṣu vīreṣu yo no goṣv ajāviṣu |
(12.2.15.c) kravyādaṃ nir ṇudāmasi yo agnir janayopanaḥ ‖15‖
(12.2.16’) anyebhyas tvā puruṣebhyo gobhyo aśvebhyas tvā |
(12.2.16.c) niḥ kravyādaṃ nudāmasi yo agnir jīvitayopanaḥ ‖16‖
(12.2.17’) yasmin devā amṛjata yasmin manuṣyā uta |
(12.2.17.c) tasmin ghṛtastāvo mṛṣṭvā tvam agne divaṃ ruha ‖17‖
(12.2.18’) samiddho agna āhuta sa no mābhyapakramīḥ |
(12.2.18.c) atraiva dīdihi dyavi jyok ca sūryaṃ dṛśe ‖18‖
(12.2.19’) sīse mṛḍḍhvaṃ nade mṛḍḍhvam agnau saṃkasuke ca yat |
(12.2.19.c) atho avyāṃ rāmāyāṃ śīrṣaktim upabarhaṇe ‖19‖
(12.2.20’) sīse malaṃ sādayitvā śīrṣaktim upabarhaṇe |
(12.2.20.c) avyām asiknyāṃ mṛṣṭvā śuddhā bhavata yajñiyāḥ ‖20‖ {8}
(12.2.21’) paraṃ mṛtyo anu parehi panthāṃ yas ta eṣa itaro devayānāt |
(12.2.21.c) cakṣuṣmate śṛṇvate te bravīmīheme vīrā bahavo bhavantu ‖21‖
(12.2.22’) ime jīvā vi mṛtair āvavṛtrann abhūd bhadrā devahutir no adya |
(12.2.22.c) prāñco agāma nṛtaye hasāya suvīrāso vidatham ā vadema ‖22‖
(12.2.23’) imaṃ jīvebhyaḥ paridhiṃ dadhāmi maiṣāṃ nu gād aparo artham etam |
(12.2.23.c) śataṃ jīvantaḥ śaradaḥ purūcīs tiro mṛtyuṃ dadhatām parvatena ‖23‖
(12.2.24’) ā rohatāyur jarasaṃ vṛṇānā anupūrvaṃ yatamānā yati stha |
(12.2.24.c) tān vas tvaṣṭā sujanimā sajoṣāḥ sarvam āyur nayatu jīvanāya ‖24‖
(12.2.25’) yathāhāny anupūrvaṃ bhavanti yatha rtava ṛtubhir yanti sākam |
(12.2.25.c) yathā na pūrvam aparo jahāty evā dhātar āyūṃṣi kalpayaiṣām ‖25‖
(12.2.26’) aśmanvatī rīyate saṃ rabhadhvaṃ vīrayadhvaṃ pra taratā sakhāyaḥ |
(12.2.26.c) atrā jahīta ye asan durevā anamīvān ut taremābhi vājān ‖26‖
(12.2.27’) ut tiṣṭhatā pra taratā sakhāyo 'śmanvatī nadī syandata iyam |
(12.2.27.c) atrā jahīta ye asann aśivāḥ śivānt syonān ut taremābhi vājān ‖27‖
(12.2.28’) vaiśvadevīṃ varcasā ā rabhadhvaṃ śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ |
(12.2.28.c) atikrāmanto duritā padāni śataṃ himāḥ sarvavīrā madema ‖28‖
(12.2.29’) udīcīnaiḥ pathibhir vāyumadbhir atikrāmanto 'varān parebhiḥ |
(12.2.29.c) triḥ sapta kṛtva ṛṣayaḥ paretā mṛtyuṃ praty auhan padayopanena ‖29‖
(12.2.30’) mṛtyoḥ padaṃ yopayanta eta drāghīya āyuḥ prataraṃ dadhānāḥ |
(12.2.30.c) āsīnā mṛtyuṃ nudatā sadhasthe 'tha jīvāso vidatham ā vadema ‖30‖ {9}
(12.2.31’) imā nārīr avidhavāḥ supatnīr āñjanena sarpiṣā saṃ spṛśantām |
(12.2.31.c) anaśravo anamīvāḥ suratnā ā rohantu janayo yonim agre ‖31‖
(12.2.32’) vyākaromi haviṣāham etau tau brahmaṇā vy ahaṃ kalpayāmi |
(12.2.32.c) svadhāṃ pitṛbhyo ajarāṃ kṛṇomi dīrgheṇāyuṣā sam imānt sṛjāmi ‖32‖
(12.2.33’) yo no agniḥ pitaro hṛtsv antar āviveśāmṛto martyeṣu |
(12.2.33.c) mayy ahaṃ taṃ pari gṛhṇāmi devaṃ mā so asmān dvikṣata mā vayaṃ tam ‖33‖
(12.2.34’) apāvṛtya gārhapatyāt kravyādā preta dakṣiṇā |
(12.2.34.c) priyaṃ pitṛbhya ātmane brahmabhyaḥ kṛṇutā priyam ‖34‖
(12.2.35’) dvibhāgadhanam ādāya pra kṣiṇāty avartyā |
(12.2.35.c) agniḥ putrasya jyeṣṭhasya yaḥ kravyād anirāhitah ‖35‖
(12.2.36’) yat kṛṣate yad vanute yac ca vasnena vindate |
(12.2.36.c) sarvaṃ martyasya tan nāsti kravyāc ced anirāhitaḥ ‖36‖
(12.2.37’) ayajñiyo hatavarcā bhavati nainena havir attave |
(12.2.37.c) chinatti kṛṣyā gor dhanād yaṃ kravyād anuvartate ‖37‖
(12.2.38’) muhur gṛdhyaiḥ pra vadaty ārtim martyo nītya |
(12.2.38.c) kravyād yān agnir antikād anuvidvān vitāvati ‖38‖
(12.2.39’) grāhyā gṛhāḥ saṃ sṛjyante striyā yan mriyate patiḥ |
(12.2.39.c) brahmaiva vidvān eṣyo yaḥ kravyādaṃ nirādadhat ‖39‖
(12.2.40’) yad ripraṃ śamalaṃ cakṛma yac ca duṣkṛtam |
(12.2.40.c) āpo mā tasmāc chumbhantv agneḥ saṃkasukāc ca yat ‖40‖ {10}
(12.2.41’) tā adharād udīcīr āvavṛtran prajānaitīḥ pathibhir devayānaiḥ |
(12.2.41.c) parvatasya vṛṣabhasyādhi pṛṣṭhe navāś caranti saritaḥ purāṇīḥ ‖41‖
(12.2.42’) agne akravyān niḥ kravyādaṃ nudā devayajanaṃ vaha ‖42‖
(12.2.43’) imaṃ kravyād ā viveśāyaṃ kravyādam anv agāt |
(12.2.43.c) vyāghrau kṛtvā nānānaṃ taṃ harāmi śivāparam ‖43‖
(12.2.44’) antardhir devānāṃ paridhir manuṣyāṇām agnir gārhpatya ubhayān antarā śritaḥ ‖44‖
(12.2.45’) jīvānām āyuḥ pra tira tvam agne pitṝṇāṃ lokam api gachantu ye mṛtāḥ |
(12.2.45.c) sugārhapatyo vitapann arātim uṣāmuṣāṃ śreyasīṃ dhehy asmai ‖45‖
(12.2.46’) sarvān agne sahamānaḥ sapatnān aiṣām ūrjaṃ rayim asmāsu dhehi ‖46‖
(12.2.47’) imam indraṃ vahniṃ paprim anvārabhadhvaṃ sa vo nir vakṣad duritād avadyāt |
(12.2.47.c) tenāpa hata śarum āpatantaṃ tena rudrasya pari pātāstām ‖47‖
(12.2.48’) anaḍvāhaṃ plavam anvārabhadhvaṃ sa vo nir vakṣad duritād avadyāt |
(12.2.48.c) ā rohata savitur nāvam etāṃ ṣaḍbhir urvībhir amatiṃ tarema ‖48‖
(12.2.49’) ahorātre anv eṣi bibhrat kṣemyas tiṣṭhan prataraṇaḥ suvīraḥ |
(12.2.49.c) anāturānt sumanasas talpa bibhraj jyog eva naḥ puruṣagandhir edhi ‖49‖
(12.2.50’) te devebhya ā vṛścante pāpaṃ jīvanti sarvadā |
(12.2.50.c) kravyād yān agnir antikād aśva ivānuvapate naḍam ‖50‖ {11}
(12.2.51’) ye 'śraddhā dhanakāmyā kravyādā samāsate |
(12.2.51.c) te vā anyeṣāṃ kumbhīṃ paryādadhati sarvadā ‖51‖
(12.2.52’) preva pipatiṣati manasā muhur ā vartate punaḥ |
(12.2.52.c) kravyād yān agnir antikād anuvidvān vitāvati ‖52‖
(12.2.53’) aviḥ kṛṣṇā bhāgadheyaṃ paśūnāṃ sīsaṃ kravyād api candraṃ ta āhuḥ |
(12.2.53.c) māṣāḥ piṣṭā bhāgadheyaṃ te havyam araṇyānyā gahvaraṃ sacasva ‖53‖
(12.2.54’) iṣīkāṃ jaratīm iṣṭvā tilpiñjaṃ daṇḍanaṃ naḍam |
(12.2.54.c) tam indra idhmam kṛtvā yamasyāgniṃ nirādadhau ‖54‖
(12.2.55’) pratyañcam arkaṃ pratyarpayitvā pravidvān panthāṃ vi hy āviveśa |
(12.2.55.c) parāmīṣām asūn dideśa dīrgheṇāyuṣā sam imānt sṛjāmi ‖55‖ {12}

(12.3.1’) pumān puṃso 'dhi tiṣṭha carmehi tatra hvayasva yatamā priyā te |
(12.3.1.c) yāvantāv agre prathamaṃ sameyathus tad vāṃ vayo yamarājye samānam ‖1‖
(12.3.2’) tāvad vāṃ cakṣus tati vīryāṇi tāvat tejas tatidhā vājināni |
(12.3.2.c) agniḥ śarīraṃ sacate yadaidho 'dhā pakvān mithunā saṃ bhavāthaḥ ‖2‖
(12.3.3’) sam asmiṃl loke sam u devayāne saṃ smā sametaṃ yamarājyeṣu |
(12.3.3.c) pūtau pavitrair upa tad dhvayethāṃ yadyad reto adhi vāṃ saṃbabhūva ‖3‖
(12.3.4’) āpas putrāso abhi saṃ viśadhvam imaṃ jīvaṃ jīvadhanyāḥ sametya |
(12.3.4.c) tāsāṃ bhajadhvam amṛtaṃ yam āhur odanaṃ pacati vāṃ janitrī ‖4‖
(12.3.5’) yaṃ vāṃ pitā pacati yaṃ ca mātā riprān nirmuktyai śamalāc ca vācaḥ |
(12.3.5.c) sa odanaḥ śatadhāraḥ svarga ubhe vy āpa nabhasī mahitvā ‖5‖
(12.3.6’) ubhe nabhasī ubhayāṃś ca lokān ye yajvanām abhijitāḥ svargāḥ |
(12.3.6.c) teṣāṃ jyotiṣmān madhumān yo agre tasmin putrair jarasi saṃ śrayethām ‖6‖
(12.3.7’) prācīṃprācīṃ pradiśam ā rabhethām etaṃ lokaṃ śraddadhānāḥ sacante |
(12.3.7.c) yad vāṃ pakvaṃ pariviṣṭam agnau tasya guptaye daṃpatī saṃ śrayethām ‖7‖
(12.3.8’) dakṣiṇāṃ diśam abhi nakṣamāṇau paryāvartethām abhi pātram etat |
(12.3.8.c) tasmin vāṃ yamaḥ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidānaḥ pakvāya śarma bahulaṃ ni yachāt ‖8‖
(12.3.9’) pratīcī diśām iyam id varaṃ yasyāṃ somo adhipā mṛḍitā ca |
(12.3.9.c) tasyāṃ śrayethāṃ sukṛtaḥ sacethām adhā pakvān mithunā saṃ bhavāthaḥ ‖9‖
(12.3.10’) uttaraṃ rāṣṭraṃ prajayottarāvad diśām udīcī kṛṇavan no agram |
(12.3.10.c) pāṅktaṃ chandaḥ puruṣo babhūva viśvair viśvāṅgaiḥ saha saṃ bhavema ‖10‖ {13}
(12.3.11’) dhruveyaṃ virāṇ namo astv asyai śivā putrebhya uta mahyam astu |
(12.3.11.c) sā no devy adite viśvavāra irya iva gopā abhi rakṣa pakvam ‖11‖
(12.3.12’) piteva putrān abhi saṃ svajasva naḥ śivā no vātā iha vāntu bhūmau |
(12.3.12.c) yam odanaṃ pacato devate iha taṃ nas tapa uta satyaṃ ca vettu ‖12‖
(12.3.13’) yadyad kṛṣṇaḥ śakuna eha gatvā tsaran viṣaktaṃ bila āsasāda |
(12.3.13.c) yad vā dāsy ārdrahastā samaṅkta ulūkhalaṃ musalaṃ śumbhatāpaḥ ‖13‖
(12.3.14’) ayaṃ grāvā pṛthubudhno vayodhāḥ pūtaḥ pavitrair apa hantu rakṣaḥ |
(12.3.14.c) ā roha carma mahi śarma yacha mā daṃpatī pautram aghaṃ ni gātām ‖14‖
(12.3.15’) vanaspatiḥ saha devair na āgan rakṣaḥ piśācā;m apabādhamānaḥ |
(12.3.15.c) sa uc chrayātai pra vadāti vācaṃ tena lokā;m abhi sarvān jayema ‖15‖
(12.3.16’) sapta medhān paśavaḥ pary agṛhṇan ya eṣāṃ jyotiṣmā;m uta yaś cakarśa |
(12.3.16.c) trayastriṃśad devatās tān sacante sa naḥ svargam abhi neṣa lokam ‖16‖
(12.3.17’) svargaṃ lokam abhi no nayāsi saṃ jāyayā saha putraiḥ syāma |
(12.3.17.c) gṛhṇāmi hastam anu maitv atra mā nas tārīn nirṛtir mo arātiḥ ‖17‖
(12.3.18’) grāhiṃ pāpmānam ati tā;m ayāma tamo vy asya pra vadāsi valgu |
(12.3.18.c) vānaspatya udyato mā jihiṃsīr mā taṇḍulaṃ vi śarīr devayantam ‖18‖
(12.3.19’) viśvavyacā ghṛtapṛṣṭho bhaviṣyant sayonir lokam upa yāhy etam |
(12.3.19.c) varṣavṛddham upa yacha śūrpaṃ tuṣaṃ palāvān apa tad vinaktu ‖19‖
(12.3.20’) trayo lokāḥ saṃmitā brāhmaṇena dyaur evāsau pṛthivy antarikṣam |
(12.3.20.c) aṃśūn gṛbhītvānvārabhethām ā pyāyantāṃ punar ā yantu śūrpam ‖20‖ {14}
(12.3.21’) pṛthag rūpāṇi bahudhā paśūnām ekarūpo bhavasi saṃ samṛddhyā |
(12.3.21.c) etāṃ tvacaṃ lohinīṃ tāṃ nudasva grāvā śumbhāti malaga iva vastrā ‖21‖
(12.3.22’) pṛthivīṃ tvā pṛthivyām ā veśayāmi tanūḥ samānī vikṛtā ta eṣā |
(12.3.22.c) yadyad dyuttaṃ likhitam arpaṇena tena mā susror brahmaṇāpi tad vapāmi ‖22‖
(12.3.23’) janitrīva prati haryāsi sūnuṃ saṃ tvā dadhāmi pṛthivīṃ pṛthivyā |
(12.3.23.c) ukhā kumbhī vedyāṃ mā vyathiṣṭhā yajñāyudhair ājyenātiṣaktā ‖23‖
(12.3.24’) agniḥ pacan rakṣatu tvā purastād indro rakṣatu dakṣiṇato marutvān |
(12.3.24.c) varuṇas tvā dṛṃhād dharuṇe pratīcyā uttarāt tvā somaḥ saṃ dadātai ‖24‖
(12.3.25’) pūtāḥ pavitraiḥ pavante abhrād divaṃ ca yanti pṛthivīṃ ca lokān |
(12.3.25.c) tā jīvalā jīvadhanyāḥ pratiṣṭhāḥ pātra āsiktāḥ pary agnir indhām ‖25‖
(12.3.26’) ā yanti divaḥ pṛthivīṃ sacante bhūmyāḥ sacante adhy antarikṣam |
(12.3.26.c) śuddhāḥ satīs tā u śumbhanta eva tā naḥ svargam abhi lokaṃ nayantu ‖26‖
(12.3.27’) uteva prabhvīr uta saṃmitāsa uta śukrāḥ śucayaś cāmṛtāsaḥ |
(12.3.27.c) tā odanaṃ daṃpatibhyāṃ praśiṣṭā āpaḥ śikṣantīḥ pacatā sunāthāḥ ‖27‖
(12.3.28’) saṃkhyātā stokāḥ pṛthivīṃ sacante prāṇāpānaiḥ saṃmitā oṣadhībhiḥ |
(12.3.28.c) asaṃkhyātā opyamānāḥ suvarṇāḥ sarvaṃ vyāpuḥ śucayaḥ śucitvam ‖28‖
(12.3.29’) ud yodhanty abhi valganti taptāḥ phenam asyanti bahulāṃś ca bindūn |
(12.3.29.c) yoṣeva dṛṣṭvā patim ṛtviyāyaitais taṇḍulair bhavatā sam āpaḥ ‖29‖
(12.3.30’) ut thāpaya sīdato budhna enān adbhir ātmānam abhi saṃ spṛśantām |
(12.3.30.c) amāsi pātrair udakaṃ yad etan mitās taṇḍulāḥ pradiśo yadīmāḥ ‖30‖ {15}
(12.3.31’) pra yacha parśuṃ tvarayā harausam ahiṃsanta oṣadhīr dāntu parvan |
(12.3.31.c) vāsāṃ somaḥ pari rājyaṃ babhūvāmanyutā no vīrudho bhavantu ‖31‖
(12.3.32’) navaṃ barhir odanāya stṛṇīta priyaṃ hṛdaś cakṣuṣo valgv astu |
(12.3.32.c) tasmin devāḥ saha daivīr viśantv imaṃ prāśnantv ṛtubhir niṣadya ‖32‖
(12.3.33’) vanaspate stīrṇam ā sīda barhir agniṣṭobhaiḥ saṃmito devatābhiḥ |
(12.3.33.c) tvaṣṭreva rūpaṃ sukṛtaṃ svadhityainā ehāḥ pari pātre dadṛśrām ‖33‖
(12.3.34’) ṣaṣṭyāṃ śaratsu nidhipā abhīchāt svaḥ pakvenābhy aśnavātai |
(12.3.34.c) upainaṃ jīvān pitaraś ca putrā etaṃ svargaṃ gamayāntam agneḥ ‖34‖
(12.3.35’) dhartā dhriyasva dharuṇe pṛthivyā acyutaṃ tvā devatāś cyāvayantu |
(12.3.35.c) taṃ tvā daṃpatī jīvantau jīvaputrāv ud vāsayātaḥ pary agnidhānāt ‖35‖
(12.3.36’) sarvānt samāgā abhijitya lokān yāvantaḥ kāmāḥ sam atītṛpas tān |
(12.3.36.c) vi gāhethām āyavanaṃ ca darvir ekasmin pātre adhy ud dharainam ‖36‖
(12.3.37’) upa stṛṇīhi prathaya purastād ghṛtena pātram abhi ghārayaitat |
(12.3.37.c) vāśrevosrā taruṇaṃ stanasyum imaṃ devāso abhihiṅkṛṇota ‖37‖
(12.3.38’) upāstarīr akaro lokam etam uruḥ prathatām asamaḥ svargaḥ |
(12.3.38.c) tasmiṃ chrayātai mahiṣaḥ suparṇo devā enaṃ devatābhyaḥ pra yachān ‖38‖
(12.3.39’) yadyaj jāyā pacati tvat paraḥparaḥ patir vā jāye tvat tiraḥ |
(12.3.39.c) saṃ tat sṛjethāṃ saha vāṃ tad astu saṃpādayantau saha lokam ekam ‖39‖
(12.3.40’) yāvanto asyāḥ pṛthivīṃ sacante asmat putrāḥ pari ye saṃbabhūvuḥ |
(12.3.40.c) sarvāṃs tā;m upa pātre hvayethāṃ nābhiṃ jānānāḥ śiśavaḥ samāyān ‖40‖ {16}
(12.3.41’) vasor yā dhārā madhunā prapīnā ghṛtena miśrā amṛtasya nābhayaḥ |
(12.3.41.c) sarvās tā ava rundhe svargaḥ ṣaṣṭyāṃ śaratsu nidhipā abhīchāt ‖41‖
(12.3.42’) nidhiṃ nidhipā abhy enam ichād anīśvarā abhitaḥ santu ye 'nye |
(12.3.42.c) asmābhir datto nihitaḥ svargas tribhiḥ kāṇḍais trīnt svargān arukṣat ‖42‖
(12.3.43’) agnī rakṣas tapatu yad videvaṃ kravyād piśāca iha mā pra pāsta |
(12.3.43.c) nudāma enam apa rudhmo asmad ādityā enam aṅgirasaḥ sacantām ‖43‖
(12.3.44’) ādityebhyo aṅgirobhyo madhv idaṃ ghṛtena miśraṃ prati vedayāmi |
(12.3.44.c) śuddhahastau brāhmaṇasyānihatyaitaṃ svargaṃ sukṛtāv apītam ‖44‖

(12.3.46’) satyāya ca tapase devatābhyo nidhiṃ śevadhiṃ pari dadma etam |

(12.3.47’) ahaṃ pacāmy ahaṃ dadāmi mamed u karman karuṇe 'dhi jāyā |
(12.3.47.c) kaumāro loko ajaniṣṭa putro 'nvārabhethāṃ vaya uttarāvat ‖47‖
(12.3.48’) na kilbiṣam atra nādhāro asti na yan mitraiḥ samamamāna eti |
(12.3.48.c) anūnaṃ pātraṃ nihitaṃ na etat paktāraṃ pakvaḥ punar ā viśāti ‖48‖
(12.3.49’) priyaṃ priyāṇāṃ kṛṇavāma tamas te yantu yatame dviṣanti |
(12.3.49.c) dhenur anaḍvān vayovaya āyad eva pauruṣeyam apa mṛtyuṃ nudantu ‖49‖
(12.3.50’) sam agnayaḥ vidur anyo anyaṃ ya oṣadhīḥ sacate yaś ca sindhūn |
(12.3.50.c) yāvanto devā divy ātapanti hiraṇyaṃ jyotiḥ pacato babhūva ‖50‖ {17}
(12.3.51’) eṣā tvacāṃ puruṣe saṃ babhūvānagnāḥ sarve paśavo ye anye |
(12.3.51.c) kṣatreṇātmānaṃ pari dhāpayātho 'motaṃ vāso mukham odanasya ‖51‖
(12.3.52’) yad akṣeṣu vadā yat samityāṃ yad vā vadā anṛtaṃ vittakāmyā |
(12.3.52.c) samānaṃ tantum abhi samvasānau tasmint sarvaṃ śamalaṃ sādayāthaḥ ‖52‖
(12.3.53’) varṣaṃ vanuṣvāpi gacha devāṃs tvaco dhūmaṃ pary ut pātayāsi |
(12.3.53.c) viśvavyacā ghṛtapṛṣṭho bhaviṣyant sayonir lokam upa yāhy etam ‖53‖
(12.3.54’) tanvaṃ svargo bahudhā vi cakre yathā vida ātmann anyavarṇām |
(12.3.54.c) apājait kṛṣṇāṃ ruśatīṃ punāno yā lohinī tāṃ te agnau juhomi ‖54‖
(12.3.55’) prācyai tvā diśe 'gnaye 'dhipataye 'sitāya rakṣitra ādityāyeṣumate |
(12.3.55.c) etaṃ pari dadmas taṃ no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ |
(12.3.55.e) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ‖55‖
(12.3.56’) dakṣiṇāyai tvā diśa indrāyādhipataye tiraścirājaye rakṣitre yamāyeṣumate |
(12.3.56.c) etaṃ pari dadmas taṃ no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ |
(12.3.56.e) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ‖56‖
(12.3.57’) pratīcyai tvā diśe varuṇāyādhipataye pṛdākave rakṣitre 'nnāyeṣumate |
(12.3.57.c) etaṃ pari dadmas taṃ no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ |
(12.3.57.e) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ‖57‖
(12.3.58’) udīcyai tvā diśe somāyādhipataye svajāya rakṣitre 'śanyā iṣumatyai |
(12.3.58.c) etaṃ pari dadmas taṃ no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ |
(12.3.58.e) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ‖58‖

(12.3.59.c) etaṃ pari dadmas taṃ no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ |

(12.3.60’) ūrdhvāyai tvā diśe bṛhaspataye 'dhipataye śvitrāya rakṣitre varṣāyeṣumate |
(12.3.60.c) etaṃ pari dadmas taṃ no gopāyatāsmākam aitoḥ |
(12.3.60.e) diṣṭaṃ no atra jarase ni neṣaj jarā mṛtyave pari ṇo dadātv atha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ‖60‖ {18}

(12.4.1’) dadāmīty eva brūyād anu cainām abhutsata |
(12.4.1.c) vaśāṃ brahmabhyo yācadbhyas tat prajāvad apatyavat ‖1‖
(12.4.2’) prajayā sa vi krīṇīte paśubhiś copa dasyati |
(12.4.2.c) ya ārṣeyebhyo yācadbhyo devānāṃ gāṃ na ditsati ‖2‖
(12.4.3’) kūṭayāsya saṃ śīryante śloṇayā kāṭam ardati |
(12.4.3.c) baṇḍayā dahyante gṛhāḥ kāṇayā dīyate svam ‖3‖
(12.4.4’) vilohito adhiṣṭhānāc chakno vindati gopatim |
(12.4.4.c) tathā vaśāyāḥ saṃvidyaṃ duradabhnā hy ucyase ‖4‖
(12.4.5’) pador asyā adhiṣṭhānād viklindur nāma vindati |
(12.4.5.c) anāmanāt saṃ śīryante yā mukhenopajighrati ‖5‖
(12.4.6’) yo asyāḥ karṇāv āskunoty ā sa deveṣu vṛścate |
(12.4.6.c) lakṣma kurva iti manyate kanīyaḥ kṛṇute svam ‖6‖
(12.4.7’) yad asyāḥ kasmai cid bhogāya bālān kaś cit prakṛntati |
(12.4.7.c) tataḥ kiśorā mriyante vatsāṃś ca ghātuko vṛkaḥ ‖7‖
(12.4.8’) yad asyā gopatau satyā loma dhvāṅkṣo ajīhiḍat |
(12.4.8.c) tataḥ kumārā mriyante yakṣmo vindaty anāmanāt ‖8‖
(12.4.9’) yad asyāḥ palpūlanaṃ śakṛd dāsī samasyati |
(12.4.9.c) tato 'parūpaṃ jāyate tasmād avyeṣyad enasaḥ ‖9‖
(12.4.10’) jāyamānābhi jāyate devānt sabrāhmaṇān vaśā |
(12.4.10.c) tasmād brahmabhyo deyaiṣā tad āhuḥ svasya gopanam ‖10‖ {19}
(12.4.11’) ya enāṃ vanim āyanti teṣāṃ devakṛtā vaśā |
(12.4.11.c) brahmajyeyaṃ tad abruvan ya enāṃ nipriyāyate ‖11‖
(12.4.12’) ya ārṣeyebhyo yācadbhyo devānāṃ gāṃ na ditsati |
(12.4.12.c) ā sa deveṣu vṛścate brāhmaṇānāṃ ca manyave ‖12‖
(12.4.13’) yo asya syād vaśābhogo anyām icheta tarhi saḥ |

(12.4.14’) yathā śevadhir nihito brāhmaṇānāṃ tathā vaśā |
(12.4.14.c) tām etad achāyanti yasmin kasmiṃś ca jāyate ‖14‖
(12.4.15’) svam etad achāyanti yad vaśāṃ brāhmaṇā abhi |
(12.4.15.c) yathainān anyasmin jinīyād evāsyā nirodhanam ‖15‖
(12.4.16’) cared evā traihāyaṇād avijñātagadā satī |
(12.4.16.c) vaśāṃ ca vidyān nārada brāhmaṇās tarhy eṣyāḥ ‖16‖
(12.4.17’) ya enām avaśām āha devānāṃ nihitaṃ nidhim |

(12.4.18’) yo asyā ūdho na vedātho asyā stanān uta |
(12.4.18.c) ubhayenaivāsmai duhe dātuṃ ced aśakad vaśām ‖18‖
(12.4.19’) duradabhnainam ā śaye yācitāṃ ca na ditsati |

(12.4.39’) mahad eṣāva tapati carantī goṣu gaur api |

(12.4.41’) yā vaśā udakalpayan devā yajñād udetya |

(12.5.53’) śrameṇa tapasā sṛṣṭā brahmaṇā vittā rte śritā ‖1‖
(12.5.2’) satyenāvṛtā śriyā prāvṛtā yaśasā parīvṛtā ‖2‖
(12.5.3’) svadhayā parihitā śraddhayā paryūḍhā dīkṣayā guptā yajñe pratiṣṭhitā loko nidhanam ‖3‖
(12.5.4’) brahma padavāyaṃ brāhmaṇo 'dhipatiḥ ‖4‖
(12.5.5’) tām ādadānasya brahmagavīṃ jinato brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyasya ‖5‖
(12.5.6’) apa krāmati sūnṛtā vīryaṃ punyā lakṣmīḥ ‖6‖ {24}

(12.5.8’) brahma ca kṣatraṃ ca rāṣṭraṃ ca viśaś ca tviṣiś ca yaśaś ca varcaś ca draviṇaṃ ca ‖8‖
(12.5.9’) āyuś ca rūpaṃ ca nāma ca kīrtiś ca prāṇaś cāpānaś ca cakṣuś ca śrotraṃ ca ‖9‖
(12.5.10’) payaś ca rasaś cānnaṃ cānnādyaṃ ca rtaṃ ca satyaṃ ceṣṭaṃ ca pūrtaṃ ca prajā ca paśavaś ca ‖10‖
(12.5.11’) tāni sarvāṇy apa krāmanti brahmagavīm ādadānasya jinato brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyasya ‖11‖ {25}
(12.5.11’) saiṣā bhīmā brahmagavy aghaviṣā sākṣāt kṛtyā kūlbajam āvṛtā ‖12‖
(12.5.13’) sarvāṇy asyāṃ ghorāṇi sarve ca mṛtyavaḥ ‖13‖
(12.5.14’) sarvāṇy asyāṃ krūrāṇi sarve puruṣavadhāḥ ‖14‖
(12.5.15’) sā brahmajyaṃ devapīyuṃ brahmagavy ādīyamānā mṛtyoḥ padvīṣa ā dyati ‖15‖
(12.5.16’) meniḥ śatavadhā hi sā brahmajyasya kṣitir hi sā ‖16‖
(12.5.17’) tasmād vai brāhmaṇānāṃ gaur durādharṣā vijānatā ‖17‖
(12.5.18’) vajro dhāvantī vaiśvānara udvītā ‖18‖
(12.5.19’) hetiḥ śaphān utkhidantī mahādevo 'pekṣamāṇā ‖19‖
(12.5.20’) kṣurapavir īkṣamāṇā vāśyamānābhi sphūrjati ‖20‖
(12.5.21’) mṛtyur hiṅkṛṇvaty ugro devaḥ puchaṃ paryasyantī ‖21‖
(12.5.22’) sarvajyāniḥ karṇau varīvarjayantī rājayakṣmo mehantī ‖22‖
(12.5.23’) menir duhyamānā śīrṣaktir dugdhā ‖23‖
(12.5.24’) sedir upatiṣṭhantī mithoyodhaḥ parāmṛṣṭā ‖24‖
(12.5.25’) śaravyā mukhe 'pinahyamāna ṛtir hanyamānā ‖25‖
(12.5.26’) aghaviṣā nipatantī tamo nipatitā ‖26‖
(12.5.27’) anugachantī prāṇān upa dāsayati brahmagavī brahmajyasya ‖27‖ {26}
(12.5.27’) vairaṃ vikṛtyamānā pautrādyaṃ vibhājyamānā ‖28‖
(12.5.29’) devahetir hriyamāṇā vyṛddhir hṛtā ‖29‖
(12.5.30’) pāpmādhidhīyamānā pāruṣyam avadhīyamānā ‖30‖
(12.5.31’) viṣaṃ prayasyantī takmā prayastā ‖31‖
(12.5.32’) aghaṃ pracyamānā duṣvapnyaṃ pakvā ‖32‖
(12.5.33’) mūlabarhaṇī paryākriyamāṇā kṣitiḥ paryākṛtā ‖33‖
(12.5.34’) asaṃjñā gandhena śug uddhriyamāṇāśīviṣa uddhṛtā ‖34‖
(12.5.35’) abhūtir upahriyamāṇā parābhūtir upahṛtā ‖35‖
(12.5.36’) śarvaḥ kruddhaḥ piśyamānā śimidā piśitā ‖36‖
(12.5.37’) avartir aśyamānā nirṛtir aśitā ‖37‖
(12.5.38’) aśitā lokāc chinatti brahmagavī brahmajyam asmāc cāmuṣmāc ca ‖38‖ {27}
(12.5.39’) tasyā āhananaṃ kṛtyā menir āśasanaṃ valaga ūbadhyam ‖39‖
(12.5.40’) asvagatā parihṇutā ‖40‖
(12.5.41’) agniḥ kravyād bhūtvā brahmagavī brahmajyaṃ praviśyātti ‖41‖
(12.5.42’) sarvāsyāṅgā parvā mūlāni vṛścati ‖42‖
(12.5.43’) chinatty asya pitṛbandhu parā bhāvayati mātṛbandhu ‖43‖
(12.5.44’) vivāhāṃ jñātīnt sarvān api kṣāpayati brahmagavī brahmajyasya kṣatriyeṇāpunardīyamānā ‖44‖
(12.5.45’) avāstum enam asvagam aprajasaṃ karoty aparāparaṇo bhavati kṣīyate ‖45‖
(12.5.46’) ya evaṃ viduṣo brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyo gām ādatte ‖46‖ {28}
(12.5.47’) kṣipraṃ vai tasyāhanane gṛdhrāḥ kurvata ailabam ‖47‖
(12.5.48’) kṣipraṃ vai tasyādahanaṃ pari nṛtyanti keśinīr āghnānāḥ pāṇinorasi kurvāṇāḥ pāpam ailabam ‖48‖
(12.5.49’) kṣipraṃ vai tasya vāstuṣu vṛkāḥ kurvata ailabam ‖49‖
(12.5.50’) kṣipraṃ vai tasya pṛchanti yat tad āsī3d idaṃ nu tā3d iti ‖50‖
(12.5.51’) chindhy ā chindhi pra chindhy api kṣāpaya kṣāpaya ‖51‖
(12.5.52’) ādadānam āṅgirasi brahmajyam upa dāsaya ‖52‖
(12.5.53’) vaiśvadevī hy ucyase kṛtyā kūlbajam āvṛtā ‖53‖
(12.5.54’) oṣantī samoṣantī brahmaṇo vajraḥ ‖54‖
(12.5.55’) kṣurapavir mṛtyur bhūtvā vi dhāva tvam ‖55‖
(12.5.56’) ā datse jinatāṃ varca iṣṭaṃ pūrtaṃ cāśiṣaḥ ‖56‖
(12.5.57’) ādāya jītaṃ jītāya loke 'muṣmin pra yachasi ‖57‖
(12.5.58’) aghnye padavīr bhava brāhmaṇasyābhiśastyā ‖58‖
(12.5.59’) meniḥ śaravyā bhavāghād aghaviṣā bhava ‖59‖
(12.5.60’) aghnye pra śiro jahi brahmajyasya kṛtāgaso devapīyor arādhasaḥ ‖60‖
(12.5.61’) tvayā pramūrṇaṃ mṛditam agnir dahatu duścitam ‖61‖ {29}
(12.5.61’) vṛśca pra vṛśca saṃ vṛśca daha pra daha saṃ daha ‖62‖
(12.5.63’) brahmajyaṃ devy aghnya ā mūlād anusaṃdaha ‖63‖
(12.5.64’) yathāyād yamasādanāt pāpalokān parāvataḥ ‖64‖
(12.5.65’) evā tvaṃ devy aghnye brahmajyasya kṛtāgaso devapīyor arādhasaḥ ‖65‖
(12.5.66’) vajreṇa śataparvaṇā tīkṣṇena kṣurabhṛṣṭinā ‖66‖
(12.5.67’) pra skandhān pra śiro jahi ‖67‖
(12.5.68’) lomāny asya saṃ chindhi tvacam asya vi veṣṭaya ‖68‖
(12.5.69’) māṃsāny asya śātaya snāvāny asya saṃ vṛha ‖69‖
(12.5.70’) asthīny asya pīḍaya majjānam asya nir jahi ‖70‖
(12.5.71’) sarvāsyāṅgā parvāṇi vi śrathaya ‖71‖
(12.5.72’) agnir enaṃ kravyāt pṛthivyā nudatām ud oṣatu vāyur antarikṣān mahato varimṇaḥ ‖72‖
(12.5.73’) sūrya enaṃ divaḥ pra ṇudatāṃ ny oṣatu ‖73‖ {30}

(13.1.1’) udehi vājin yo apsv antar idaṃ rāṣṭraṃ pra viśa sūnṛtāvat |
(13.1.1.c) yo rohito viśvam idaṃ jajāna sa tvā rāṣṭrāya subhṛtaṃ bibhartu ‖1‖
(13.1.2’) ud vāja ā gan yo apsv antar viśa ā roha tvadyonayo yāḥ |
(13.1.2.c) somaṃ dadhāno 'pa oṣadhīr gāś catuṣpado dvipada ā veśayeha ‖2‖
(13.1.3’) yūyam ugrā marutaḥ pṛśnimātara indreṇa yujā pra mṛṇīta śatrūn |
(13.1.3.c) ā vo rohitaḥ śṛṇavat sudānavas triṣaptāso marutaḥ svādusaṃmudaḥ ‖3‖
(13.1.4’) ruho ruroha rohita ā ruroha garbho janīnāṃ januṣām upastham |
(13.1.4.c) tābhiḥ saṃrabdham anv avindan ṣaḍ urvīr gātuṃ prapaśyann iha rāṣṭram āhāḥ ‖4‖
(13.1.5’) ā te rāṣṭram iha rohito 'hārṣīd vy āsthan mṛdho abhayaṃ te abhūt |
(13.1.5.c) tasmai te dyāvāpṛthivī revatībhiḥ kāmaṃ duhātām iha śakvarībhiḥ ‖5‖
(13.1.6’) rohito dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna tatra tantuṃ parameṣṭhī tatāna |
(13.1.6.c) tatra śiśriye 'ja ekapādo 'dṛṃhad dyāvāpṛthivī balena ‖6‖
(13.1.7’) rohito dyāvāpṛthivī adṛṃhat tena sva stabhitaṃ tena nākaḥ |
(13.1.7.c) tenāntarikṣaṃ vimitā rajāṃsi tena devā amṛtam anv avindan ‖7‖
(13.1.8’) vi rohito amṛśad viśvarūpaṃ samākurvāṇaḥ praruho ruhaś ca |
(13.1.8.c) divaṃ rūḍhvā mahatā mahimnā saṃ te rāṣṭram anaktu payasā ghṛtena ‖8‖
(13.1.9’) yās te ruhaḥ praruho yās ta āruho yābhir āpṛṇāsi divam antarikṣam |
(13.1.9.c) tāsāṃ brahmaṇā payasā vavṛdhāno viśi rāṣṭre jāgṛhi rohitasya ‖9‖
(13.1.10’) yas te viśas tapasaḥ saṃbabhūvur vatsaṃ gāyatrīm anu tā ihāguḥ |
(13.1.10.c) tās tvā viśantu manasā śivena saṃmātā vatso abhy etu rohitaḥ ‖10‖ {1}
(13.1.11’) ūrdhvo rohito adhi nāke asthād viśvā rūpāṇi janayan yuvā kaviḥ |
(13.1.11.c) tigmenāgnir jyotiṣā vi bhāti tṛtīye cakre rajasi priyāṇi ‖11‖
(13.1.12’) sahasraśṛṅgo vṛṣabho jātavedā ghṛtāhutaḥ somapṛṣṭhaḥ suvīraḥ |
(13.1.12.c) mā mā hāsīn nāthito net tvā jahāni gopoṣaṃ ca me vīrapoṣaṃ ca dhehi ‖12‖
(13.1.13’) rohito yajñasya janitā mukhaṃ ca rohitāya vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi |
(13.1.13.c) rohitaṃ devā yanti sumanasyamānāḥ sa mā rohaiḥ sāmityai rohayatu ‖13‖
(13.1.14’) rohito yajñaṃ vy adadhād viśvakarmaṇe tasmāt tejāṃsy upa memāny āguḥ |
(13.1.14.c) voceyaṃ te nābhiṃ bhuvanasyādhi majmani ‖14‖
(13.1.15’) ā tvā ruroha bṛhaty uta paṅktir ā kakub varcasā jātavedaḥ |
(13.1.15.c) ā tvā rurohoṣṇihākṣaro vaṣaṭkāra ā tvā ruroha rohito retasā saha ‖15‖
(13.1.16’) ayaṃ vaste garbhaṃ pṛthivyā divaṃ vaste 'yam antarikṣam |
(13.1.16.c) ayaṃ bradhnasya viṣṭapi svar lokān vy ānaśe ‖16‖
(13.1.17’) vācas pate pṛthivī naḥ syonā syonā yonis talpā naḥ suśevā |
(13.1.17.c) ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu taṃ tvā parameṣṭhin pary agnir āyuṣā varcasā dadhātu ‖17‖
(13.1.18’) vācas pata ṛtavaḥ pañca ye nau vaiśvakarmaṇāḥ pari ye saṃbabhūvuḥ |
(13.1.18.c) ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu taṃ tvā parameṣṭhin pari rohita āyuṣā varcasā dadhātu ‖18‖
(13.1.19’) vācas pate saumanasaṃ manaś ca goṣṭhe no gā janaya yoniṣu prajāḥ |
(13.1.19.c) ihaiva prāṇaḥ sakhye no astu taṃ tvā parameṣṭhin pary aham āyuṣā varcasā dadhātu ‖19‖
(13.1.20’) pari tvā dhāt savitā devo agnir varcasā mitrāvaruṇāv abhi tvā |
(13.1.20.c) sarvā arātīr avakrāmann ehīdaṃ rāṣṭram akaraḥ sunṛtāvat ‖20‖ {2}
(13.1.21’) yaṃ tvā pṛṣatī rathe praṣṭir vahati rohita |
(13.1.21.c) śubhā yāsi riṇann apaḥ ‖21‖
(13.1.22’) anuvratā rohiṇī rohitasya sūriḥ suvarṇā bṛhatī suvarcāḥ |
(13.1.22.c) tayā vājān viśvarūpāṃ jayema tayā viśvāḥ pṛtanā abhi ṣyāma ‖22‖
(13.1.23’) idaṃ sado rohiṇī rohitasyāsau panthāḥ pṛṣatī yena yāti |
(13.1.23.c) tāṃ gandharvāḥ kaśyapā un nayanti tāṃ rakṣanti kavayo 'pramādam ‖23‖
(13.1.24’) sūryasyāśvā harayaḥ ketumantaḥ sadā vahanty amṛtāḥ sukhaṃ ratham |
(13.1.24.c) ghṛtapāvā rohito bhrājamāno divaṃ devaḥ pṛṣatīm ā viveśa ‖24‖
(13.1.25’) yo rohito vṛṣabhas tigmaśṛṅgaḥ pary agniṃ pari sūryaṃ babhūva |

(13.1.26.c) sarvo ruroha rohito ruhaḥ ‖26‖
(13.1.27’) vi mimīṣva payasvatīṃ ghṛtācīṃ devānāṃ dhenur anapaspṛg eṣā |
(13.1.27.c) indraḥ somaṃ pibatu kṣemo astv agniḥ pra stautu vi mṛdho nudasva ‖27‖
(13.1.28’) samiddho agniḥ samidhāno ghṛtavṛddho ghṛtāhutaḥ |

(13.1.29’) hantv enān pra dahatv arir yo naḥ pṛtanyati |

(13.1.30’) avācīnān ava jahīndra vajreṇa bāhumān |
(13.1.30.c) adhā sapatnān māmakān agnes tejobhir ādiṣi ‖30‖ {3}
(13.1.31’) agne sapatnān adharān pādayāsmad vyathayā sajātam utpipānaṃ bṛhaspate |
(13.1.31.c) indrāgnī mitrāvaruṇāv adhare padyantām apratimanyūyamānāḥ ‖31‖
(13.1.32’) udyaṃs tvaṃ deva sūrya sapatnān ava me jahi |
(13.1.32.c) avainān aśmanā jahi te yantv adhamaṃ tamaḥ ‖32‖
(13.1.33’) vatso virājo vṛṣabho matīnām ā ruroha śukrapṛṣṭho 'ntarikṣam |

(13.1.36.c) tiraḥ samudram ati rocase 'rṇavam ‖36‖
(13.1.37’) rohite dyāvāpṛthivī adhi śrite vasujiti gojiti saṃdhanājiti |
(13.1.37.c) sahasraṃ yasya janimāni sapta ca voceyaṃ te nābhiṃ bhuvanasyādhi majmani ‖37‖
(13.1.38’) yaśā yāsi pradiśo diśaś ca yaśāḥ paśūnām uta carṣaṇīnām |

(13.1.39’) amutra sann iha vetthetaḥ saṃs tāni paśyasi |

(13.1.40’) devo devān marcayasy antaś carasy arṇave |

(13.1.41’) avaḥ pareṇa para enāvareṇa padā vatsaṃ bibratī gaur ud asthāt |

(13.1.42’) ekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpady aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī |

(13.1.43.c) ut tvā yajñā brahmapūtā vahanty adhvagato harayas tvā vahanti ‖43‖

(13.1.50’) satye anyaḥ samāhito 'psv anyaḥ sam idhyate |
(13.1.50.c) brahmeddhāv agnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ ‖50‖ {5}
(13.1.51’) yaṃ vātaḥ pariśumbhati yaṃ vendro brahmaṇaspatiḥ |
(13.1.51.c) brahmeddhāvagnī ījāte rohitasya svarvidaḥ ‖51‖
(13.1.52’) vediṃ bhūmiṃ kalpayitvā divaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇām |
