(13.1.53’) varṣam ājam ghraṃso agnir vedir bhūmir akalpata |
(13.1.53.c) tatraitān parvatān agnir gīrbhir ūrdhvā;m akalpayat ‖53‖
(13.1.54’) gīrbhir ūrdhvān kalpayitvā rohito bhūmim abravīt |
(13.1.54.c) tvayīdaṃ sarvaṃ jāyatāṃ yad bhūtaṃ yac ca bhāvyam ‖54‖
(13.1.55’) sa yajñaḥ prathamo bhūto bhavyo ajāyata |
(13.1.55.c) tasmād dha jajña idaṃ sarvaṃ yat kiṃ cedaṃ virocate rohitena ṛṣiṇābhṛtam ‖55‖
(13.1.56’) yaś ca gāṃ padā sphurati pratyaṅ sūryaṃ ca mehati |
(13.1.56.c) tasya vṛścāmi te mūlaṃ na chāyāṃ karavo 'param ‖56‖
(13.1.57’) yo mābhichāyam atyeṣi māṃ cāgniṃ cāntarā |
(13.1.57.c) tasya vṛścāmi te mūlaṃ na chāyāṃ karavo 'param ‖57‖
(13.1.58’) yo adya deva sūrya tvāṃ ca māṃ cāntarāyati |
(13.1.58.c) duṣvapnyaṃ tasmiṃ chamalaṃ duritāni ca mṛjmahe ‖58‖
(13.1.59’) mā pra gāma patho vayaṃ mā yajñād indra sominaḥ |
(13.1.59.c) mānta sthur no arātayaḥ ‖59‖
(13.1.60’) yo yajñasya prasādhanas tantur deveṣv ātataḥ |
(13.1.60.c) tam āhutam aśīmahi ‖60‖ {6}

(13.2.1’) ud asya ketavo divi śukrā bhrājanta īrate |
(13.2.1.c) ādityasya nṛcakṣaso mahivratasya mīḍhuṣaḥ ‖1‖
(13.2.2’) diśāṃ prajñānāṃ svarayantam arciṣā supakṣam āśuṃ patayantam arṇave |
(13.2.2.c) stavāma sūryaṃ bhuvanasya gopāṃ yo raśmibhir diśa ābhāti sarvāḥ ‖2‖
(13.2.3’) yat prāṅ pratyaṅ svadhayā yāsi śībhaṃ nānārūpe ahanī karṣi māyayā |
(13.2.3.c) tad āditya mahi tat te mahi śravo yad eko viśvaṃ pari bhūma jāyase ‖3‖
(13.2.4’) vipaścitaṃ taraṇiṃ bhrājamānaṃ vahanti yaṃ haritaḥ sapta bahvīḥ |
(13.2.4.c) srutād yam attrir divam unnināya taṃ tvā paśyanti pariyāntam ājim ‖4‖
(13.2.5’) mā tvā dabhan pariyāntam ājiṃ svasti durgā;m ati yāhi śībham |
(13.2.5.c) divaṃ ca sūrya pṛthivīṃ ca devīm ahorātre vimimāno yad eṣi ‖5‖
(13.2.6’) svasti te sūrya carase rathāya yenobhāv antau pariyāsi sadyaḥ |
(13.2.6.c) yaṃ te vahanti harito vahiṣṭhāḥ śatam aśvā yadi vā sapta bahvīḥ ‖6‖
(13.2.7’) sukhaṃ sūrya ratham aṃśumantaṃ syonaṃ suvahnim adhi tiṣṭha vājinam |
(13.2.7.c) yaṃ te vahanti harito vahiṣṭhāḥ śatam aśvā yadi vā sapta bahvīḥ ‖7‖
(13.2.8’) sapta sūryo harito yātave rathe hiraṇyatvacaso bṛhatīr ayukta |
(13.2.8.c) amoci śukro rajasaḥ parastād vidhūya devas tamo divam āruhat ‖8‖
(13.2.9’) ut ketunā bṛhatā deva āgann apāvṛk tamo 'bhi jyotir aśrait |
(13.2.9.c) divyaḥ suparṇaḥ sa vīro vy akhyad aditeḥ putro bhuvanāni viśvā ‖9‖
(13.2.10’) udyan raśmīn ā tanuṣe viśvā rupāṇi puṣyasi |
(13.2.10.c) ubhā samudrau kratunā vi bhāsi sarvāṃl lokān paribhūr bhrājamānaḥ ‖10‖ {7}
(13.2.11’) pūrvāparaṃ carato māyayaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto 'rṇavam |
(13.2.11.c) viśvānyo bhuvanā vicaṣṭe hairaṇyair anyaṃ harito vahanti ‖11‖
(13.2.12’) divi tvāttrir adhārayat sūryā māsāya kartave |
(13.2.12.c) sa eṣi sudhṛtas tapan viśvā bhūtāvacākaśat ‖12‖
(13.2.13’) ubhāv antau sam arṣasi vatsaḥ saṃmātarāv iva |
(13.2.13.c) nanv etad itaḥ purā brahma devā amī viduḥ ‖13‖
(13.2.14’) yat samudram anu śritaṃ tat siṣāsati sūryaḥ |
(13.2.14.c) adhvāsya vitato mahān pūrvaś cāparaś ca yaḥ ‖14‖
(13.2.15’) taṃ sam āpnoti jūtibhis tato nāpa cikitsati |
(13.2.15.c) tenāmṛtasya bhakṣaṃ devānāṃ nāva rundhate ‖15‖
(13.2.16’) ud u tyaṃ jātavedasaṃ devaṃ vahanti ketavaḥ |
(13.2.16.c) dṛśe viśvāya sūryam ‖16‖
(13.2.17’) apa tye tāyavo yathā nakṣatrā yanty aktubhiḥ |
(13.2.17.c) sūrāya viśvacakṣase ‖17‖
(13.2.18’) adṛśrann asya ketavo vi raśmayo janā;m anu |
(13.2.18.c) bhrājanto agnayo yathā ‖18‖
(13.2.19’) taraṇir viśvadarśato jyotiṣkṛd asi sūrya |
(13.2.19.c) viśvam ā bhāsi rocana ‖19‖
(13.2.20’) pratyaṅ devānāṃ viśaḥ pratyaṅṅ ud eṣi mānuṣīḥ |
(13.2.20.c) pratyaṅ viśvaṃ svar dṛśe ‖20‖ {8}
(13.2.21’) yenā pāvaka cakṣasā bhuraṇyantaṃ janā;m anu |
(13.2.21.c) tvaṃ varuṇa paśyasi ‖21‖
(13.2.22’) vi dyām eṣi rajas pṛthv ahar mimāno aktubhiḥ |
(13.2.22.c) paśyan janmāni sūrya ‖22‖
(13.2.23’) sapta tvā harito rathe vahanti deva sūrya |
(13.2.23.c) śociṣkeśaṃ vicakṣaṇam ‖23‖
(13.2.24’) ayukta sapta śundhyuvaḥ sūro rathasya naptyaḥ |
(13.2.24.c) tābhir yāti svayuktibhiḥ ‖24‖
(13.2.25’) rohito divam āruhat tapasā tapasvī |
(13.2.25.c) sa yonim aiti sa u jāyate punaḥ sa devānām adhipatir babhūva ‖25‖
(13.2.26’) yo viśvacarṣaṇir uta viśvatomukho yo viśvataspāṇir uta viśvataspṛthaḥ |
(13.2.26.c) saṃ bāhubhyāṃ bharati saṃ patatrair dyāvāpṛthivī janayan deva ekaḥ ‖26‖
(13.2.27’) ekapād dvipado bhūyo vi cakrame dvipāt tripādam abhy eti paścāt |
(13.2.27.c) dvipād dha ṣaṭpado bhūyo vi cakrame ta ekapadas tanvaṃ sam āsate ‖27‖
(13.2.28’) atandro yāsyan harito yad āsthād dve rūpe kṛṇute rocamānaḥ |
(13.2.28.c) ketumān udyant sahamāno rajāṃsi viśvā āditya pravato vi bhāsi ‖28‖
(13.2.29’) baṇ mahā;m asi sūrya baḍ āditya mahā;m asi |
(13.2.29.c) mahāṃs te mahato mahimā tvam āditya mahā;m asi ‖29‖
(13.2.30’) rocase divi rocase antarikṣe pataṅga pṛthivyāṃ rocase rocase apsv antaḥ |
(13.2.30.c) ubhā samudrau rucyā vy āpitha devo devāsi mahiṣaḥ svarjit ‖30‖ {9}
(13.2.31’) arvāṅ parastāt prayato vyadhva āśur vipaścit patayan pataṅgaḥ |
(13.2.31.c) viṣṇur vicittaḥ śavasādhitiṣṭhan pra ketunā sahate viśvam ejat ‖31‖
(13.2.32’) citrāś cikitvān mahiṣaḥ suparṇa ārocayan rodasī antarikṣam |
(13.2.32.c) ahorātre pari sūryaṃ vasāne prāsya viśvā tirato vīryāṇi ‖32‖
(13.2.33’) tigmo vibhrājan tanvaṃ śiśāno 'raṃgamāsaḥ pravato rarāṇaḥ |
(13.2.33.c) jyotiṣmān pakṣī mahiṣo vayodhā viśvā āsthāt pradiśaḥ kalpamānaḥ ‖33‖
(13.2.34’) citraṃ devānām ketur anīkaṃ jyotiṣmān pradiśaḥ sūrya udyan |
(13.2.34.c) divākaro 'ti dyumnais tamāṃsi viśvātārīd duritāni śukraḥ ‖34‖
(13.2.35’) citraṃ devānām ud agād anīkaṃ cakṣur mitrasya varuṇasyāgneḥ |
(13.2.35.c) āprād dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣaṃ sūrya ātmā jagatas tasthuṣaś ca ‖35‖
(13.2.36’) uccā patantam aruṇaṃ suparṇaṃ madhye divas taraṇiṃ bhrājamānam |
(13.2.36.c) paśyama tvā savitāraṃ yam āhur ajasraṃ jyotir yad avindad attriḥ ‖36‖
(13.2.37’) divas pṛṣṭhe dhāvamānaṃ suparṇam adityāḥ putraṃ nāthakāma upa yāmi bhītaḥ |
(13.2.37.c) sa naḥ sūrya pra tira dīrgham āyur mā riṣāma sumatau te syāma ‖37‖
(13.2.38’) sahasrāhṇyaṃ viyatāv asya pakṣau harer haṃsasya patataḥ svargam |
(13.2.38.c) sa devānt sarvān urasy upadadya saṃpaśyan yāti bhuvanāni viśvā ‖38‖
(13.2.39’) rohitaḥ kālo abhavad rohito 'gre prajāpatiḥ |
(13.2.39.c) rohito yajñānāṃ mukhaṃ rohitaḥ svar ābharat ‖39‖
(13.2.40’) rohito loko abhavad rohito 'ty atapad divam |
(13.2.40.c) rohito raśmibhir bhūmiṃ samudram anu saṃ carat ‖40‖ {10}
(13.2.41’) sarvā diśaḥ sam acarad rohito 'dhipatir divaḥ |
(13.2.41.c) divaṃ samudram ād bhūmiṃ sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ vi rakṣati ‖41‖
(13.2.42’) ārohan chukro bṛhatīr atandro dve rūpe kṛṇute rocamānaḥ |
(13.2.42.c) citraś cikitvān mahiṣo vātamāyā yāvato lokān abhi yad vibhāti ‖42‖
(13.2.43’) abhy anyad eti pary anyad asyate 'horātrābhyāṃ mahiṣaḥ kalpamānaḥ |
(13.2.43.c) sūryaṃ vayaṃ rajasi kṣiyantaṃ gātuvidaṃ havāmahe nādhamānāḥ ‖43‖
(13.2.44’) pṛthivīpro mahiṣo nādhamānasya gātur adabdhacakṣuḥ pari viśvaṃ babhūva |
(13.2.44.c) viśvaṃ saṃpaśyant suvidatro yajatra idaṃ śṛṇotu yad ahaṃ bravīmi ‖44‖
(13.2.45’) pary asya mahimā pṛthivīṃ samudraṃ jyotiṣā vibhrājan pari dyām antarikṣam |
(13.2.45.c) sarvaṃ saṃpaśyant suvidatro yajatra idaṃ śṛṇotu yad ahaṃ bravīmi ‖45‖
(13.2.46’) abodhy agniḥ samidhā janānāṃ prati dhenum ivāyatīm uṣasam |
(13.2.46.c) yahvā iva pra vayām ujjihānāḥ pra bhānavaḥ sisrate nākam acha ‖46‖ {11}

(13.3.1’) ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī jajāna yo drāpim kṛtvā bhuvanāni vaste |
(13.3.1.c) yasmin kṣiyanti pradiśaḥ ṣaḍ urvīr yāḥ pataṅgo anu vicākaśīti |
(13.3.1.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.2’) yasmād vātā ṛtuthā pavante yasmāt samudrā adhi vikṣaranti |
(13.3.2.c) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.2.e) ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ‖2‖
(13.3.3’) yo mārayati prāṇayati yasmāt prāṇanti bhuvanāni viśvā |
(13.3.3.c) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.3.e) ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ‖3‖
(13.3.4’) yaḥ prāṇena dyāvāpṛthivī tarpayaty apānena samudrasya jaṭharaṃ yaḥ piparti |
(13.3.4.c) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.4.e) ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ‖4‖
(13.3.5’) yasmin virāṭ parameṣṭhī prajāpatir agnir vaiśvānaraḥ saha paṅktyā śritaḥ |
(13.3.5.c) yaḥ parasya prāṇaṃ paramasya teja ādade |
(13.3.5.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.6’) yasmin ṣaḍ urvīḥ pañca diśo adhiśritāś catasra āpo yajñasya trayo 'kṣarāḥ |
(13.3.6.c) yo antarā rodasī kruddhaś cakṣuṣaikṣata |
(13.3.6.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇam jināti |
(13.3.7’) yo annādo annapatir babhūva brahmaṇas patir uta yaḥ |
(13.3.7.c) bhūto bhaviṣyat bhuvanasya yas patiḥ |
(13.3.7.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.8’) ahorātrair vimitaṃ triṃśadaṅgaṃ trayodaśaṃ māsaṃ yo nirmimīte |
(13.3.8.c) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.8.e) ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ‖8‖
(13.3.9’) kṛsṇaṃ niyānaṃ harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divam ut patanti |
(13.3.9.c) ta āvavṛtrant sadanād ṛtasya |
(13.3.9.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.10’) yat te candraṃ kaśyapa rocanāvad yat saṃhitaṃ puṣkalaṃ citrabhānu yasmint sūryā ārpitāḥ sapta sākam |
(13.3.10.c) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.10.e) ud vepaya rohita pra kṣiṇīhi brahmajyasya prati muñca pāśān ‖10‖ {12}
(13.3.11’) bṛhad enam anu vaste purastād rathaṃtaraṃ prati gṛhṇāti paścāt |
(13.3.11.c) jyotir vasāne sadam apramādam |
(13.3.11.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.12’) bṛhad anyataḥ pakṣa āsīd rathaṃtaram anyataḥ sabale sadhrīcī |
(13.3.12.c) yad rohitam ajanayanta devāḥ |
(13.3.12.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.13’) sa varuṇaḥ sāyam agnir bhavati sa mitro bhavati prātar udyan |
(13.3.13.c) sa savitā bhūtvāntarikṣeṇa yāti sa indro bhūtvā tapati madhyato divam |
(13.3.13.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13.3.14.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13.3.16.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13.3.17.c) yad ekaṃ jyotir bahudhā vibhāti |

(13.3.18’) sapta yuñjanti ratham ekacakram eko aśvo vahati saptanāmā |

(13.3.18.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |
(13.3.19’) aṣṭadhā yukto vahati vahnir ugraḥ pitā devānāṃ janitā matīnām |

(13.3.21’) nimrucas tisro vyuṣo ha tisras trīṇi rajāṃsi divo aṅga tisrah |
(13.3.21.c) vidmā te agne tredhā janitraṃ tredhā devānāṃ janimāni vidma |
(13.3.21.e) tasya devasya kruddhasyaitad āgo ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ jināti |

(13.3.26.c) sa ha dyām adhi rohati ruho ruroha rohitaḥ ‖26‖ {14}

(13.4.26’) sa eti savitā svar divas pṛṣṭhe 'vacākaśat ‖1‖
(13.4.2’) raśmibhir nabha ābhṛtaṃ mahendra ety āvṛtaḥ ‖2‖
(13.4.3’) sa dhātā sa vidhartā sa vāyur nabha ucchritam ‖3‖
(13.4.4’) so 'ryamā sa varuṇaḥ sa rudraḥ sa mahādevaḥ ‖4‖
(13.4.5’) so agniḥ sa u sūryaḥ sa u eva mahāyamaḥ ‖5‖
(13.4.6’) taṃ vatsā upa tiṣṭhanty ekaśīrṣāṇo yutā daśa ‖6‖
(13.4.7’) paścāt prāñca ā tanvanti yad udeti vi bhāsati ‖7‖
(13.4.8’) tasyaiṣa māruto gaṇaḥ sa eti śikyākṛtaḥ ‖8‖
(13.4.9’) raśmibhir nabha ābhṛtaṃ mahendra ety āvṛtaḥ ‖9‖
(13.4.10’) tasyeme nava kośā viṣṭambhā navadhā hitāḥ ‖10‖
(13.4.11’) sa prajābhyo vi paśyati yac ca prāṇati yac ca na ‖11‖
(13.4.12’) tam idaṃ nigataṃ sahaḥ sa eṣa eka ekavṛd eka eva ‖12‖
(13.4.13’) ete asmin devā ekavṛto bhavanti ‖13‖ {15}
(13.4.13’) kīrtiś ca yaśaś cāmbhaś ca nabhaś ca brāhmaṇavarcasaṃ cānnaṃ cānnādyaṃ ca ‖14‖
(13.4.15’) ya etaṃ devam ekavṛtaṃ veda ‖15‖
(13.4.16’) na dvitīyo na tṛtīyaś caturtho nāpy ucyate ‖16‖
(13.4.17’) na pañcamo na ṣaṣṭhaḥ saptamo nāpy ucyate ‖17‖
(13.4.18’) nāṣṭamo na navamo daśamo nāpy ucyate ‖18‖
(13.4.19’) sa sarvasmai vi paśyati yac ca prāṇati yac ca na ‖19‖
(13.4.20’) tam idaṃ nigataṃ sahaḥ sa eṣa eka ekavṛd eka eva ‖20‖
(13.4.21’) sarve asmin devā ekavṛto bhavanti ‖21‖ {16}
(13.4.21’) brahma ca tapaś ca kīrtiś ca yaśaś cāmbhaś ca nabhaś ca brāhmaṇavarcasaṃ cānnaṃ cānnādyaṃ ca ‖2‖
(13.4.23’) bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca śraddhā ca ruciś ca svargaś ca svadhā ca ‖23‖
(13.4.24’) ya etaṃ devam ekavṛtaṃ veda ‖24‖
(13.4.25’) sa eva mṛtyuḥ so 'mṛtaṃ so 'bhvaṃ sa rakṣaḥ ‖25‖
(13.4.26’) sa rudro vasuvanir vasudeye namovāke vaṣaṭkāro 'nu saṃhitaḥ ‖26‖
(13.4.27’) tasyeme sarve yātava upa praśiṣam āsate ‖27‖
(13.4.28’) tasyāmū sarvā nakṣatrā vaśe candramasā saha ‖28‖ {17}
(13.4.28’) sa vā ahno 'jāyata tasmād ahar ajāyata ‖29‖
(13.4.30’) sa vai rātryā ajāyata tasmād rātrir ajāyata ‖30‖
(13.4.31’) sa vā antarikṣād ajāyata tasmād antarikṣam ajāyata ‖31‖
(13.4.32’) sa vai vāyor ajāyata tasmād vāyur ajāyata ‖32‖
(13.4.33’) sa vai divo 'jāyata tasmād dyaur adhi ajāyata ‖33‖
(13.4.34’) sa vai digbhyo 'jāyata tasmād diśo 'jāyanta ‖34‖
(13.4.35’) sa vai bhūmer ajāyata tasmād bhūmir ajāyata ‖35‖
(13.4.36’) sa vā agner ajāyata tasmād agnir ajāyata ‖36‖
(13.4.37’) sa vā adbhyo 'jāyata tasmād āpo 'jāyanta ‖37‖
(13.4.38’) sa vā ṛgbhyo 'jāyata tasmād ṛco 'jāyanta ‖38‖
(13.4.39’) sa vai yajñād ajāyata tasmād yajño 'jāyata ‖39‖
(13.4.40’) sa yajñas tasya yajñaḥ sa yajñasya śiras kṛtam ‖40‖
(13.4.41’) sa stanayati sa vi dyotate sa u aśmānam asyati ‖41‖
(13.4.42’) pāpāya vā bhadrāya vā puruṣāyāsurāya vā ‖42‖
(13.4.43’) yad vā kṛṇoṣy oṣadhīr yad vā varṣasi bhadrayā yad vā janyam avīvṛdhaḥ ‖43‖
(13.4.44’) tāvāṃs te maghavan mahimopo te tanvaḥ śatam ‖44‖
(13.4.45’) upo te badhve baddhāni yadi vāsi nyarbudam ‖45‖ {18}
(13.4.45’) bhūyān indro namurād bhūyān indrāsi mṛtyubhyaḥ ‖46‖
(13.4.47’) bhūyān arātyāḥ śacyāḥ patis tvam indrāsi vibhūḥ prabhūr iti tvopāsmahe vayam ‖47‖
(13.4.48’) namas te astu paśyata paśya mā paśyata ‖48‖
(13.4.49’) annādyena yaśasā tejasā brāhmaṇavarcasena ‖49‖
(13.4.50’) ambho amo mahaḥ saha iti tvopāsmahe vayam ‖50‖
(13.4.51’) ambho aruṇaṃ rajataṃ rajaḥ saha iti tvopāsmahe vayam ‖51‖ {19}
(13.4.51’) uruḥ pṛthuḥ subhūr bhuva iti tvopāsmahe vayam ‖52‖
(13.4.53’) pratho varo vyaco loka iti tvopāsmahe vayam ‖53‖
(13.4.54’) bhavadvasur idadvasuḥ saṃyadvasur āyadvasur iti tvopāsmahe vayam ‖54‖
(13.4.55’) namas te astu paśyata paśya mā paśyata ‖55‖
(13.4.56’) annādyena yaśasā tejasā brāhmaṇavarcasena ‖56‖ {20}

(14.1.1’) satyenottabhitā bhūmiḥ sūryeṇottabhitā dyauḥ |
(14.1.1.c) ṛtenādityās tiṣṭhanti divi somo adhi śritaḥ ‖1‖
(14.1.2’) somenādityā balinaḥ somena pṛthivī mahī |
(14.1.2.c) atho nakṣatrāṇām eṣām upasthe soma āhitaḥ ‖2‖
(14.1.3’) somaṃ manyate papivān yat saṃpiṃṣanty oṣadhim |
(14.1.3.c) somaṃ yaṃ brahmāṇo vidur na tasyāśnāti pārthivaḥ ‖3‖
(14.1.4’) yat tvā soma prapibanti tata ā pyāyase punaḥ |
(14.1.4.c) vāyuḥ somasya rakṣitā samānāṃ māsa ākṛtiḥ ‖4‖
(14.1.5’) āchadvidhānair gupito bārhataiḥ soma rakṣitaḥ |
(14.1.5.c) grāvṇām ic chṛṇvan tiṣṭhasi na te aśnāti pārthivaḥ ‖5‖
(14.1.6’) cittir ā upabarhaṇaṃ cakṣur ā abhyañjanam |
(14.1.6.c) dyaur bhūmiḥ kośa āsīd yad ayāt sūryā patim ‖6‖
(14.1.7’) raibhy āsīd anudeyī nārāśaṃsī nyocanī |
(14.1.7.c) suryāyā bhadram id vāso gāthayati pariṣkṛtā ‖7‖
(14.1.8’) stomā āsan pratidhayaḥ kurīraṃ chanda opaśaḥ |
(14.1.8.c) sūryāyā aśvinā varāgnir āsīt purogavaḥ ‖8‖
(14.1.9’) somo vadhūyur abhavad aśvināstām ubhā varā |
(14.1.9.c) sūryāṃ yat patye śaṃsantīṃ manasā savitādadāt ‖9‖
(14.1.10’) mano asyā ana āsīd dyaur āsīd uta chadiḥ |
(14.1.10.c) śukrāv anaḍvāhāv āstāṃ yad ayāt sūryā patim ‖10‖ {1}
(14.1.11’) ṛksāmābhyām abhihitau gāvau te sāmanāv aitām |
(14.1.11.c) śrotre te cakre āstāṃ divi panthāś carācaraḥ ‖11‖
(14.1.12’) śucī te cakre yātyā vyāno akṣa āhataḥ |
(14.1.12.c) ano manasmayaṃ sūryārohat prayatī patim ‖12‖
(14.1.13’) sūryāyā vahatuḥ prāgāt savitā yam avāsṛjat |
(14.1.13.c) maghāsu hanyante gāvaḥ phalgunīṣu vy uhyate ‖13‖
(14.1.14’) yad aśvinā pṛchamānāv ayātaṃ tricakreṇa vahatuṃ sūryāyāḥ |
(14.1.14.c) kvaikaṃ cakraṃ vām āsīt kva deṣṭrāya tasthathuḥ ‖14‖
(14.1.15’) yad ayātaṃ śubhas patī vareyaṃ sūryām upa |
(14.1.15.c) viśve devā anu tad vām ajānan putraḥ pitaram avṛṇīta pūṣā ‖15‖
(14.1.16’) dve te cakre sūrye brahmāṇa ṛtuthā viduḥ |
(14.1.16.c) athaikaṃ cakraṃ yad guhā tad addhātaya id viduḥ ‖16‖
(14.1.17’) aryamaṇaṃ yajāmahe subandhuṃ pativedanam |
(14.1.17.c) urvārukam iva bandhanāt preto muñcāmi nāmutaḥ ‖17‖
(14.1.18’) preto muñcāmi nāmutaḥ subaddhām amutas karam |
(14.1.18.c) yatheyam indra mīḍhvaḥ suputrā subhagāsati ‖18‖
(14.1.19’) pra tvā muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśād yena tvābadhnāt savitā suśevāḥ |
(14.1.19.c) ṛtasya yonau sukṛtasya loke syonaṃ te astu sahasaṃbhalāyai ‖19‖
(14.1.20’) bhagas tveto nayatu hastagṛhyāśvinā tvā pra vahatāṃ rathena |
(14.1.20.c) gṛhān gacha gṛhapatnī yathāso vaśinī tvaṃ vidatham ā vadāsi ‖20‖ {2}
(14.1.21’) iha priyaṃ prajāyai te sam ṛdhyatām asmin gṛhe gārhapatyāya jāgṛhi |
(14.1.21.c) enā patyā tanvaṃ saṃ spṛśasvātha jirvir vidatham ā vadāsi ‖21‖
(14.1.22’) ihaiva staṃ mā vi yauṣṭaṃ viśvam āyur vy aśnutam |
(14.1.22.c) krīḍantau putrair naptṛbhir modamānau svastakau ‖22‖
(14.1.23’) pūrvāparaṃ carato māyaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto 'rṇavam |
(14.1.23.c) viśvānyo bhuvanā vicaṣṭa ṛtūṃr anyo vidadhaj jāyase navaḥ ‖23‖
(14.1.24’) navonavo bhavasi jāyamāno 'hnāṃ ketur uṣasām eṣy agram |
(14.1.24.c) bhāgaṃ devebhyo vi dadhāsy āyan pra candramas tirase dīrgham āyuḥ ‖24‖
(14.1.25’) parā dehi śāmulyaṃ brahmabhyo vi bhajā vasu |
(14.1.25.c) kṛtyaiṣā padvatī bhūtvā jāyā viśate patim ‖25‖
(14.1.26’) nīlalohitaṃ bhavati kṛtyāsaktir vy ajyate |
(14.1.26.c) edhante asyā jñātayaḥ patir bandheṣu badhyate ‖26‖
(14.1.27’) aślīlā tanūr bhavati ruśatī pāpayāmuyā |
(14.1.27.c) patir yad vadhvo vāsasaḥ svam aṅgam abhyūrṇute ‖27‖
(14.1.28’) āśasanaṃ viśasanam atho adhivikartanam |
(14.1.28.c) sūryāyāḥ paśya rūpāṇi tāni brahmota śumbhati ‖28‖
(14.1.29’) tṛṣṭam etat kaṭukam apāṣṭhavad viṣavan naitad attave |
(14.1.29.c) sūryāṃ yo brahmā veda sa id vādhūyam arhati ‖29‖
(14.1.30’) sa it tat syonaṃ harati brahmā vāsaḥ sumaṅgalam |
(14.1.30.c) prāyaścittiṃ yo adhyeti yena jāyā na riṣyati ‖30‖ {3}
(14.1.31’) yuvaṃ bhagaṃ saṃ bharataṃ samṛddham ṛtaṃ vadantāv ṛtodyeṣu |
(14.1.31.c) brahmaṇas pate patim asyai rocaya cāru saṃbhalo vadatu vācam etām ‖31‖
(14.1.32’) ihed asātha na paro gamāthemaṃ gāvaḥ prajayā vardhayātha |
(14.1.32.c) śubhaṃ yatīr usriyāḥ somavarcaso viśve devāḥ krann iha vo manāṃsi ‖32‖
(14.1.33’) imaṃ gāvaḥ prajayā saṃ viśāthāyaṃ devānāṃ na mināti bhāgam |
(14.1.33.c) asmai vaḥ pūṣā marutaś ca sarve asmai vo dhātā savitā suvāti ‖33‖
(14.1.34’) anṛkṣarā ṛjavaḥ santu panthano yebhiḥ sakhāyo yanti no vareyam |
(14.1.34.c) saṃ bhagena sam aryamṇā saṃ dhātā sṛjatu varcasā ‖34‖
(14.1.35’) yac ca varco akṣeṣu surāyāṃ ca yad āhitam |
(14.1.35.c) yad goṣv aśvinā varcas tenemāṃ varcasāvatam ‖35‖
(14.1.36’) yena mahānaghnyā jaghanam aśvinā yena vā surā |
(14.1.36.c) yenākṣā abhyaṣicyanta tenemāṃ varcasāvatam ‖36‖
(14.1.37’) yo anidhmo dīdayad apsv antar yaṃ viprāsa īḍate adhvareṣu |
(14.1.37.c) apāṃ napān madhumatīr apo dā yābhir indro vāvṛdhe vīryāvān ‖37‖
(14.1.38’) idam ahaṃ ruśantaṃ grābhaṃ tanūdūṣim apohāmi |
(14.1.38.c) yo bhadro rocanas tam ud acāmi ‖38‖
(14.1.39’) āsyai brāhmaṇāḥ snapanīr harantv avīraghnīr ud ajantv āpaḥ |
(14.1.39.c) aryamṇo agniṃ pary etu pūṣan pratīkṣante śvaśuro devaraś ca ‖39‖
(14.1.40’) śaṃ te hiraṇyaṃ śam u santv āpaḥ śaṃ methir bhavatu śaṃ yugasya tardma |
(14.1.40.c) śaṃ ta āpaḥ śatapavitrā bhavantu śam u patyā tanvaṃ saṃ spṛśasva ‖40‖ {4}
(14.1.41’) khe rathasya khe 'nasaḥ khe yugasya śatakrato |
(14.1.41.c) apālām indra triṣ pūtvākṛṇoḥ sūryatvacam ‖41‖
(14.1.42’) āśāsānā saumanasaṃ prajāṃ saubhāgyaṃ rayim |
(14.1.42.c) patyur anuvratā bhūtvā saṃ nahyasvāmṛtāya kam ‖42‖
(14.1.43’) yathā sindhur nadīnāṃ sāmrājyaṃ suṣuve vṛṣā |
(14.1.43.c) evā tvaṃ samrājñy edhi patyur astaṃ paretya ‖43‖
(14.1.44’) samrājñy edhi śvaśureṣu samrājñy uta devṛṣu |
(14.1.44.c) nanānduḥ samrājñy edhi samrājñy uta śvaśrvāḥ ‖44‖
(14.1.45’) yā akṛntann avayan yāś ca tatnire yā devīr antā;m abhito 'dadanta |
(14.1.45.c) tās tvā jarase saṃ vyayantv āyuṣmatīdaṃ pari dhatsva vāsaḥ ‖45‖
(14.1.46’) jīvaṃ rudanti vi nayanty adhvaraṃ dīrghām anu prasitiṃ dīdhyur naraḥ |
(14.1.46.c) vāmaṃ pitṛbhyo ya idaṃ samīrire mayaḥ patibhyo janaye pariṣvaje ‖46‖
(14.1.47’) syonaṃ dhruvaṃ prajāyai dhārayāmi te 'śmānaṃ devyāḥ pṛthivyā upasthe |
(14.1.47.c) tam ā tiṣṭhānumādyā suvarcā dīrghaṃ ta āyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu ‖47‖
(14.1.48’) yenāgnir asyā bhūmyā hastaṃ jagrāha dakṣiṇam |
(14.1.48.c) tena gṛhṇāmi te hastaṃ mā vyathiṣṭhā mayā saha prajayā ca dhanena ca ‖48‖
(14.1.49’) devas te savitā hastaṃ gṛhṇātu somo rājā suprajasaṃ kṛṇotu |
(14.1.49.c) agniḥ subhagāṃ jatavedāḥ patye patnīṃ jaradaṣṭim kṛṇotu ‖49‖
(14.1.50’) gṛhṇāmi te saubhagatvāya hastaṃ mayā patyā jaradaṣṭir yathāsaḥ |
(14.1.50.c) bhago aryamā savitā puraṃdhir mahyaṃ tvādur gārhapatyāya devāḥ ‖50‖ {5}
(14.1.51’) bhagas te hastam agrahīt savitā hastam agrahīt |
(14.1.51.c) patnī tvam asi dharmaṇāhaṃ gṛhapatis tava ‖51‖
(14.1.52’) mameyam astu poṣyā mahyaṃ tvādād bṛhaspatiḥ |
(14.1.52.c) mayā patyā prajāvati saṃ jīva śaradaḥ śatam ‖52‖
(14.1.53’) tvaṣṭā vāso vy adadhāc chubhe kaṃ bṛhaspateḥ praśiṣā kavīnām |
(14.1.53.c) tenemāṃ nārīṃ savitā bhagaś ca sūryām iva pari dhattāṃ prajayā ‖53‖
(14.1.54’) indrāgnī dyāvāpṛthivī mātariśvā mitrāvaruṇā bhago aśvinobhā |
(14.1.54.c) bṛhaspatir maruto brahma soma imāṃ nāriṃ prajayā vardhayantu ‖54‖
(14.1.55’) bṛhaspatiḥ prathamaḥ sūryāyāḥ śīrṣe keśā;m akalpayat |
(14.1.55.c) tenemām aśvinā nārīṃ patye saṃ śobhayāmasi ‖55‖
(14.1.56’) idaṃ tad rūpaṃ yad avasta yoṣā jāyāṃ jijñāse manasā carantīm |
(14.1.56.c) tām anv artiṣye sakhibhir navagvaiḥ ka imān vidvān vi cacarta pāśān ‖56‖
(14.1.57’) ahaṃ vi ṣyāmi mayi rūpam asyā vedad it paśyan manasaḥ kulāyam |
(14.1.57.c) na steyam admi manasod amucye svayaṃ śrathnāno varuṇasya pāśān ‖57‖
(14.1.58’) pra tvā muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśād yena tvābadhnāt savitā suśevāḥ |
(14.1.58.c) uruṃ lokaṃ sugam atra panthāṃ kṛṇomi tubhyaṃ sahapatnyai vadhu ‖58‖
(14.1.59’) ud yachadhvam apa rakṣo hanāthemaṃ nārīṃ sukṛte dadhāta |
(14.1.59.c) dhātā vipaścit patim asyai viveda bhago rājā pura etu prajānan ‖59‖
(14.1.60’) bhagas tatakṣa caturaḥ pādān bhagas tatakṣa catvāry uṣpalāni |
(14.1.60.c) tvaṣṭā pipeśa madhyato 'nu vardhrānt sā no astu sumaṅgalī ‖60‖
(14.1.61’) sukiṃśukaṃ vahatuṃ viśvarūpaṃ hiraṇyavarṇaṃ suvṛtaṃ sucakram |
(14.1.61.c) ā roha sūrye amṛtasya lokaṃ syonaṃ patibhyo vahatuṃ kṛṇu tvam ‖61‖
(14.1.62’) abhrātṛghnīṃ varuṇāpaśughnīṃ bṛhaspate |
(14.1.62.c) indrāpatighnīm putriṇīm āsmabhyaṃ savitar vaha ‖62‖
(14.1.63’) mā hiṃsiṣṭaṃ kumāryaṃ sthūṇe devakṛte pathi |
(14.1.63.c) śālāyā devyā dvāraṃ syonaṃ kṛṇmo vadhūpatham ‖63‖
(14.1.64’) brahmāparaṃ yujyatāṃ brahma pūrvaṃ brahmāntato madhyato brahma sarvataḥ |
(14.1.64.c) anāvyādhāṃ devapurāṃ prapadya śivā syonā patiloke vi rāja ‖64‖ {6}

(14.2.1’) tubhyam agre pary avahant sūryāṃ vahatunā saha |
(14.2.1.c) sa naḥ patibhyo jāyāṃ dā agne prajayā saha ‖1‖
(14.2.2’) punaḥ patnīm agnir adād āyuṣā saha varcasā |
(14.2.2.c) dīrghāyur asyā yaḥ patir jīvāti śaradaḥ śatam ‖2‖
(14.2.3’) somasya jāyā prathamaṃ gandharvas te 'paraḥ patiḥ |
(14.2.3.c) tṛtīyo agniṣ ṭe patis turīyas te manuṣyajāḥ ‖3‖
(14.2.4’) somo dadad gandharvāya gandharvo dadad agnaye |
(14.2.4.c) rayiṃ ca putrāṃs cādād agnir mahyam atho imām ‖4‖
(14.2.5’) ā vām agant sumatir vājinīvasū ny aśvinā hṛtsu kāmā araṃsata |
(14.2.5.c) abhūtaṃ gopā mithunā śubhas patī priyā aryamṇo duryā;m aśīmahi ‖5‖
(14.2.6’) sā mandasānā manasā śivena rayiṃ dhehi sarvavīraṃ vacasyam |
(14.2.6.c) sugaṃ tīrthaṃ suprapāṇaṃ śubhas patī sthāṇuṃ pathiṣṭhām apa durmatiṃ hatam ‖6‖
(14.2.7’) yā oṣadhayo yā nadyo yāni kṣetrāṇi yā vanā |
(14.2.7.c) tās tvā vadhu prajāvatīṃ patye rakṣantu rakṣasaḥ ‖7‖
(14.2.8’) emaṃ panthām arukṣāma sugaṃ svastivāhanam |
(14.2.8.c) yasmin vīro na riṣyaty anyeṣāṃ vindate vasu ‖8‖
(14.2.9’) idaṃ su me naraḥ śṛṇuta yayāśiṣā daṃpatī vāmam aśnutaḥ |
(14.2.9.c) ye gandharvā apsarasaś ca devīr eṣu vānaspatyeṣu ye 'dhi tasthuḥ |
(14.2.9.e) syonās te asyai vadhvai bhavantu mā hiṃsiṣur vahatum uhyamānam ‖9‖
(14.2.10’) ye vadhvaś candraṃ vahatuṃ yakṣmā yanti janā;m anu |
(14.2.10.c) punas tān yajñiyā devā nayantu yata āgatāḥ ‖10‖ {7}
(14.2.11’) mā vidan paripanthino ya āsīdanti daṃpatī |
(14.2.11.c) sugena durgam atītām apa drāntv arātayaḥ ‖11‖
(14.2.12’) saṃ kāśayāmi vahatuṃ brahmaṇā gṛhair aghoreṇa cakṣuṣā mitriyeṇa |
(14.2.12.c) paryāṇaddhaṃ viśvarūpaṃ yad asti syonaṃ patibhyaḥ savitā tat kṛṇotu ‖12‖
(14.2.13’) śivā nārīyam astam āgann imaṃ dhātā lokam asyai dideśa |
(14.2.13.c) tām aryamā bhago aśvinobhā prajāpatiḥ prajayā vardhayantu ‖13‖
(14.2.14’) ātmanvaty urvarā nārīyam āgan tasyāṃ naro vapata bījam asyām |
(14.2.14.c) sā vaḥ prajāṃ janayad vakṣaṇābhyo bibhratī dugdham ṛṣabhasya retaḥ ‖14‖
(14.2.15’) prati tiṣṭha virāḍ asi viṣṇur iveha sarasvati |
(14.2.15.c) sinīvāli pra jāyatāṃ bhagasya sumatāv asat ‖15‖
(14.2.16’) ud va ūrmiḥ śamyā hantv āpo yoktrāṇi muñcata |
(14.2.16.c) māduṣkṛtau vyenasāv aghnyāv aśunam āratām ‖16‖
(14.2.17’) aghoracakṣur apatighnī syonā śagmā suśevā suyamā gṛhebhyaḥ |
(14.2.17.c) vīrasūr devṛkāmā saṃ tvayaidhiṣīmahi sumasyamānā ‖17‖
(14.2.18’) adevṛghny apatighnīhaidhi śivā paśubhyaḥ suyamā suvarcāh |
(14.2.18.c) prajāvatī vīrasūr devṛkāmā syonemam agniṃ gārhapatyaṃ saparya ‖18‖
(14.2.19’) ut tiṣṭhetaḥ kim ichantīdam āgā ahaṃ tveḍe abhibhūḥ svād gṛhāt |
(14.2.19.c) śūnyaiṣī nirṛte yājaganthot tiṣṭhārāte pra pata meha raṃsthāḥ ‖19‖
(14.2.20’) yadā gārhapatyam asaparyait pūrvam agniṃ vadhūr iyam |
(14.2.20.c) adhā sarasvatyai nāri pitṛbhyaś ca namas kuru ‖20‖ {8}
(14.2.21’) śarma varmaitad ā harāsyai nāryā upastire |
(14.2.21.c) sinīvāli pra jāyatāṃ bhagasya sumatāv asat ‖21‖
(14.2.22’) yaṃ balbajaṃ nyasyatha carma copastṛṇīthana |
(14.2.22.c) tad ā rohatu suprajā yā kanyā vindate patim ‖22‖
(14.2.23’) upa stṛṇīhi balbajam adhi carmaṇi rohite |
(14.2.23.c) tatropaviśya suprajā imam agniṃ saparyatu ‖23‖
(14.2.24’) ā roha carmopa sīdāgnim eṣa devo hanti rakṣāṃsi sarvā |
(14.2.24.c) iha prajāṃ janaya patye asmai sujyaiṣṭhyo bhavat putras ta eṣaḥ ‖24‖
(14.2.25’) vi tiṣṭhantāṃ mātur asyā upasthān nānārūpāḥ paśavo jāyamānāḥ |
(14.2.25.c) sumaṅgaly upa sīdemam agniṃ saṃpatnī prati bhūṣeha devān ‖25‖
(14.2.26’) sumaṅgalī prataraṇī gṛhāṇāṃ suśevā patye śvaśurāya śaṃbhūḥ |
(14.2.26.c) syonā śvaśrvai pra gṛhān viśemān ‖26‖
(14.2.27’) syonā bhava śvaśurebhyaḥ syonā patye gṛhebhyaḥ |
(14.2.27.c) syonāsyai sarvasyai viśe syonā puṣṭāyaiṣāṃ bhava ‖27‖
(14.2.28’) sumaṅgalīr iyaṃ vadhūr imāṃ sameta paśyata |
(14.2.28.c) saubhāgyam asyai dattvā daurbhāgyair viparetana ‖28‖
(14.2.29’) yā durhārdo yuvatayo yāś ceha jaratīr api |
(14.2.29.c) varco nv asyai saṃ dattāthāstaṃ viparetana ‖29‖
(14.2.30’) rukmaprastaraṇaṃ vahyaṃ viśvā rūpāṇi bibhratam |
(14.2.30.c) ārohat sūryā sāvitrī bṛhate saubhagāya kam ‖30‖ {9}
(14.2.31’) ā roha talpaṃ sumanasyamāneha prajāṃ janaya patye asmai |
(14.2.31.c) indrāṇīva subudhā budhyamānā jyotiragrā uṣasaḥ prati jāgarāsi ‖31‖
(14.2.32’) devā agre ny apadyanta patnīḥ sam aspṛśanta tanvas tanūbhiḥ |
(14.2.32.c) sūryeva nāri viśvarūpā mahitvā prajāvatī patyā saṃ bhaveha ‖32‖
(14.2.33’) ut tiṣṭheto viśvāvaso namaseḍāmahe tvā |
(14.2.33.c) jāmim icha pitṛṣadaṃ nyaktāṃ sa te bhāgo januṣā tasya viddhi ‖33‖
(14.2.34’) apsarasaḥ sadhamādaṃ madanti havirdhānam antarā sūryaṃ ca |
(14.2.34.c) tās te janitram abhi tāḥ parehi namas te gandharvartunā kṛṇomi ‖34‖
(14.2.35’) namo gandharvasya namase namo bhāmāya cakṣuṣe ca kṛṇmaḥ |
(14.2.35.c) viśvāvaso brahmaṇā te namo 'bhi jāyā apsarasaḥ parehi ‖35‖
(14.2.36’) rāyā vayaṃ sumanasaḥ syāmod ito gandharvam āvīvṛtāma |
(14.2.36.c) agant sa devaḥ paramaṃ sadhastham aganma yatra pratiranta āyuḥ ‖36‖
(14.2.37’) saṃ pitarāv ṛtviye sṛjethāṃ mātā pitā ca retaso bhavāthaḥ |
(14.2.37.c) marya iva yoṣām adhi rohayaināṃ prajāṃ kṛṇvāthām iha puṣyataṃ rayim ‖37‖
(14.2.38’) tāṃ pūṣaṃ chivatamām erayasva yasyāṃ bījaṃ manuṣyā vapanti |
(14.2.38.c) yā na ūrū uśatī viśrayāti yasyām uśantaḥ praharema śepaḥ ‖38‖
(14.2.39’) ā rohorum upa dhatsva hastaṃ pari ṣvajasva jāyāṃ sumanasyamānaḥ |
(14.2.39.c) prajāṃ kṛṇvāthām iha modamānau dīrghaṃ vām āyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu ‖39‖
(14.2.40’) ā vāṃ prajāṃ janayatu prajāpatir ahorātrābhyāṃ sam anaktv aryamā |
(14.2.40.c) adurmaṅgalī patilokam ā viśemaṃ śaṃ no bhava dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade ‖40‖ {10}
(14.2.41’) devair dattaṃ manunā sākam etad vādhūyaṃ vāso vadhvaś ca vastram |
(14.2.41.c) yo brahmaṇe cikituṣe dadāti sa id rakṣāṃsi talpāni hanti ‖41‖
(14.2.42’) yaṃ me datto brahmabhāgaṃ vadhūyor vādhūyaṃ vāso vadhvaś ca vastram |
(14.2.42.c) yuvaṃ brahmaṇe 'numanyamānau bṛhaspate sākam indraś ca dattam ‖42‖
(14.2.43’) syonād yoner adhi badhyamānau hasāmudau mahasā modamānau |
(14.2.43.c) sugū suputrau sugṛhau tarātho jīvāv uṣaso vibhātīḥ ‖43‖
(14.2.44’) navaṃ vasānaḥ surabhiḥ suvāsā udāgāṃ jīva uṣaso vibhātīḥ |
(14.2.44.c) āṇḍāt patatrīvāmukṣi viśvasmād enasas pari ‖44‖
(14.2.45’) śumbhanī dyāvāpṛthivī antisumne mahivrate |
(14.2.45.c) āpaḥ sapta susruvur devīs tā no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖45‖
(14.2.46’) sūryāyai devebhyo mitrāya varuṇāya ca |
(14.2.46.c) ye bhūtasya pracetasas tebhya idam akaraṃ namaḥ ‖46‖
(14.2.47’) ya ṛte cid abhiśriṣaḥ purā jatrubhya ātṛdaḥ |
(14.2.47.c) saṃdhātā saṃdhiṃ maghavā purūvasur niṣkartā vihrutaṃ punaḥ ‖47‖
(14.2.48’) apāsmat tama uchatu nīlaṃ piśaṅgam uta lohitaṃ yat |
(14.2.48.c) nirdahanī yā pṛṣātaky asmin tāṃ sthāṇāv adhy ā sajāmi ‖48‖
(14.2.49’) yāvatīḥ kṛtyā upavāsane yāvanto rājño varuṇasya pāśāḥ |
(14.2.49.c) vyṛddhayo yā asamṛddhayo yā asmin tā sthāṇāv adhi sādayāmi ‖49‖
(14.2.50’) yā me priyatamā tanūḥ sā me bibhāya vāsasaḥ |
(14.2.50.c) tasyāgre tvaṃ vanaspate nīviṃ kṛṇuṣva mā vayaṃ riṣāma ‖50‖ {11}
(14.2.51’) ye antā yāvatīḥ sico ya otavo ye ca tantavaḥ |
(14.2.51.c) vāso yat patnībhir utaṃ tan na syonam upa spṛśāt ‖51‖
(14.2.52’) uśatīḥ kanyalā imāḥ pitṛlokāt patiṃ yatīḥ |
(14.2.52.c) ava dīkṣām asṛkṣata svāhā ‖52‖
(14.2.53’) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14.2.53.c) varco goṣu praviṣṭaṃ yat tenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ‖53‖
(14.2.54’) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14.2.54.c) tejo goṣu praviṣṭaṃ yat tenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ‖54‖
(14.2.55’) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14.2.55.c) bhajo goṣu praviṣṭo yas tenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ‖55‖
(14.2.56’) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14.2.56.c) yaśo goṣu praviṣṭaṃ yat tenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ‖56‖
(14.2.57’) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14.2.57.c) payo goṣu praviṣṭaṃ yat tenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ‖57‖
(14.2.58’) bṛhaspatināvasṛṣṭāṃ viśve devā adhārayan |
(14.2.58.c) raso goṣu praviṣṭo yas tenemāṃ saṃ sṛjāmasi ‖58‖
(14.2.59’) yadīme keśino janā gṛhe te samanartiṣū rodena kṛṇvanto 'gham |
(14.2.59.c) agniṣ ṭvā tasmād enasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām ‖59‖
(14.2.60’) yadīyaṃ duhitā tava vikeśy arudad gṛhe rodena kṛṇvaty agham |
(14.2.60.c) agniṣ ṭvā tasmād enasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām ‖60‖ {12}
(14.2.61’) yaj jāmayo yad yuvatayo gṛhe te samanartiṣū rodena kṛṇvatīr agham |
(14.2.61.c) agniṣ ṭvā tasmād enasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām ‖61‖
(14.2.62’) yat te prajāyāṃ paśuṣu yad vā gṛheṣu niṣṭhitam aghakṛdbhir aghaṃ kṛtam |
(14.2.62.c) agniṣ ṭvā tasmād enasaḥ savitā ca pra muñcatām ‖62‖
(14.2.63’) iyaṃ nāry upa brūte pūlyāny āvapantikā |
(14.2.63.c) dīrghāyur astu me patir jīvāti śaradaḥ śatam ‖63‖
(14.2.64’) ihemāv indra saṃ nuda cakravākeva daṃpatī |
(14.2.64.c) prajayainau svastakau viśvam āyur vy aśnutām ‖64‖
(14.2.65’) yad āsandyām upadhāne yad vopavāsane kṛtam |
(14.2.65.c) vivāhe kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakrur āsnāne tāṃ ni dadhmasi ‖65‖
(14.2.66’) yad duṣkṛtaṃ yac chamalaṃ vivāhe vahatau ca yat |
(14.2.66.c) tat saṃbhalasya kambale mṛjmahe duritaṃ vayam ‖66‖
(14.2.67’) saṃbhale malaṃ sādayitvā kambale duritaṃ vayam |
(14.2.67.c) abhūma yajñiyāḥ śuddhāḥ pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat ‖67‖
(14.2.68’) kṛtrimaḥ kaṇṭakaḥ śatadan ya eṣaḥ |
(14.2.68.c) apāsyāḥ keśyaṃ malam apa śīrṣaṇyaṃ likhāt ‖68‖
(14.2.69’) aṅgādaṅgād vayam asyā apa yakṣmaṃ ni dadhmasi |
(14.2.69.c) tan mā prāpat pṛthivīṃ mota devān divaṃ mā prāpad urv antarikṣam |
(14.2.69.e) apo mā prāpan malam etad agne yamam mā prāpat pitṝṃś ca sarvān ‖69‖
(14.2.70’) saṃ tvā nahyāmi payasā pṛthivyāḥ saṃ tvā nahyāmi payasauṣadhīnām |
(14.2.70.c) saṃ tvā nahyāmi prajayā dhanena sā saṃnaddhā sanuhi vājam emam ‖70‖ {13}
(14.2.71’) amo 'ham asmi sā tvaṃ sāmāham asmy ṛk tvaṃ dyaur ahaṃ pṛthivī tvam |
(14.2.71.c) tāv iha saṃ bhavāva prajām ā janayāvahai ‖71‖
(14.2.72’) janiyanti nāv agravaḥ putriyanti sudānavaḥ |
(14.2.72.c) ariṣṭāsū sacevahi bṛhate vājasātaye ‖72‖
(14.2.73’) ye pitaro vadhūdarśā imaṃ vahatum āgaman |
(14.2.73.c) te asyai vadhvai saṃpatnyai prajāvac charma yachantu ‖73‖
(14.2.74’) yedaṃ pūrvāgan raśanāyamānā prajām asyai draviṇaṃ ceha dattvā |
(14.2.74.c) tāṃ vahantv agatasyānu panthāṃ virāḍ iyaṃ suprajā aty ajaiṣīt ‖74‖
(14.2.75’) pra budhyasva subudhā budhyamānā dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya |
(14.2.75.c) gṛhān gacha gṛhapatnī yathāso dīrghaṃ ta āyuḥ savitā kṛṇotu ‖75‖ {14}

(15.1.1’) vrātya āsīd īyamāna eva sa prajāpatiṃ sam airayat ‖1‖
(15.1.2’) sa prajāpatiḥ suvarṇam ātmann apaśyat tat prājanayat ‖2‖
(15.1.3’) tad ekam abhavat tal lalāmam abhavat tan mahad abhavat taj jyeṣṭham abhavat tad |
(15.1.3.c) brahmābhavat tat tapo 'bhavat tat satyam abhavat tena prājāyata ‖3‖
(15.1.4’) so 'vardhata sa mahān abhavat sa mahādevo 'bhavat ‖4‖
(15.1.5’) sa devānām īśāṃ pary ait sa īśāno 'bhavat ‖5‖
(15.1.6’) sa ekavrātyo bhavat sa dhanur ādatta tad evendradhanuḥ ‖6‖
(15.1.7’) nīlam asyodaraṃ lohitaṃ pṛṣṭham ‖7‖
(15.1.8’) nīlenaivāpriyaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ prorṇoti lohitena dviṣantaṃ vidhyatīti brahmavādino vadanti ‖8‖
(15.1.1’) sa ud atiṣṭhat sa prācīṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖1‖
(15.1.2’) taṃ bṛhac ca rathaṃtaraṃ cādityāś ca viśve ca devā anuvyacalan ‖2‖
(15.1.3’) bṛhate ca vai sa rathaṃtarāya cādityebhyaś ca viśvebhyaś ca devebhya ā vṛścate ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ vrāty
am upavadati ‖3‖
(15.1.4’) bṛhataś ca vai sa rathaṃtarasya cādityānāṃ ca viśveṣāṃ ca devānāṃ priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda
‖4‖
(15.1.5’) tasya prācyāṃ diśi śraddhā puṃścalī mitro māgadho vijñānaṃ vaso 'har uṣṇīṣaṃ rātrī keśā harit
au pravartau kalmalir maṇiḥ ‖5‖
(15.1.6’) bhūtaṃ ca bhaviṣyac ca pariṣkandau mano vipatham ‖6‖
(15.1.7’) mātariśvā ca pavamānaś ca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiś ca yaśaś ca puraḥsarau ‖7
‖
(15.1.8’) 	ainaṃ kīrtir gachaty ā yaśo gachati ya evaṃ veda ‖8‖ [1]
(15.1.9’) sa ud atiṣṭhat sa dakṣiṇāṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖9‖
(15.1.10’) taṃ yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vai sa vāmadevyaṃ ca yajñaś ca yajamānaś ca paśavaś cānuvyacalan ‖10‖
(15.1.11’) yajñāyajñiyāya ca vai sa vāmadevyāya ca yajñāya ca yajamānāya ca paśubhyaś cā vṛścate ya evaṃ vidvā.
msaṃ vrātyam upavadati ‖11‖
(15.1.12’) yajñāyajñiyasya ca vai sa vāmadevyasya ca yajñasya ca yajamānasya ca paśūnāṃ ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya ev
aṃ veda ‖12‖
(15.1.13’) tasya dakṣiṇāyāṃ diśy uṣāḥ puṃścalī mantro māgadho vijñānaṃ vāso 'har uṣṇīṣaṃ rātrī keśā
haritau pravartau kalmalir maṇiḥ ‖13‖
(15.1.14’) amāvāsyā ca paurṇamāsī ca pariṣkandau mano vipatham |
(15.1.14.c) mātariśvā ca pavamānaś ca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiś ca yaśaś ca puraḥsarau |
(15.1.14.e) ainaṃ kīrtir gachaty ā yaśo gachati ya evaṃ veda ‖14‖ [2]
(15.1.15’) sa ud atiṣṭhat sa pratīcīṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖15‖
(15.1.16’) taṃ vairūpaṃ ca vairājaṃ cāpaś ca varuṇaś ca rājānuvyacalan ‖16‖
(15.1.17’) vairūpāya ca vai sa vairājāya cādbhyaś ca varuṇāya ca rājña ā vṛścate ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ vrātyam up
avadati ‖17‖
(15.1.18’) vairūpasya ca vai sa vairājasya cāpāṃ ca varuṇasya ca rājñaḥ priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖18‖
(15.1.19’) tasya pratīcyāṃ diśīrā puṃścalī haso māgadho vijñānaṃ vāso 'har uṣṇīṣaṃ rātrī keśā haritau
pravartau kalmalir maṇiḥ ‖19‖
(15.1.20’) ahaś ca rātrī ca pariṣkandau mano vipatham |
(15.1.20.c) mātariśvā ca pavamānaś ca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ kīrtiś ca yaśaś ca puraḥsarau |
(15.1.20.e) ainaṃ kīrtir gachaty ā yaśo gachati ya evaṃ veda ‖20‖ [3]
(15.1.21’) sa ud atiṣṭhat sa udīcīṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖21‖
(15.1.22’) taṃ śyaitam ca naudhasaṃ ca saptarṣayaś ca somaś ca rājānuvyacalan ‖22‖
(15.1.23’) śyaitāya ca vai sa naudhasāya ca saptarṣibhyaś ca somāya ca rājña ā vṛścate ya evaṃ vidvāṃsaṃ vrātya
m upavadati ‖23‖
(15.1.24’) śyaitasya ca vai sa naudhasasya ca saptarṣīṇāṃ ca somasya ca rājñaḥ priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖
24‖
(15.1.25’) tasyodīcyāṃ diśi vidyut puṃścalī stanayitnur māgadho vijñānaṃ vāso 'har uṣṇīṣaṃ rātrī keśā ha
ritau pravartau kalmalir maṇiḥ ‖25‖
(15.1.26’) śrutaṃ ca viśrutaṃ ca pariṣkandau mano vipatham ‖26‖
(15.1.27’) mātariśvā ca pavamānaś ca vipathavāhau vātaḥ sārathī reṣmā pratodaḥ |
(15.1.27.c) kīrtiś ca yaśaś ca puraḥsarau ‖27‖
(15.1.28’) ainaṃ kīrtir gachaty ā yaśo gachati ya evaṃ veda ‖28‖ [4]

(15.3.1’) sa saṃvatsaram ūrdhvo 'tiṣṭhat taṃ devā abruvan vrātya kiṃ nu tiṣṭhasīti ‖1‖
(15.3.2’) so 'bravīd āsandīṃ me saṃ bharantv iti ‖2‖
(15.3.3’) tasmai vrātyāyāsandīṃ sam abharan ‖3‖
(15.3.4’) tasyā grīṣmaś ca vasantaś ca dvau pādāv āstāṃ śarac ca varṣāś ca dvau ‖4‖
(15.3.5’) bṛhac ca rathaṃtaraṃ cānūcye āstāṃ yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca tiraścye ‖5‖
(15.3.6’) ṛcaḥ prāñcas tantavo yajūṃṣi tiryañcaḥ ‖6‖
(15.3.7’) veda āstaraṇaṃ brahmopabarhaṇam ‖7‖
(15.3.8’) sāmāsāda udgītho 'paśrayaḥ ‖8‖
(15.3.9’) tām āsandīṃ vrātya ārohat ‖9‖
(15.3.10’) tasya devajanāḥ pariṣkandā āsant saṃkalpāḥ prahāyyā viśvāni bhūtāny upasadaḥ ‖10‖
(15.3.11’) viśvāny evāsya bhūtāny upasado bhavanti ya evaṃ veda ‖11‖
(15.3.1’) tasmai prācyā diśaḥ ‖1‖
(15.3.2’) vāsantau māsau goptārāv akurvan bṛhac ca rathaṃtaraṃ cānuṣṭhātārau ‖2‖
(15.3.3’) vāsantāv enaṃ māsau prācyā diśo gopāyato bṛhac ca rathaṃtaraṃ cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ‖3‖ [1]
(15.3.4’) tasmai dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ ‖4‖
(15.3.5’) graiṣmau māsau goptārāv akurvan yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cānuṣṭhātārau ‖5‖
(15.3.6’) graiṣmāv enaṃ māsau dakṣiṇāyā diśo gopāyato yajñāyajñiyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda
‖6‖ [2]
(15.3.7’) tasmai pratīcyā diśaḥ ‖7‖
(15.3.8’) vārṣikau māsau goptārāv akurvan vairūpaṃ ca vairājaṃ cānuṣṭhātārau ‖8‖
(15.3.9’) vārṣikāv enaṃ māsau pratīcyā diśo gopāyato vairūpaṃ ca vairājaṃ cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ‖9‖ [3]
(15.3.10’) tasmā udīcyā diśaḥ ‖10‖
(15.3.11’) śāradau māsau goptārāv akurvaṃ chyaitaṃ ca naudhasaṃ cānuṣṭhātārau ‖11‖
(15.3.12’) śāradāv enaṃ māsāv udīcyā diśo gopāyataḥ śyaitaṃ ca naudhasaṃ cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ‖12‖ [4]
(15.3.13’) tasmai dhruvāyā diśaḥ ‖13‖
(15.3.14’) haimanau māsau goptārāv akurvan bhūmiṃ cāgniṃ cānuṣṭhātārau ‖14‖
(15.3.15’) haimanāv enaṃ māsau dhruvāyā diśo gopāyato bhūmiś cāgniś cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ‖15‖ [5]
(15.3.16’) tasmā ūrdhvāyā diśaḥ ‖16‖
(15.3.17’) śaiśirau māsau goptārāv akurvan divaṃ cādityaṃ cānuṣṭhātārau ‖17‖
(15.3.18’) śaiśirāv enaṃ māsāv ūrdhvāyā diśo gopāyato dyauś cādityaś cānu tiṣṭhato ya evaṃ veda ‖18‖ [6]
(15.3.1’) tasmai prācyā diśo antardeśād bhavam iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan ‖1‖ [1]
(15.3.2’) bhava enam iṣvāsaḥ prācyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati ‖2‖
(15.3.3’) nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ‖3‖ [1]
(15.3.4’) tasmai dakṣiṇāyā diśo antardeśāc charvam iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan ‖4‖
(15.3.5’) śarva enam iśvāso dakṣiṇāyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nā
sya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ‖5‖ [2]
(15.3.6’) tasmai pratīcyā diśo antardeśāt paśupatim iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan ‖6‖
(15.3.7’) paśupatir enam iṣvāsaḥ pratīcyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśano n;
āsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ‖7‖ [3]
(15.3.8’) tasmā udīcyā diśo antardeśād ugraṃ devam iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan ‖8‖
(15.3.9’) ugra enaṃ deva iṣvāsa udīcyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ‖9‖ [4]
(15.3.10’) tasmai dhruvāyā diśo antardeśād rudram iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan ‖10‖
(15.3.11’) rudra enam iṣvāso dhruvāyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya
paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ‖11‖ [5]
(15.3.12’) tasmā ūrdhvāyā diśo antardeśān mahādevam iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan ‖12‖
(15.3.13’) mahādeva enam iṣvāsa ūrdhvāyā diśo antardeśād anuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno n;
āsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ‖13‖ [6]
(15.3.14’) tasmai sarvebhyo antardeśebhya īśānam iṣvāsam anuṣṭhātāram akurvan ‖14‖
(15.3.15’) īśāna enam iṣvāsaḥ sarvebhyo antardeśebhyo 'nuṣṭhātānu tiṣṭhati ‖15‖
(15.3.16’) nainaṃ śarvo na bhavo neśāno nāsya paśūn na samānān hinasti ya evaṃ veda ‖16‖ [7]
(15.3.1’) sa dhruvāṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖1‖
(15.3.2’) taṃ bhūmiś cāgniś cauṣadhayaś ca vanaspatayaś ca vānaspatyāś ca vīrudhaś cānuvyacalan ‖2‖
(15.3.3’) bhūmeś ca vai so 'gneś cauṣadhīnāṃ ca vanaspatīnāṃ ca vānaspatyānāṃ |
(15.3.3.c) ca vīrudhāṃ ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖3‖ [1]
(15.3.4’) sa ūrdhvāṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖4‖
(15.3.5’) tam ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ ca sūryaś ca candraś ca nakṣatrāṇi cānuvyacalan ‖5‖
(15.3.6’) ṛtasya ca vai sa satyasya ca sūryasya ca candrasya ca nakṣatrāṇāṃ ca |
(15.3.6.c) priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖6‖ [2]
(15.3.7’) sa uttamāṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖7‖
(15.3.8’) tam ṛcaś ca sāmāni ca yajūṃṣi ca brahma cānuvyacalan ‖8‖
(15.3.9’) ṛcāṃ ca vai sa sāmnāṃ ca yajuṣāṃ ca brahmaṇaś ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖9‖ [3]
(15.3.10’) sa bṛhatīṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖10‖
(15.3.11’) tam itihāsaś ca purāṇaṃ ca gāthāś ca nārāśaṃsīś cānuvyacalan ‖11‖
(15.3.12’) itihāsasya ca vai sa purāṇasya ca gāthānāṃ ca nārāśaṃsīnāṃ ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖12
‖ [4]
(15.3.13’) sa paramāṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖13‖
(15.3.14’) tam āhavanīyaś ca gārhapatyaś ca dakṣiṇāgniś ca yajñaś ca yajamānaś ca paśavaś cānuvyacalan ‖14‖
(15.3.15’) āhavanīyasya ca vai sa gārhapatyasya ca dakṣiṇāgneś ca yajñasya ca yajamānasya ca paśūnāṃ ca priyaṃ dhā
ma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖15‖ [5]
(15.3.16’) so 'nādiṣṭāṃ diśam anu vy acalat ‖16‖
(15.3.17’) tam ṛtavaś cārtavāś ca lokāś ca laukyāś ca māsāś cārdhamāsāś cāhorātre cānuvyacalan ‖17‖
(15.3.18’) ṛtūnāṃ ca vai sa ārtavānāṃ ca lokānāṃ ca laukyānāṃ ca māsānāṃ cārdhamāsānāṃ cāhorātrayoś ca p
riyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖18‖ [6]
(15.3.19’) so 'nāvṛttāṃ diśam anu vy acalat tato nāvartsyann amanyata ‖19‖
(15.3.20’) tam ditiś cāditiś ceḍā cendrāṇī cānuvyacalan ‖20‖
(15.3.21’) diteś ca vai so 'diteś ceḍāyāś cendrāṇyāś ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖21‖ [7]
(15.3.22’) sa diśo 'nu vy acalat taṃ virāḍ anu vy acalat sarve ca devāḥ sarvāś ca devatāḥ ‖22‖
(15.3.23’) virājaś ca vai sa sarveṣāṃ ca devānāṃ sarvāsāṃ ca devatānāṃ priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖23|
| [8]
(15.3.24’) sa sarvān antardeśān anu vy acalat ‖24‖
(15.3.25’) taṃ prajāpatiś ca parameṣṭhī ca pitā ca pitāmahaś cānuvyacalan ‖25‖
(15.3.26’) prajāpateś ca vai parameṣṭhinaś ca pituś ca pitāmahasya ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖26‖ [9]

(15.7.1’) sa mahimā sadrur bhūtvāntaṃ pṛthivyā agachat sa samudro 'bhavat ‖1‖
(15.7.2’) taṃ prajāpatiś ca parameṣṭhī ca pitā ca pitāmahaś cāpaś ca śraddhā ca varṣaṃ bhūtvānuvyavartayanta ‖2
‖
(15.7.3’) ainam āpo gachaty ainaṃ śraddhā gachaty ainaṃ varṣaṃ gachati ya evaṃ veda ‖3‖
(15.7.4’) taṃ śraddhā ca yajñaś ca lokaś cānnaṃ cānnādyaṃ ca bhūtvābhiparyāvartanta ‖4‖
(15.7.5’) ainaṃ śraddhā gachaty ainaṃ yajño gachaty ainaṃ loko gachaty ainam annaṃ gachaty ainam annādyaṃ gachati ya ev
aṃ veda ‖5‖

(15.8.1’) so 'rajyata tato rājanyo 'jāyata ‖1‖
(15.8.2’) sa viśaḥ sabandhūn annam annādyam abhyudatiṣṭhat ‖2‖
(15.8.3’) viśāṃ ca vai sa sabandhūnāṃ cānnasya cānnādyasya ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖3‖

(15.9.1’) sa viśo 'nu vy acalat ‖1‖
(15.9.2’) taṃ sabhā ca samitiś ca senā ca śurā cānuvyacalan ‖2‖
(15.9.3’) sabhāyāś ca vai sa samiteś ca senāyāś ca surāyāś ca priyaṃ dhāma bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖3‖

(15.10.1’) tad yasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyo rājño 'tithir gṛhān āgachet ‖1‖
(15.10.2’) śreyāṃsam enam ātmano mānayet tathā kṣatrāya nā vṛścate tathā rāṣṭrāya nā vṛścate ‖2‖
(15.10.3’) ato vai brahma ca kṣatraṃ cod atiṣṭhatāṃ te abrūtāṃ kaṃ pra viśāveti ‖3‖
(15.10.4’) ato vai bṛhaspatim eva brahma pra viśatv indraṃ kṣatraṃ tathā vā iti ‖4‖
(15.10.5’) ato vai bṛhaspatim eva brahma prāviśad indraṃ kṣatraṃ ‖5‖
(15.10.6’) iyaṃ vā u pṛthivī bṛhaspatir dyaur evendraḥ ‖6‖
(15.10.7’) ayaṃ vā u agnir brahmāsāv ādityaḥ kṣatram ‖7‖
(15.10.8’) ainaṃ brahma gachati brahmavarcasī bhavati ‖8‖
(15.10.9’) yaḥ pṛthivīṃ bṛhaspatim agniṃ brahma veda ‖9‖
(15.10.10’) ainam indriyaṃ gachatīndriyavān bhavati ‖10‖
(15.10.11’) ya ādityaṃ kṣatraṃ divam indraṃ veda ‖11‖

(15.11.1’) tad yasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyo 'tithir gṛhān āgachet ‖1‖
(15.11.2’) svayam enam abhyudetya brūyād vrātya kvāvātsīr vrātyodakaṃ vrātya tarpayantu vrātya yathā te priyaṃ tath
āstu vrātya yathā te vaśas tathāstu vrātya yathā te nikāmas tathāstv iti ‖2‖
(15.11.3’) yad enam āha vrātya kvāvātsīr iti patha eva tena devayānān ava runddhe ‖3‖
(15.11.4’) yad enam āha vrātyodakam ity apa eva tenāva runddhe ‖4‖
(15.11.5’) yad enam āha vrātya tarpayantv iti prāṇam eva tena varṣīyāṃsaṃ kurute ‖5‖
(15.11.6’) yad enam āha vrātya yathā te priyaṃ tathāstv iti priyam eva tenāva runddhe ‖6‖
(15.11.7’) ainaṃ priyaṃ gachati priyaḥ priyasya bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖7‖
(15.11.8’) yad enam āha vrātya yathā te vaśas tathāstv iti vaśam eva tenāva runddhe ‖8‖
(15.11.9’) ainaṃ vaśo gachati vaśī vaśināṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖9‖
(15.11.10’) yad enam āha vrātya yathā te nikāmas tathāstv iti nikāmam eva tenāva runddhe ‖10‖
(15.11.11’) ainaṃ nikāmo gachati nikāme nikāmasya bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖11‖

(15.12.1’) tad yasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātya uddhṛteṣv agniṣu adhiśrite 'gnihotre 'tithir gṛhān āgachet ‖1‖
(15.12.2’) svayam enam abhyudetya brūyād vrātyāti sṛja hoṣyāmīti ‖2‖
(15.12.3’) sa cātisṛjej juhuyān na cātisṛjen na juhuyāt ‖3‖
(15.12.4’) sa ya evaṃ viduṣā vrātyenātisṛsṭo juhoti ‖4‖
(15.12.5’) pra pitṛyāṇaṃ panthāṃ jānāti pra devayānam ‖5‖
(15.12.6’) na deveṣv ā vṛścate hutam asya bhavati ‖6‖
(15.12.7’) pary asyāsmiṃl loka āyatanaṃ śiṣyate ya evaṃ viduṣā vrātyenātisṛṣṭo juhoti ‖7‖
(15.12.8’) atha ya evaṃ viduṣā vrātyenānatisṛṣṭo juhoti ‖8‖
(15.12.9’) na pitṛyāṇaṃ panthāṃ jānāti na devayānam ‖9‖
(15.12.10’) ā deveṣu vṛścate ahutam asya bhavati ‖10‖
(15.12.11’) nāsyāsmiṃl loka āyatanaṃ śiṣyate ya evaṃ viduṣā vrātyenānatisṛṣṭo juhoti ‖11‖
(15.12.1’) tad yasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātya ekāṃ rātrim atithir gṛhe vasati ‖1‖
(15.12.2’) ye pṛthivyāṃ punyā lokās tān eva tenāva runddhe ‖2‖ [1]
(15.12.3’) tad yasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyo dvitīyāṃ rātrim atithir gṛhe vasati ‖3‖
(15.12.4’) ye 'ntarikṣe punyā lokās tān eva tenāva runddhe ‖4‖ [2]
(15.12.5’) tad yasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyas tṛtīyāṃ rātrim atithir gṛhe vasati ‖5‖
(15.12.6’) ye divi punyā lokās tān eva tenāva runddhe ‖6‖ [3]
(15.12.7’) tad yasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyaś caturthīṃ rātrim atithir gṛhe vasati ‖7‖
(15.12.8’) ye puṇyānāṃ punyā lokās tān eva tenāva runddhe ‖8‖ [4]
(15.12.9’) tad yasyaivaṃ vidvān vrātyo 'parimitā rātrīr atithir gṛhe vasati ‖9‖
(15.12.10’) ya evāparimitāḥ punyā lokās tān eva tenāva runddhe ‖10‖ [5]
(15.12.11’) atha yasyāvrātyo vrātyabruvo nāmabibhraty atithir gṛhān āgachet ‖11‖ [6]
(15.12.12’) karṣed enaṃ na cainam karṣet ‖12‖ [7]
(15.12.13’) asyai devatāyā udakaṃ yācāmīmāṃ devatāṃ vāsaya imām imāṃ devatāṃ pari veveṣmīty enaṃ pari veviṣy
āt ‖13‖ [8]
(15.12.14’) tasyām evāsya tad devatāyāṃ hutaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖14‖ [9]
(15.12.1’) sa yat prācīṃ diśam anu vyacalan mārutaṃ śardho bhūtvānuvyacalan mano 'nnādaṃ kṛtvā ‖1‖
(15.12.2’) manasānnādenānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖2‖ [1]
(15.12.3’) sa yad dakṣiṇāṃ diśam anu vyacalad bhūtvānuvyacalad balam annādaṃ kṛtvā ‖3‖
(15.12.4’) balenānnādenānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖4‖ [2]
(15.12.5’) sa yat pratīcīṃ diśam anu vyacalad varuṇo rājā bhūtvānuvyacalad apo 'nnādīḥ kṛtvā ‖5‖
(15.12.6’) adbhir annādībhir annam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖6‖ [3]
(15.12.7’) sa yad udīcīṃ diśam anu vyacalad varuṇo rājā bhūtvānuvyacalad saptarṣibhir huta āhutim annādīṃ kṛtv
ā ‖7‖
(15.12.8’) āhutyānnādyānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖8‖ [4]
(15.12.9’) sa yad dhruvāṃ diśam anu vyacalad viṣṇur bhūtvānuvyacalad virājam annādīṃ kṛtvā ‖9‖
(15.12.10’) virājānnādyānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖10‖ [5]
(15.12.11’) sa yat paśūn anu vyacalad rudro bhūtvānuvyacalad oṣadhīr annādīḥ kṛtvā ‖11‖
(15.12.12’) oṣadhībhir annādībhir annam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖12‖ [6]
(15.12.13’) sa yat pitṝn anu vyacalad yamo rājā bhūtvānuvyacalat svadhākāram annādaṃ kṛtvā ‖13‖
(15.12.14’) svadhākāreṇānnādenānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖14‖ [7]
(15.12.15’) sa yan manuṣyān anu vyacalad agnir bhūtvānuvyacalat svāhākāram annādaṃ kṛtvā ‖15‖
(15.12.16’) svāhākāreṇānnādenānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖16‖ [8]
(15.12.17’) sa yad ūrdhvāṃ diśam anu vyacalad bṛhaspatir bhūtvānuvyacalad vaṣaṭkāram annādaṃ kṛtvā ‖17‖
(15.12.18’) vaṣaṭkāreṇāṇnādenānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖18‖ [9]
(15.12.19’) sa yad devān anu vyacalad īśāno bhūtvānuvyacalan manyum annādaṃ kṛtvā ‖19‖
(15.12.20’) manyunānnādenānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖20‖ [10]
(15.12.21’) sa yat prajā anu vyacalat prajāpatir bhūtvānuvyacalat prāṇam annādaṃ kṛtvā ‖21‖
(15.12.22’) prāṇenānnādenānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖22‖ [11]
(15.12.23’) sa yat sarvānn antardeśān anu vyacalat parameṣṭhī bhūtvānuvyacalad brahmānnādaṃ kṛtvā ‖23‖
(15.12.24’) brahmaṇānnādenānnam atti ya evaṃ veda ‖24‖ [12]

(15.15.1’) tasya vrātyasya ‖1‖
(15.15.2’) sapta prāṇāḥ saptāpānāḥ sapta vyānāḥ ‖2‖
(15.15.3’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.15.3.c) yo 'sya prathamaḥ prāṇa ūrdhvo nāmāyaṃ so agniḥ ‖3‖
(15.15.4’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.15.4.c) yo 'sya dvitīyaḥ prāṇaḥ prauḍho nāmāsau sa ādityaḥ ‖4‖
(15.15.5’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.15.5.c) yo 'sya tṛtīyaḥ prāṇo 'bhyūḍho nāmāsau sa candramāḥ ‖5‖
(15.15.6’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.15.6.c) yo 'sya caturthah prāṇo vibhūr nāmāyaṃ sa pavamānaḥ ‖6‖
(15.15.7’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.15.7.c) yo 'sya pañcamaḥ prāṇo yonir nāma tā imā āpaḥ ‖7‖
(15.15.8’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.15.8.c) yo 'sya ṣaṣṭhaḥ prāṇaḥ priyo nāma ta ime paśavaḥ ‖8‖
(15.15.9’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.15.9.c) yo 'sya saptamaḥ prāṇo 'parimito nāma tā prajāḥ ‖9‖

(15.16.1’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.16.1.c) yo 'sya prathamo 'pānaḥ sā paurṇamāsī ‖1‖
(15.16.2’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.16.2.c) yo 'sya dvitīyo 'pānaḥ  sāṣṭakā |
(15.16.3’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.16.3.c) yo 'sya tṛtīyo 'pānaḥ sāmāvāsyā ‖3‖
(15.16.4’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.16.4.c) yo 'sya caturtho 'pānaḥ sā śraddhā ‖4‖
(15.16.5’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.16.5.c) yo 'sya pañcamo 'pānaḥ sā dīkṣā ‖5‖
(15.16.6’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.16.6.c) yo 'sya ṣaṣṭo 'pānaḥ sa yajñaḥ ‖6‖
(15.16.7’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.16.7.c) yo 'sya saptamo 'pānas tā imā dakṣiṇāḥ ‖7‖

(15.17.1’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.1.c) yo 'sya prathamo vyānaḥ seyaṃ bhūmiḥ ‖1‖
(15.17.2’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.2.c) yo 'sya dvitīyo vyānas tad antarikṣam ‖2‖
(15.17.3’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.3.c) yo 'sya tṛtīyo vyānaḥ sā dyauḥ ‖3‖
(15.17.4’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.4.c) yo 'sya caturtho vyānas tāni nakṣatrāṇi ‖4‖
(15.17.5’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.5.c) yo 'sya pañcamo vyānas ta ṛtavaḥ ‖5‖
(15.17.6’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.6.c) yo 'sya ṣaṣṭho vyānas ta ārtavāḥ ‖6‖
(15.17.7’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.7.c) yo 'sya saptamo vyānaḥ sa saṃvatsaraḥ ‖7‖
(15.17.8’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.8.c) samānam arthaṃ pari yanti devāḥ saṃvatsaraṃ vā etad ṛtavo 'nupariyanti vrātyaṃ ca ‖8‖
(15.17.9’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.9.c) yad ādityam abhisaṃviśanty amāvāsyāṃ caiva paurṇamāsīṃ ca ‖9‖
(15.17.10’) tasya vrātyasya |
(15.17.10.c) ekaṃ tad eṣām amṛtatvam ity āhutir eva ‖10‖

(15.18.1’) tasya vrātyasya ‖1‖
(15.18.2’) yad asya dakṣiṇam akṣy asau sa ādityo yad asya savyam akṣy asau sa candramāḥ ‖2‖
(15.18.3’) yo 'sya dakṣiṇaḥ karṇo 'yaṃ so agnir yo 'sya savyaḥ karṇo 'yaṃ sa pavamānaḥ ‖3‖
(15.18.4’) ahorātre nāsike ditiś cāditiś ca śīrṣakapāle saṃvatsaraḥ śiraḥ ‖4‖
(15.18.5’) ahnā pratyaṅ vrātyo rātryā prāṅ namo vrātyāya ‖5‖

(16.1.1’) atisṛṣṭo apāṃ vṛṣabho 'tisṛṣṭā agnayo divyāḥ ‖1‖
(16.1.2’) rujan parirujan mṛṇan pramṛṇan ‖2‖
(16.1.3’) mroko manohā khano nirdāha ātmadūṣis tanūdūṣiḥ ‖3‖
(16.1.4’) idaṃ tam ati sṛjāmi taṃ mābhyavanikṣi ‖4‖
(16.1.5’) tena tam abhyatisṛjāmo yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ ‖5‖
(16.1.6’) apām agram asi samudraṃ vo 'bhyavasṛjāmi ‖6‖
(16.1.7’) yo 'psv agnir ati taṃ sṛjāmi mrokaṃ khaniṃ tanūdūṣim ‖7‖
(16.1.8’) yo va āpo 'gnir āviveśa sa eṣa yad vo ghoraṃ tad etat ‖8‖
(16.1.9’) indrasya va indriyeṇābhi ṣiñcet ‖9‖
(16.1.10’) ariprā āpo apa ripram asmat ‖10‖
(16.1.11’) prāsmad eno vahantu pra duṣvapnyaṃ vahantu ‖11‖
(16.1.12’) śivena mā cakṣuṣā paśyatāpaḥ śivayā tanvopa spṛśata tvacaṃ me ‖12‖
(16.1.13’) śivān agnīn apsuṣado havāmahe mayi kṣatraṃ varca ā dhatta devīḥ ‖13‖

(16.2.1’) nir durarmaṇya ūrjā madhumatī vāk ‖1‖
(16.2.2’) madhumatī stha madhumatīṃ vācam udeyam ‖2‖
(16.2.3’) upahūto me gopāḥ upahūto gopīthaḥ ‖3‖
(16.2.4’) suśrutau karṇau bhadraśrutau karṇau bhadraṃ ślokaṃ śrūyāsam ‖4‖
(16.2.5’) suśrutiś ca mopaśrutiś ca mā hāsiṣṭāṃ sauparṇaṃ cakṣur ajasraṃ jyotiḥ ‖5‖
(16.2.6’) ṛṣīṇāṃ prastaro 'si namo 'stu daivāya prastarāya ‖6‖

(16.3.1’) mūrdhāhaṃ rayīṇāṃ mūrdhā samānānāṃ bhūyāsam ‖1‖
(16.3.2’) rujaś ca mā venaś ca mā hāsiṣṭāṃ mūrdhā ca mā vidharmā ca mā hāsiṣṭām ‖2‖
(16.3.3’) urvaś ca mā camasaś ca mā hāsiṣṭāṃ dhartā ca mā dharuṇaś ca mā hāsiṣṭām ‖3‖
(16.3.4’) vimokaś ca mārdrapaviś ca mā hāsiṣṭām ārdradānuś ca mā mātariśvā ca mā hāsiṣṭām ‖4‖
(16.3.5’) bṛhaspatir ma ātmā nṛmaṇā nāma hṛdyaḥ ‖5‖
(16.3.6’) asaṃtāpaṃ me hṛdayam urvī gavyūtiḥ samudro asmi vidharmaṇā ‖6‖

(16.4.1’) nābhir ahaṃ rayīṇāṃ nābhiḥ samānānāṃ bhūyāsam ‖1‖
(16.4.2’) svāsad asi sūṣā amṛto martyeśv ā ‖2‖
(16.4.3’) mā māṃ prāṇo hāsīn mo apāno 'vahāya parā gāt ‖3‖
(16.4.4’) sūryo māhnaḥ pātv agniḥ pṛthivyā vāyur antarikṣād yamo manuṣyebhyaḥ sarasvatī pārthivebhyaḥ ‖4‖
(16.4.5’) prāṇāpanau mā mā hāsiṣṭam mā jane pra meṣi ‖5‖
(16.4.6’) svasty adyoṣaso doṣasaś ca sarva āpaḥ sarvagaṇo aśīya ‖6‖
(16.4.7’) śakvarī stha paśavo mopa stheṣur mitrāvaruṇau me prāṇāpānāv agnir me dakṣaṃ dadhātu ‖7‖

(16.5.7’) vidma te svapna janitraṃ grāhyāḥ putro 'si yamasya karaṇaḥ ‖1‖
(16.5.7’) vidma te svapna janitraṃ nirṛtyāḥ putro 'si yamasya karaṇaḥ |
(16.5.7.c) antako 'si mṛtyur asi |
(16.5.7.e) taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyāt pāhi ‖4‖
(16.5.7’)  vidma te svapna janitraṃ abhūtyāḥ putro 'si yamasya karaṇaḥ |
(16.5.7.c) antako 'si mṛtyur asi |
(16.5.7.e) taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyāt pāhi ‖5‖
(16.5.7’) vidma te svapna janitraṃ nirbhūtyāḥ putro 'si yamasya karaṇaḥ |
(16.5.7.c) antako 'si mṛtyur asi |
(16.5.7.e) taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyāt pāhi ‖6‖
(16.5.7’) vidma te svapna janitraṃ parābhūtyāḥ putro 'si yamasya karaṇaḥ |
(16.5.7.c) antako 'si mṛtyur asi |
(16.5.7.e) taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyāt pāhi ‖7‖
(16.5.7’) vidma te svapna janitraṃ devajāmīnāṃ putro 'si yamasya karaṇaḥ ‖8‖

(16.6.1’) ajaiṣmādyāsanām adyāmūm anāgaso vayam ‖1‖
(16.6.2’) uṣo yasmād duṣvapnyād abhaiṣmāpa tad uchatu ‖2‖
(16.6.3’) dviṣate tat parā vaha śapate tat parā vaha ‖3‖
(16.6.4’) yaṃ dviṣmo yaś ca no dveṣṭi tasmā enad gamayāmaḥ ‖4‖
(16.6.5’) uṣā devī vācā saṃvidānā vāg devy uṣasā saṃvidānā ‖5‖
(16.6.6’) uṣas patir vācas patinā saṃvidāno vācas patinā saṃvidānaḥ ‖6‖
(16.6.7’) te 'muṣmai parā vahantv arāyān durṇāmnaḥ sadānvāḥ ‖7‖
(16.6.8’) kumbhīkāḥ dūṣīkāḥ pīyakān ‖8‖
(16.6.9’) jāgradduṣvapnyaṃ svapneduṣvapnyam ‖9‖
(16.6.10’) anāgamiṣyato varān avitteḥ saṃkalpān amucyā druhaḥ pāśān ‖10‖
(16.6.11’) tad amuṣmā agne devāḥ parā vahantu vaghrir yathāsad vithuro na sādhuḥ ‖11‖

(16.7.1’) tenainaṃ vidhyāmy abhūtyainaṃ vidhyāmi nirbhūtyainaṃ vidhyāmi parābhūtyainaṃ vidhyāmi grāhyainaṃ vidhyāmi ta
masainaṃ vidhyāmi ‖1‖
(16.7.2’) devānām enaṃ ghoraiḥ krūraiḥ praiṣair abhipreṣyāmi ‖2‖
(16.7.3’) vaiśvānarasyainaṃ damṣṭrayor api dadhāmi ‖3‖
(16.7.4’) evānevāva sā garat ‖4‖
(16.7.5’) yo 'smān dveṣṭi tam ātmā dveṣṭu yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ sa ātmānaṃ dveṣṭu ‖5‖
(16.7.6’) nir dviṣantaṃ divo niḥ pṛthivyā nir antarikṣād bhajāma ‖6‖
(16.7.7’) suyāmaṃś cākṣuṣa ‖7‖
(16.7.8’) idam aham āmuṣyāyaṇe 'muṣyāḥ putre duṣvapnyaṃ mṛje ‖8‖
(16.7.9’) yad adoado abhyagachaṃ yad doṣā yat pūrvāṃ rātrim ‖9‖
(16.7.10’) yaj jāgrad yat supto yad divā yan naktam ‖10‖
(16.7.11’) yad aharahar abhigachāmi tasmād enam ava daye ‖11‖
(16.7.12’) taṃ jahi tena mandasva tasya pṛṣṭīr api śṛṇīhi ‖12‖

(17.1.1’) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ sāsahānaṃ sahīyāṃsam |
(17.1.1.c) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |
(17.1.1.e) īḍyaṃ nāma hva indram āyuṣmān bhūyāsam ‖1‖

(17.1.2.c) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |

(17.1.3’) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ sāsahānaṃ sahīyāṃsam |
(17.1.3.c) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |

(17.1.4’) viṣāsahiṃ sahamānaṃ sāsahānaṃ sahīyāṃsam |
(17.1.4.c) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |

(17.1.5.c) sahamānaṃ sahojitaṃ svarjitaṃ gojitaṃ saṃdhanājitam |

(17.1.6’) ud ihy ud ihi sūrya varcasā mābhyudihi |
(17.1.6.c) dviṣaṃś ca mahyaṃ radhyatu mā cāhaṃ dviṣate radhaṃ taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17.1.6.e) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ‖6‖
(17.1.7’) ud ihy ud ihi sūrya varcasā mābhyudihi |
(17.1.7.c) āṃś ca paśyāmi yāṃś ca na teṣu mā sumatiṃ kṛdhi taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17.1.7.e) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ‖7‖
(17.1.8’) mā tvā dabhant salile apsv antar ye pāśina upatiṣṭhanty atra |
(17.1.8.c) hitvāśastiṃ divam ārukṣa etāṃ sa no mṛḍa sumatau te syāma taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17.1.8.e) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ‖8‖
(17.1.9’) tvaṃ na indra mahate saubhagāyādabdhebhiḥ pari pāhy aktubhis taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17.1.9.c) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ‖9‖
(17.1.10’) tvaṃ na indrotibhiḥ śivābhiḥ śaṃtamo bhava |
(17.1.10.c) ārohaṃs tridivaṃ divo gṛṇānaḥ somapītaye priyadhāmā svastaye taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāni |
(17.1.10.e) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ‖10‖ {1}
(17.1.11’) tvam indrāsi viśvajit sarvavit puruhūtas tvam indra |
(17.1.11.c) tvam indremaṃ suhavaṃ stomam erayasva sa no mṛḍa sumatau te syāma taved viṣṇo bahūdhā vīryāṇi |
(17.1.11.e) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ‖11‖

(17.1.12.e) tvaṃ naḥ pṛṇīhi paśubhir viśvarūpaiḥ sudhāyāṃ mā dhehi parame vyoman ‖12‖

(18.1.1’) o cit sakhāyaṃ sakhyā vavṛtyāṃ tiraḥ puru cid arṇavaṃ jaganvān |

(18.1.2’) na te sakhā sakhyaṃ vaṣṭy etat salakṣmā yad viṣurūpā bhavati |
(18.1.2.c) mahas putrāso asurasya vīrā divo dhartāra urviyā pari khyan ‖2‖
(18.1.3’) uśanti ghā te amṛtāsa etad ekasya cit tyajasaṃ martyasya |
(18.1.3.c) ni te mano manasi dhāyy asme janyuḥ patis tanvam ā viviṣyāḥ ‖3‖
(18.1.4’) na yat purā cakṛmā kad dha nūnam ṛtaṃ vadanto anṛtaṃ rapema |

(18.1.9’) na tiṣṭhanti na ni miṣanty ete devānāṃ spaśa iha ye caranti |
(18.1.9.c) anyena mad āhano yāhi tūyaṃ tena vi vṛha rathyeva cakrā ‖9‖

(18.1.10.c) divā pṛthivyā mithunā sabandhū yamīr yamasya vivṛhād ajāmi ‖10‖ {1}
(18.1.11’) ā ghā tā gachān uttarā yugāni yatra jāmayaḥ kṛṇavann ajāmi |
(18.1.11.c) upa barbṛhi vṛṣabhāya bāhum anyam ichasva subhage patiṃ mat ‖11‖
(18.1.12’) kiṃ bhrātāsad yad anāthaṃ bhavāti kim u svasā yan nirṛtir nigachāt |
(18.1.12.c) kāmamūtā bahv etad rapāmi tanvā me tanvaṃ saṃ pipṛgdhi ‖12‖
(18.1.13’) na te nāthaṃ yamy atrāham asmi na te tanūṃ tanvā sam papṛcyām |
(18.1.13.c) anyena mat pramudaḥ kalpayasva na te bhrātā subhage vaṣṭy etat ‖13‖
(18.1.14’) na vā u te tanūṃ tanvā saṃ pipṛcyāṃ pāpam āhur yaḥ svasāraṃ nigachāt |
(18.1.14.c) asaṃyad etan manaso hṛdo me bhrātā svasuḥ śayane yac chayīya ‖14‖
(18.1.15’) bato batāsi yama naiva te mano hṛdayaṃ cāvidām ā |
(18.1.15.c) anyā kila tvāṃ kakṣyeva yuktaṃ pari ṣvajātau libujeva vṛkṣam ‖15‖
(18.1.16’) anyam ū ṣu yamy anya u tvāṃ pari ṣvajātau libujeva vṛkṣam |
(18.1.16.c) tasya vā tvaṃ mana ichā sa vā tavādhā kṛṇuṣva saṃvidaṃ subhadrām ‖16‖
(18.1.17’) trīṇi chandāṃsi kavayo vi yetire pururūpaṃ darśataṃ viśvacakṣaṇam |
(18.1.17.c) āpo vātā oṣadhayas tāny ekasmin bhuvana ārpitāni ‖17‖
(18.1.18’) vṛṣā vṛṣṇe duduhe dohasā divaḥ payāṃsi yahvo aditer adābhyaḥ |
(18.1.18.c) viśvaṃ sa veda varuṇo yathā dhiyā sa yajñiyo yajati yajñiyā;m ṛtūn ‖18‖
(18.1.19’) rapad gandharvīr apyā ca yoṣaṇā nadasya nāde pari pātu no manaḥ |
(18.1.19.c) iṣṭasya madhye aditir ni dhātu no bhrātā no jyeṣṭhaḥ prathamo vi vocati ‖19‖
(18.1.20’) so cit nu bhadrā kṣumatī yaśasvaty uṣā uvāsa manave svarvatī |
(18.1.20.c) yad īm uśantam uśatām anu kratum agniṃ hotāraṃ vidathāya jījanan ‖20‖ {2}
(18.1.21’) adha tyaṃ drapsaṃ vibhvaṃ vicakṣanaṃ vir ābharad iṣiraḥ śyeno adhvare |
(18.1.21.c) yadī viśo vṛṇate dasmam āryā agniṃ hotāram adha dhīr ajāyata ‖21‖
(18.1.22’) sadāsi raṇvo yavaseva puṣyate hotrābhir agne manuṣaḥ svadhvaraḥ |
(18.1.22.c) viprasya vā yac chaśamāna ukthyo vājaṃ sasavā;m upayāsi bhūribhiḥ ‖22‖
(18.1.23’) ud īraya pitarā jāra ā bhagam iyakṣati haryato hṛtta iṣyati |
(18.1.23.c) vivakti vahniḥ svapasyate makhas taviṣyate asuro vepate matī ‖23‖
(18.1.24’) yas te agne sumatiṃ marto akhyat sahasaḥ sūno ati sa pra śṛṇve |
(18.1.24.c) iṣaṃ dadhāno vahamāno aśvair ā sa dyumā;m amavān bhūṣati dyūn ‖24‖
(18.1.25’) śrudhī no agne sadane sadhasthe yukṣvā ratham amṛtasya dravitnum |
(18.1.25.c) ā no vaha rodasī devaputre mākir devānām apa bhūr iha syāḥ ‖25‖
(18.1.26’) yad agna eṣā samitir bhavāti devī deveṣu yajatā yajatra |
(18.1.26.c) ratnā ca yad vibhajāsi svadhāvo bhāgaṃ no atra vasumantaṃ vītāt ‖26‖
(18.1.27’) anv agnir uṣasām agram akhyad anv ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ |
(18.1.27.c) anu sūrya uṣaso anu raśmīn dyāvāpṛthivī ā viveśa ‖27‖
(18.1.28’) praty agnir uṣasām agram akhyat praty ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ |
(18.1.28.c) prati sūryasya purudhā ca raśmīn prati dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatāna ‖28‖
(18.1.29’) dyāvā ha kṣāmā prathame ṛtenābhiśrāve bhavataḥ satyavācā |
(18.1.29.c) devo yan martān yajathāya kṛṇvant sīdad dhotā pratyaṅ svam asuṃ yan ‖29‖
(18.1.30’) devo devān paribhūr ṛtena vahā no havyaṃ prathamaś cikitvān |
(18.1.30.c) dhūmaketuḥ samidhā bhāṛjīko mandro hotā nityo vācā yajīyān ‖30‖ {3}
(18.1.31’) arcāmi vāṃ vardhāyāpo ghṛtasnū dyāvābhūmī śṛṇutaṃ rodasī me |
(18.1.31.c) ahā yad devā asunītim āyan madhvā no atra pitarā śiśītām ‖31‖
(18.1.32’) svāvṛg devasyāmṛtaṃ yadī gor ato jātāso dhārayanta urvī |
(18.1.32.c) viśve devā anu tat te yajur gur duhe yad enī divyaṃ ghṛtam vāḥ ‖32‖
(18.1.33’) kiṃ svin no rājā jagṛhe kad asyāti vrataṃ cakṛmā ko vi veda |
(18.1.33.c) mitras cid dhi ṣmā juhurāṇo devāṃ chloko na yātām api vājo asti ‖33‖
(18.1.34’) durmantv atrāmṛtasya nāma salakṣmā yad viṣurūpā bhavāti |
(18.1.34.c) yamasya yo manavate sumantv agne tam ṛṣva pāhy aprayuchan ‖34‖
(18.1.35’) yasmin devā vidathe mādayante vivasvataḥ sadane dhārayante |
(18.1.35.c) sūrye jyotir adadhur māsy aktūn pari dyotaniṃ carato ajasrā ‖35‖
(18.1.36’) yasmin devā manmani saṃcaranty apīcye na vayam asya vidma |
(18.1.36.c) mitro no atrāditir anāgānt savitā devo varuṇāya vocat ‖36‖
(18.1.37’) sakhāya ā śiṣāmahe brahmendrāya vajriṇe |
(18.1.37.c) stuṣa ū ṣu nṛtamāya dhṛṣṇave ‖37‖
(18.1.38’) śavasā hy asi śruto vṛtrahatyena vṛtrahā |
(18.1.38.c) maghair maghono ati śūra dāśasi ‖38‖
(18.1.39’) stego na kṣām aty eṣi pṛthivīṃ mahī no vātā iha vāntu bhūmau |
(18.1.39.c) mitro no atra varuṇo yujamāno agnir vane na vy asṛṣṭa śokam ‖39‖
(18.1.40’) stuhi śrutaṃ gartasadaṃ janānāṃ rājānaṃ bhīmam upahatnum ugram |
(18.1.40.c) mṛḍā jaritre rudra stavāno anyam asmat te ni vapantu senyam ‖40‖ {4}
(18.1.41’) sarasvatīṃ devayanto havante sarasvatīm adhvare tāyamāne |
(18.1.41.c) sarasvatīṃ sukṛto havante sarasvatī dāśuṣe vāryaṃ dāt ‖41‖
(18.1.42’) sarasvatīṃ pitaro havante dakṣinā yajñam abhinakṣamāṇāḥ |
(18.1.42.c) āsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam anamīvā iṣa ā dhehy asme ‖42‖
(18.1.43’) sarasvati yā sarathaṃ yayāthokthaiḥ svadhābhir devi pitṛbhir madantī |
(18.1.43.c) sahasrārgham iḍo atra bhāgaṃ rāyas poṣaṃ yajamānāya dhehi ‖43‖
(18.1.44’) ud īrathām avara ut parāsa un madhyamāḥ pitaraḥ somyāsaḥ |
(18.1.44.c) asuṃ ya īyur avṛkā ṛtajñās te no 'vantu pitaro haveṣu ‖44‖
(18.1.45’) āhaṃ pitṝnt suvidatrā;m avitsi napātaṃ ca vikramaṇaṃ ca viṣṇoḥ |
(18.1.45.c) barhiṣado ye svadhayā sutasya bhajanta pitvas ta ihāgamiṣṭhāḥ ‖45‖
(18.1.46’) idaṃ pitṛbhyo namo astv adya ye pūrvāso ye aparāsa īyuḥ |
(18.1.46.c) ye pārthive rajasy ā niṣaktā ye vā nūnaṃ suvṛjanāsu dikṣu ‖46‖
(18.1.47’) mātalī kavyair yamo aṅgirobhir bṛhaspatir ṛkvabhir vāvṛdhānaḥ |
(18.1.47.c) yāṃś ca devā vāvṛdhur ye ca devāṃs te no 'vantu pitaro haveṣu ‖47‖
(18.1.48’) svāduṣ kilāyaṃ madhumā;m utāyaṃ tīvraḥ kilāyaṃ rasavā;m utāyam |
(18.1.48.c) uto nv asya papivāṃsam indraṃ na kaś cana sahata āhaveṣu ‖48‖
(18.1.49’) pareyivāṃsaṃ pravato mahīr iti bahubhyaḥ panthām anupaspaśānam |
(18.1.49.c) vaivasvataṃ saṃgamanaṃ janānāṃ yamaṃ rājānaṃ haviṣā saparyata ‖49‖
(18.1.50’) yamo no gātuṃ prathamo viveda naiṣā gavyūtir apabhartavā u |
(18.1.50.c) yatrā naḥ pūrve pitaraḥ paretā enā jajñānāḥ pathyā anu svāḥ ‖50‖ {5}
(18.1.51’) barhiṣadaḥ pitara ūty arvāg imā vo havyā cakṛmā juṣadhvam |
(18.1.51.c) ta ā gatāvasā śaṃtamenādhā naḥ śaṃ yor arapo dadhāta ‖51‖
(18.1.52’) ācyā jānu dakṣiṇato niṣadyedaṃ no havir abhi gṛṇantu viśve |
(18.1.52.c) mā hiṃsiṣṭa pitaraḥ kena cin no yad va āgaḥ puruṣatā karāma ‖52‖
(18.1.53’) tvaṣṭā duhitre vahatuṃ kṛṇoti tenedaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ sam eti |
(18.1.53.c) yamasya mātā paryuhyamānā maho jāyā vivasvato nanāśa ‖53‖
(18.1.54’) prehi prehi pathibhiḥ pūryāṇair yenā te pūrve pitaraḥ paretāḥ |
(18.1.54.c) ubhā rājānau svadhayā madantau yamaṃ paśyāsi varuṇaṃ ca devam ‖54‖
(18.1.55’) apeta vīta vi ca sarpatāto 'smā etaṃ pitaro lokam akran |
(18.1.55.c) ahobhir adbhir aktubhir vyaktaṃ yamo dadāty avasānam asmai ‖55‖
(18.1.56’) uśantas tvedhīmahy uśantaḥ sam idhīmahi |
(18.1.56.c) uśann uśata ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave ‖56‖
(18.1.57’) dyumantas tvedhīmahi dyumantaḥ sam idhīmahi |
(18.1.57.c) dyumān dyumata ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave ‖57‖
(18.1.58’) aṅgiraso naḥ pitaro navagvā atharvāṇo bhṛgavaḥ somyāsaḥ |
(18.1.58.c) teṣāṃ vayaṃ sumatau yajñiyānām api bhadre saumanase syāma ‖58‖
(18.1.59’) aṅgirobhir yajñiyair ā gahīha yama vairūpair iha mādayasva |
(18.1.59.c) vivasvantaṃ huve yaḥ pitā te 'smin barhiṣy ā niṣadya ‖59‖
(18.1.60’) imaṃ yama prastaram ā hi rohāṅgirobhiḥ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidānaḥ |
(18.1.60.c) ā tvā mantrāḥ kaviśastā vahantv enā rājan haviṣo mādayasva ‖60‖
(18.1.61’) ita eta udāruhan divas pṛṣṭhānv āruhan |
(18.1.61.c) pra bhūrjayo yathā pathā dyām aṅgiraso yayuḥ ‖61‖ {6}

(18.2.1’) yamāya somaḥ pavate yamāya kriyate haviḥ |
(18.2.1.c) yamaṃ ha yajño gachaty agnidūto araṃkṛtaḥ ‖1‖
(18.2.2’) yamāya madhumattamaṃ juhotā pra ca tiṣṭhata |
(18.2.2.c) idaṃ nama ṛṣibhyaḥ pūrvajebhyaḥ pūrvebhyaḥ pathikṛdbhyaḥ ‖2‖
(18.2.3’) yamāya ghṛtavat payo rājñe havir juhotana |
(18.2.3.c) sa no jīveṣv ā yamed dīrgham āyuḥ pra jīvase ‖3‖
(18.2.4’) mainam agne vi daho mābhi śūśuco māsya tvacaṃ cikṣipo mā śarīram |
(18.2.4.c) śṛtaṃ yadā karasi jātavedo 'them enaṃ pra hiṇutāt pitṝ;mr upa ‖4‖
(18.2.5’) yadā śṛtaṃ kṛṇavo jātavedo 'themam enaṃ pari dattāt pitṛbhyaḥ |
(18.2.5.c) yado gachāty asunītim etām atha devānāṃ vaśanīr bhavāti ‖5‖
(18.2.6’) trikadrukebhiḥ pavate ṣaḍ urvīr ekam id bṛhat |
(18.2.6.c) triṣṭub gāyatrī chandāṃsi sarvā tā yama ārpitā ‖6‖
(18.2.7’) sūryaṃ cakṣuṣā gacha vātam ātmanā divaṃ ca gacha pṛthivīṃ ca dharmabhiḥ |
(18.2.7.c) apo vā gacha yadi tatra te hitam oṣadhīṣu prati tiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ ‖7‖
(18.2.8’) ajo bhāgas tapasas taṃ tapasva taṃ te śocis tapatu taṃ te arciḥ |
(18.2.8.c) yās te śivās tanvo jātavedas tābhir vahainaṃ sukṛtām u lokam ‖8‖
(18.2.9’) yās te śocayo raṃhayo jātavedo yābhir āpṛṇāsi divam antarikṣam |
(18.2.9.c) ajaṃ yantam anu tāḥ sam ṛṇvatām athetarābhiḥ śivatamābhiḥ śṛtaṃ kṛdhi ‖9‖
(18.2.10’) ava sṛja punar agne pitṛbhyo yas ta āhutaś carati svadhāvān |
(18.2.10.c) āyur vasāna upa yātu śeṣaḥ saṃ gachatāṃ tanvā suvarcāḥ ‖10‖ {7}
(18.2.11’) ati drava śvānau sārameyau caturakṣau śabalau sādhunā pathā |
(18.2.11.c) adhā pitṝnt suvidatrā;m apīhi yamena ye sadhamādaṃ madanti ‖11‖
(18.2.12’) yau te śvānau yama rakṣitārau caturakṣau pathiṣadī nṛcakṣasā |
(18.2.12.c) tābhyāṃ rājan pari dhehy enaṃ svasty asmā anamīvaṃ ca dhehi ‖12‖
(18.2.13’) urūṇasāv asutṛpāv udumbalau yamasya dūtau carato janā;m anu |
(18.2.13.c) tāv asmabhyaṃ dṛśaye sūryāya punar dātām asum adyeha bhadram ‖13‖
(18.2.14’) soma ekebhyaḥ pavate ghṛtam eka upāsate |
(18.2.14.c) yebhyo madhu pradhāvati tāṃś cid evāpi gachatāt ‖14‖
(18.2.15’) ye cit pūrva ṛtasātā ṛtajātā ṛtāvṛdhaḥ |
(18.2.15.c) ṛṣīn tapasvato yama tapojā;m api gachatāt ‖15‖
(18.2.16’) tapasā ye anādhṛṣyās tapasā ye svar yayuḥ |
(18.2.16.c) tapo ye cakrire mahas tāṃś cid evāpi gachatāt ‖16‖
(18.2.17’) ye yudhyante pradhaneṣu śūrāso ye svar tanūtyajaḥ |
(18.2.17.c) ye vā sahasradakṣiṇās tāṃ cid evāpi gachatāt ‖17‖
(18.2.18’) sahasraṇīthāḥ kavayo ye gopāyanti sūryam |
(18.2.18.c) ṛṣīn tapasvato yama tapojā;m api gachatāt ‖18‖
(18.2.19’) syonāsmai bhava pṛthivy anṛkṣarā niveśanī |
(18.2.19.c) yachāsmai śarma saprathāḥ ‖19‖
(18.2.20’) asaṃbādhe pṛthivyā urau loke ni dhīyasva |
(18.2.20.c) svadhā yāś cakṛṣe jīvan tās te santu madhuścutaḥ ‖20‖ {8}
(18.2.21’) hvayāmi te manasā mana ihemān gṛhām upa jujuṣāṇa ehi |
(18.2.21.c) saṃ gachasva pitṛbhiḥ saṃ yamena syonās tvā vātā upa vāntu śagmāḥ ‖21‖
(18.2.22’) ut tvā vahantu maruta udavāhā udaprutaḥ |
(18.2.22.c) ajena kṛṇvantaḥ śītaṃ varṣeṇokṣantu bāl iti ‖22‖
(18.2.23’) ud ahvam āyur āyuṣe kratve dakṣāya jīvase |
(18.2.23.c) svān gachatu te mano adhā pitṝ;mr upa drava ‖23‖
(18.2.24’) mā te mano māsor māṅgānāṃ mā rasasya te |
(18.2.24.c) mā te hāsta tanvaḥ kiṃ caneha ‖24‖
(18.2.25’) mā tvā vṛkṣaḥ saṃ bādhiṣṭa mā devī pṛthivī mahī |
(18.2.25.c) lokaṃ pitṛṣu vittvaidhasva yamarājasu ‖25‖
(18.2.26’) yat te aṅgam atihitaṃ parācair apānaḥ prāṇo ya u vā te paretaḥ |
(18.2.26.c) tat te saṃgatya pitaraḥ sanīḍā ghāsād ghāsaṃ punar ā veśayantu ‖26‖
(18.2.27’) apemaṃ jīvā arudhan gṛhebhyas taṃ nir vahata pari grāmād itaḥ |
(18.2.27.c) mṛtyur yamasyāsīd dūtaḥ pracetā asūn pitṛbhyo gamayāṃ cakāra ‖27‖
(18.2.28’) ye dasyavaḥ pitṛṣu praviṣṭā jñātimukhā ahutādaś caranti |
(18.2.28.c) parāpuro nipuro ye bharanty agniṣ ṭān asmāt pra dhamāti yajñāt ‖28‖
(18.2.29’) saṃ viśantv iha pitaraḥ svā naḥ syonaṃ kṛṇvantah pratiranta āyuḥ |
(18.2.29.c) tebhyaḥ śakema haviṣā nakṣamāṇā jyog jīvantaḥ śaradaḥ purūcīḥ ‖29‖
(18.2.30’) yāṃ te dhenuṃ nipṛṇāmi yam u kṣīra odanam |
(18.2.30.c) tenā janasyāso bhartā yo 'trāsad ajīvanaḥ ‖30‖ {9}
(18.2.31’) aśvāvatīṃ pra tara yā suśevā rkṣākaṃ vā prataraṃ navīyaḥ |
(18.2.31.c) yas tvā jaghāna vadhyaḥ so astu mā so anyad vidata bhāgadheyam ‖31‖
(18.2.32’) yamaḥ paro 'varo vivasvān tataḥ paraṃ nāti paśyāmi kiṃ cana |
(18.2.32.c) yame adhvaro adhi me niviṣṭo bhuvo vivasvān anvātatāna ‖32‖
(18.2.33’) apāgūhann amṛtāṃ martyebhyaḥ kṛtvā savarṇām adadhur vivasvate |
(18.2.33.c) utāśvināv abharad yat tad āsīd ajahād u dvā mithunā saraṇyūḥ ‖33‖
(18.2.34’) ye nikhātā ye paroptā ye dagdhā ye coddhitāḥ |
(18.2.34.c) sarvāṃs tān agna ā vaha pitṝn haviṣe attave ‖34‖
(18.2.35’) ye agnidagdhā ye anagnidagdhā madhye divaḥ svadhayā mādayante |
(18.2.35.c) tvaṃ tān vettha yadi te jātavedaḥ svadhayā yajñaṃ svadhitiṃ juṣantām ‖35‖
(18.2.36’) śaṃ tapa māti tapo agne mā tanvaṃ tapaḥ |
(18.2.36.c) vaṇeṣu śuṣmo astu te pṛthivyām astu yad dharaḥ ‖36‖
(18.2.37’) dadāmy asmā avasānam etad ya eṣa āgan mama ced abhūd iha |
(18.2.37.c) yamaś cikitvān praty etad āha mamaiṣa rāya upa tiṣṭhatām iha ‖37‖
(18.2.38’) imāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18.2.38.c) śate śaratsu no purā ‖38‖
(18.2.39’) premāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18.2.39.c) śate śaratsu no purā ‖39‖
(18.2.40’) apemāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18.2.40.c) śate śaratsu no purā ‖40‖ {10}
(18.2.41’)  vīmāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18.2.41.c) śate śaratsu no purā ‖41‖
(18.2.42’) nir imāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18.2.42.c) śate śaratsu no purā ‖42‖
(18.2.43’) ud imāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18.2.43.c) śate śaratsu no purā ‖43‖
(18.2.44’) sam imāṃ mātrāṃ mimīmahe yathāparaṃ na māsātai |
(18.2.44.c) śate śaratsu no purā ‖44‖
(18.2.45’) amāsi mātrāṃ svar agām āyuṣmān bhūyāsam |
(18.2.45.c) yathāparaṃ na māsātai śate śaratsu no purā ‖45‖
(18.2.46’) prāṇo apāno vyāna āyuś cakṣur dṛśaye sūryāya |
(18.2.46.c) aparipareṇa pathā yamarājñaḥ pitṝn gacha ‖46‖
(18.2.47’) ye agravaḥ śaśamānāh pareyur hitvā dveṣāṃsy anapatyavantaḥ |
(18.2.47.c) te dyām udityāvidanta lokaṃ nākasya pṛṣṭhe adhi dīdhyānāḥ ‖47‖
(18.2.48’) udanvatī dyaur avamā pīlumatīti madhyamā |
(18.2.48.c) tṛtīyā ha pradyaur iti yasyāṃ pitara āsate ‖48‖
(18.2.49’) ye na pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā ya āviviśur urv antarikṣam |
(18.2.49.c) ya ākṣiyanti pṛthivīm uta dyāṃ tebhyaḥ pitṛbhyo namasā vidhema ‖49‖
(18.2.50’) idam id vā u nāparaṃ divi paśyasi sūryam |
(18.2.50.c) mātā putraṃ yathā sicābhy enaṃ bhūma ūrṇuhi ‖50‖ {11}
(18.2.51’) idam id vā u nāparaṃ jarasy anyad ito 'param |
(18.2.51.c) jāyā patim iva vāsasābhy enaṃ bhūma ūrṇuhi ‖51‖
(18.2.52’) abhi tvorṇomi pṛthivyā mātur vastreṇa bhadrayā |
(18.2.52.c) jīveṣu bhadraṃ tan mayi svadhā pitṛṣu sā tvayi ‖52‖
(18.2.53’) agnīṣomā pathikṛtā syonaṃ devebhyo ratnaṃ dadhathur vi lokam |
(18.2.53.c) upa preṣyantaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ yo vahāty añjoyānaiḥ pathibhis tatra gachatam ‖53‖
(18.2.54’) pūṣā tvetaś cyāvayatu pra vidvān anaṣṭapaśur bhuvanasya gopāḥ |
(18.2.54.c) sa tvaitebhyaḥ pari dadat pitṛbhyo 'gnir devebhyaḥ suvidatriyebhyaḥ ‖54‖
(18.2.55’) āyur viśvāyuḥ pari pātu tvā pūṣā tvā pātu prapathe purastāt |
(18.2.55.c) yatrāsate sukṛto yatra ta īyus tatra tvā devaḥ savitā dadhātu ‖55‖
(18.2.56’) imau yunajmi te vahnī asunītāya voḍhave |
(18.2.56.c) tābhyāṃ yamasya sādanaṃ samitiś cāva gachatāt ‖56‖
(18.2.57’) etat tvā vāsaḥ prathamaṃ nv āgann apaitad ūha yad ihābibhaḥ purā |
(18.2.57.c) iṣṭāpūrtam anusaṃkrāma vidvān yatra te dattaṃ bahudhā vibandhuṣu ‖57‖
(18.2.58’) agner varma pari gobhir vyayasva saṃ prorṇuṣva medasā pīvasā ca |
(18.2.58.c) net tvā dhṛṣṇur harasā jarhṛṣāṇo dadhṛg vidhakṣan parīṅkhayātai ‖58‖
(18.2.59’) daṇḍaṃ hastād ādadāno gatāsoḥ saha śrotreṇa varcasā balena |
(18.2.59.c) atraiva tvam iha vayaṃ suvīrā viśvā mṛdho abhimātīr jayema ‖59‖
(18.2.60’) dhanur hastād ādadāno mṛtasya saha kṣatreṇa varcasā balena |
(18.2.60.c) samāgṛbhāya vasu bhūri puṣṭam arvāṅ tvam ehy upa jīvalokam ‖60‖ {12}

(18.3.1’) iyaṃ nārī patilokaṃ vṛṇānā ni padyata upa tvā martya pretam |
(18.3.1.c) dharmaṃ purāṇam anupālayantī tasyai prajāṃ draviṇaṃ ceha dhehi ‖1‖
(18.3.2’) ud īrṣva nāry abhi jīvalokaṃ gatāsum etam upa śeṣa ehi |
(18.3.2.c) hastagrābhasya dadhiṣos tavedaṃ patyur janitvam abhi saṃ babhūtha ‖2‖
(18.3.3’) apaśyaṃ yuvatiṃ nīyamānāṃ jīvāṃ mṛtebhyaḥ pariṇīyamānām |
(18.3.3.c) andhena yat tamasā prāvṛtāsīt prākto apācīm anayaṃ tad enām ‖3‖
(18.3.4’) prajānaty aghnye jīvalokaṃ devānāṃ panthām anusaṃcarantī |
(18.3.4.c) ayaṃ te gopatis taṃ juṣasva svargaṃ lokam adhi rohayainam ‖4‖
(18.3.5’) upa dyām upa vetasam avattaro nadīnām |
(18.3.5.c) agne pittam apām asi ‖5‖
(18.3.6’) yaṃ tvam agne samadahas tam u nir vāpaya punaḥ |
(18.3.6.c) kyāmbūr atra rohatu śāṇḍadūrvā vyalkaśā ‖6‖
(18.3.7’) idaṃ ta ekam pura ū ta ekaṃ tṛtīyena jyotiṣā saṃ viśasva |
(18.3.7.c) saṃveśane tanvā cārur edhi priyo devānāṃ parame sadhasthe ‖7‖
(18.3.8’) ut tiṣṭha prehi pra dravaukaḥ kṛṇuṣva salile sadhasthe |
(18.3.8.c) tatra tvaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidānaḥ saṃ somena madasva saṃ svadhābhiḥ ‖8‖
(18.3.9’) pra cyavasva tanvaṃ saṃ bharasva mā te gātrā vi hāyi mo śarīram |
(18.3.9.c) mano niviṣṭam anusaṃviśasva yatra bhūmer juṣase tatra gacha ‖9‖
(18.3.10’) varcasā māṃ pitaraḥ somyāso añjantu devā madhunā ghṛtena |
(18.3.10.c) cakṣuṣe mā prataraṃ tārayanto jarase mā jaradaṣṭiṃ vardhantu ‖10‖ {13}
(18.3.11’) varcasā māṃ sam anaktv agnir medhāṃ me viṣṇur ny anaktv āsan |
(18.3.11.c) rayiṃ me viśve ni yachantu devāḥ syonā māpaḥ pavanaiḥ punantu ‖11‖
(18.3.12’) mitrāvaruṇā pari mām adhātām ādityā mā svaravo vardhayantu |
(18.3.12.c) varco ma indro ny anaktu hastayor jaradaṣṭiṃ mā savitā kṛṇotu ‖12‖
(18.3.13’) yo mamāra prathamo martyānāṃ yaḥ preyāya prathamo lokam etam |
(18.3.13.c) vaivasvataṃ saṃgamanaṃ janānāṃ yamaṃ rājānaṃ haviṣā saparyata ‖13‖
(18.3.14’) parā yāta pitara ā ca yātāyaṃ vo yajño madhunā samaktaḥ |
(18.3.14.c) datto asmabhyaṃ draviṇeha bhadraṃ rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ dadhāta ‖14‖
(18.3.15’) kaṇvaḥ kakṣīvān purumīḍho agastyaḥ śyāvāśvaḥ sobhary arcanānāḥ |
(18.3.15.c) viśvāmitro 'yaṃ jamadagnir atrir avantu naḥ kaśyapo vāmadevaḥ ‖15‖
(18.3.16’) viśvāmitra jamadagne vasiṣṭha bharadvāja gotama vāmadeva |
(18.3.16.c) śardir no atrir agrabhīn namobhiḥ susaṃśāsaḥ pitaro mṛḍatā naḥ ‖16‖
(18.3.17’) kasye mṛjānā ati yanti ripram āyur dadhānāḥ prataraṃ navīyaḥ |
(18.3.17.c) āpyāyamānāḥ prajayā dhanenādha syāma surabhayo gṛheṣu ‖17‖
(18.3.18’) añjate vy añjate sam añjate kratuṃ rihanti madhunābhy añjate |
(18.3.18.c) sindhor ucchvāse patayantam ukṣaṇaṃ hiraṇyapāvāḥ paśum āsu gṛhnate ‖18‖
(18.3.19’) yad vo mudraṃ pitaraḥ somyaṃ ca teno sacadhvaṃ svayaśaso hi bhūta |
(18.3.19.c) te arvāṇaḥ kavaya ā śṛṇota suvidatrā vidathe huyamānāḥ ‖19‖
(18.3.20’) ye atrayo aṅgiraso navagvā iṣṭāvanto rātiṣāco dadhānāḥ |
(18.3.20.c) dakṣiṇāvantaḥ sukṛto ya u sthāsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam ‖20‖ {14}
(18.3.21’) adhā yathā naḥ pitaraḥ parāsaḥ pratnāso agna ṛtam āśaśānāḥ |
(18.3.21.c) śucīd ayan dīdhyata ukthaśasaḥ kṣāmā bhindanto aruṇīr apa vran ‖21‖

(18.3.22.c) śucanto agniṃ vāvṛdhanta indram urvīm gavyāṃ pariṣadaṃ no akran ‖22‖
(18.3.23’) ā yūtheva kṣumati paśvo akhyad devānāṃ janimānty ugraḥ |
(18.3.23.c) martāsaś cid urvaśīr akṛpran vṛdhe cid arya uparasyāyoḥ ‖23‖
(18.3.24’) akarma te svapaso abhūma ṛtam avasrann uṣaso vibhātīḥ |
(18.3.24.c) viśvaṃ tad bhadraṃ yad avanti devā bṛhad vadema vidathe suvīrāḥ ‖24‖
(18.3.25’) indro mā marutvān prācyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari |
(18.3.25.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖25‖

(18.3.26.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖26‖
(18.3.27’) aditir mādityaiḥ pratīcyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari |
(18.3.27.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖27‖
(18.3.28’) somo mā viśvair devair udīcyā diśaḥ pātu bāhucyutā pṛthivī dyām ivopari |
(18.3.28.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖28‖
(18.3.29’) dhartā ha tvā dharuṇo dhārayātā ūrdhvaṃ bhānuṃ savitā dyām ivopari |
(18.3.29.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖29‖

(18.3.36’) dhartāsi dharuno 'si vaṃsago 'si ‖36‖

(18.3.38’) itaś ca māmutaś cāvatāṃ yame iva yatamāne yad aitam |
(18.3.38.c) pra vāṃ bharan mānuṣā devayanto ā sīdatāṃ svam u lokaṃ vidāne ‖38‖
(18.3.39’) svāsasthe bhavatam indave no yuje vāṃ brahma pūrvyaṃ namobhiḥ |
(18.3.39.c) vi śloka eti pathyeva sūriḥ śṛṇvantu viśve amṛtāsa etat ‖39‖
(18.3.40’) trīṇi padāni rupo anv arohac catuṣpadīm anv etad vratena |
(18.3.40.c) akṣareṇa prati mimīte arkam ṛtasya nābhāv abhi saṃ punāti ‖40‖ {16}
(18.3.41’) devebhyaḥ kam avṛṇīta mṛtyuṃ prajāyai kim amṛtaṃ nāvṛṇīta |
(18.3.41.c) bṛhaspatir yajñam atanuta ṛṣiḥ priyāṃ yamas tanvam ā rireca ‖41‖
(18.3.42’) tvam agna īḍito jātavedo 'vāḍ ḍhavyāni surabhīṇi kṛtvā |
(18.3.42.c) prādāḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhayā te akṣann addhi tvaṃ deva prayatā havīṃṣi ‖42‖
(18.3.43’) āsīnāso aruṇīnām upasthe rayiṃ dhatta dāśuṣe martyāya |
(18.3.43.c) putrebhyaḥ pitaras tasya vasvaḥ pra yachata ta ihorjaṃ dadhāta ‖43‖
(18.3.44’) agniṣvāttāḥ pitara eha gachata sadaḥsadaḥ sadata supraṇītayaḥ |
(18.3.44.c) atto havīṃṣi prayatāni barhiṣi rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ dadhāta ‖44‖
(18.3.45’) upahūtā naḥ pitaraḥ somyāso barhiṣyeṣu nidhiṣu priyeṣu |
(18.3.45.c) ta ā gamantu ta iha śruvantv adhi bruvantu te 'vantv asmān ‖45‖
(18.3.46’) ye naḥ pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā anūjahire somapīthaṃ vasiṣṭhāḥ |
(18.3.46.c) tebhir yamaḥ samrarāṇo havīṃṣy uśann uśadbhiḥ pratikāmam attu ‖46‖
(18.3.47’) ye tātṛṣur devatrā jehamānā hotrāvidaḥ stomataṣṭāso arkaiḥ |
