(18.3.48’) ye satyāso havirado haviṣpā indreṇa devaiḥ sarathaṃ tureṇa |
(18.3.48.c) āgne yāhi suvidatrebhir arvāṅ paraiḥ pūrvair ṛṣibhir gharmasadbhiḥ ‖48‖
(18.3.49’) upa sarpa mātaraṃ bhūmim etām uruvyacasaṃ pṛthivīṃ suśevām |
(18.3.49.c) ūrṇamradāḥ pṛthivī dakṣiṇāvata eṣā tvā pātu prapathe purastāt ‖49‖
(18.3.50’) uc chvañcasva pṛthivi mā ni bādhathāḥ sūpāyanāsmai bhava sūpasarpaṇā |
(18.3.50.c) mātā putraṃ yathā sicābhy enaṃ bhūma ūrṇuhi ‖50‖ {17}
(18.3.51’) ucchvañcamānā pṛthivī su tiṣṭhatu sahasraṃ mita upa hi śrayantām |
(18.3.51.c) te gṛhāso ghṛtaścutaḥ syonā viśvāhāsmai śaraṇāḥ santv atra ‖51‖
(18.3.52’) ut te stabhnāmi pṛthivīṃ tvat parīmaṃ logaṃ nidadhan mo aham riṣam |
(18.3.52.c) etāṃ sthūṇāṃ pitaro dhārayanti te tatra yamaḥ sādanā te kṛṇotu ‖52‖
(18.3.53’) imam agne camasaṃ mā vi jihvaraḥ priyo devānām uta somyānām |
(18.3.53.c) ayaṃ yaś camaso devapānas tasmin devā amṛtā mādayantām ‖53‖
(18.3.54’) atharvā pūrṇam camasam yam indrāyābibhar vājinīvate |
(18.3.54.c) tasmin kṛṇoti sukṛtasya bhakṣaṃ tasmin induḥ pavate viśvadānim ‖54‖
(18.3.55’) yat te kṛṣṇaḥ śakuna ātutoda pipīlaḥ sarpa uta vā śvāpadaḥ |
(18.3.55.c) agniṣ ṭad viśvād agadaṃ kṛṇotu somaś ca yo brāhmaṇā;m āviveśa ‖55‖
(18.3.56’) payasvatīr oṣadhayaḥ payasvan māmakaṃ payaḥ |
(18.3.56.c) apāṃ payaso yat payas tena mā saha śumbhatu ‖56‖
(18.3.57’) imā nārīr avidhavāḥ supatnīr āñjanena sarpiṣā saṃ spṛśantām |
(18.3.57.c) anaśravo anamīvāḥ suratnā ā rohantu janayo yonim agre ‖57‖
(18.3.58’) saṃ gachasva pitṛbhiḥ saṃ yameneṣṭāpūrtena parame vyoman |
(18.3.58.c) hitvāvadyaṃ punar astam ehi saṃ gachatāṃ tanvā suvarcāḥ ‖58‖
(18.3.59’) ye naḥ pituḥ pitaro ye pitāmahā ya āviviśur urv antarikṣam |
(18.3.59.c) tebhyaḥ svarād asunītir no adya vathāvaśaṃ tanvaḥ kalpayāti ‖59‖
(18.3.60’) śaṃ te nīhāro bhavatu śaṃ te pruṣvāva śīyatām |
(18.3.60.c) śītike śītikāvati hlādike hlādikāvati |
(18.3.60.e) maṇḍūky apsu śaṃ bhuva imaṃ sv agniṃ śamaya ‖60‖ {18}
(18.3.61’) vivasvān no abhayaṃ kṛṇotu yaḥ sutrāmā jīradānuḥ sudānuḥ |
(18.3.61.c) iheme vīrā bahavo bhavantu gomad aśvavan mayy astu puṣṭam ‖61‖
(18.3.62’) vivasvān no amṛtatve dadhātu paraitu mṛtyur amṛtaṃ na aitu |
(18.3.62.c) imān rakṣatu puruṣān ā jarimṇo mo sv eṣām asavo yamaṃ guḥ ‖62‖
(18.3.63’) yo dadhre antarikṣe na mahnā pitṝṇāṃ kaviḥ pramatir matīnām |

(18.3.65’) pra ketunā bṛhatā bhāty agnir ā rodasī vṛṣabho roravīti |
(18.3.65.c) divaś cid antād upamām ud ānaḍ apām upasthe mahiṣo vavardha ‖65‖
(18.3.66’) nāke suparṇam upa yat patantaṃ hrdā venanto abhyacakṣata tvā |
(18.3.66.c) hiraṇyapakṣaṃ varuṇasya dūtaṃ yamasya yonau śakunaṃ bhuranyum ‖66‖
(18.3.67’) indra kratuṃ na ā bhara pitā putrebhyo yathā |
(18.3.67.c) śikṣā ṇo asmin puruhūta yāmani jīvā jyotir aśīmahi ‖67‖
(18.3.68’) apūpāpihitān kumbhān yāṃs te devā adhārayan |
(18.3.68.c) te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutaḥ ‖68‖
(18.3.69’) yās te dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrā svadhāvatīḥ |
(18.3.69.c) tās te santu vibhvīḥ prabhvīs tās te yamo rājānu manyatām ‖69‖
(18.3.70’) punar dehi vanaspate ya eṣa nihitas tvayi |

(18.3.71’) ā rabhasva jātavedas tejasvad dharo astu te |
(18.3.71.c) śarīram asya saṃ dahāthainaṃ dehi sukṛtām u loke ‖71‖
(18.3.72’) ye te pūrve parāgatā apare pitaraś ca ye |
(18.3.72.c) tebhyo ghṛtasya kulyaitu śatadhārā vyundatī ‖72‖
(18.3.73’) etad ā roha vaya unmṛjānaḥ svā iha bṛhad u dīdayante |
(18.3.73.c) abhi prehi madhyato māpa hāsthāḥ pitṝnāṃ lokaṃ prathamo yo atra ‖73‖ {19}

(18.4.1’) ā rohata janitrīṃ jātavedasaḥ pitṛyānaiḥ saṃ va ā rohayāmi |
(18.4.1.c) avāḍ ḍhavyeṣito havyavāha ījānaṃ yuktāḥ sukṛtāṃ dhatta loke ‖1‖
(18.4.2’) devā yajñam ṛtavaḥ kalpayanti haviḥ puroḍāśaṃ sruco yajñāyudhāni |
(18.4.2.c) tebhir yāhi pathibhir devayānair yair ījānāḥ svargaṃ yanti lokam ‖2‖
(18.4.3’) ṛtasya panthām anu paśya sādhv aṅgirasaḥ sukṛto yena yanti |
(18.4.3.c) tebhir yāhi pathibhiḥ svargaṃ yatrādityā madhu bhakṣayanti tṛtīye nāke adhi vi śrayasva ‖3‖
(18.4.4’) trayaḥ suparṇā uparasya māyū nākasya pṛṣṭhe adhi viṣṭapi śritāḥ |
(18.4.4.c) svargā lokā amṛtena viṣṭhā iṣam ūrjaṃ yajamānāya duhrām ‖4‖
(18.4.5’) juhūr dādhāra dyām upabhṛd antarikṣaṃ dhruvā dādhāra pṛthivīṃ pratiṣṭhām |
(18.4.5.c) pratīmāṃ lokā ghṛtapṛṣṭhāḥ svargāḥ kāmaṃkāmaṃ yajamānāya duhrām ‖5‖
(18.4.6’) dhruva ā roha pṛthivīṃ viśvabhojasam antarikṣam upabhṛd ā kramasva |
(18.4.6.c) juhu dyāṃ gacha yajamānena sākaṃ sruveṇa vatsena diśaḥ prapīnāḥ sarvā dhukṣvāhṛṇyamānaḥ ‖6‖
(18.4.7’) tīrthais taranti pravato mahīr iti yajñakṛtaḥ sukṛto yena yanti |
(18.4.7.c) atrādadhur yajamānāya lokaṃ diśo bhūtāni yad akalpayanta ‖7‖
(18.4.8’) aṅgirasām ayanaṃ pūrvo agnir ādityānām ayanaṃ gārhapatyo dakṣiṇānām ayanaṃ dakṣiṇāgniḥ |
(18.4.8.c) mahimānam agner vihitasya brahmaṇā samaṅgaḥ sarva upa yāhi śagmaḥ ‖8‖
(18.4.9’) pūrvo agniṣ ṭvā tapatu śaṃ purastāc chaṃ paścāt tapatu gārhapatyaḥ |
(18.4.9.c) dakṣiṇāgniṣ ṭe tapatu śarma varmottarato madhyato antarikṣād diśodiśo agne pari pāhi ghorāt ‖9‖
(18.4.10’) yūyam agne śaṃtamābhis tanūbhir ījānam abhi lokaṃ svargam |
(18.4.10.c) aśvā bhūtvā pṛṣṭivāho vahātha yatra devaiḥ sadhamādaṃ madanti ‖10‖ {20}
(18.4.11’) śam agne paścāt tapa śaṃ purastāc cham uttarāc cham adharāt tapainam |
(18.4.11.c) ekas tredhā vihito jātavedaḥ samyag enaṃ dhehi sukṛtām u loke ‖11‖
(18.4.12’) śam agnayaḥ samiddhā ā rabhantāṃ prājāpatyaṃ medhyaṃ jātavedasaḥ |
(18.4.12.c) śṛtaṃ kṛṇvanta iha māva cikṣipan ‖12‖
(18.4.13’) yajña eti vitataḥ kalpamāna ījānam abhi lokaṃ svargam |
(18.4.13.c) tam agnayaḥ sarvahutaṃ juṣantāṃ prājāpatyaṃ medhyaṃ jātavedasaḥ ‖13‖
(18.4.14’) ījānaś citam ārukṣad agniṃ nākasya pṛṣṭhād divam utpatiṣyan |
(18.4.14.c) tasmai pra bhāti nabhaso jyotiṣīmānt svargaḥ panthāḥ sukṛte devayānaḥ ‖14‖
(18.4.15’) agnir hotādhvaryuṣ ṭe bṛhaspatir indro brahmā dakṣiṇatas te astu |
(18.4.15.c) huto 'yaṃ saṃsthito yajña eti yatra pūrvam ayanaṃ hutānām ‖15‖
(18.4.16’) apūpavān kṣīravāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.16.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖16‖
(18.4.17’) apūpavān dadhivāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.17.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖17‖
(18.4.18’) apūpavān drapsavāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.18.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖18‖
(18.4.19’) apūpavān ghṛtavāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.19.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖19‖
(18.4.20’) apūpavān māṃsavāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.20.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖20‖ {21}
(18.4.21’) apūpavān annavāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.21.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖21‖
(18.4.22’) apūpavān madhumāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.22.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖22‖
(18.4.23’) apūpavān rasavāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.23.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖23‖
(18.4.24’) apūpavān apavāṃś carur eha sīdatu |
(18.4.24.c) lokakṛtaḥ pathikṛto yajāmahe ye devānāṃ hutabhāgā iha stha ‖24‖
(18.4.25’) apūpāpihitān kumbhān yāṃs te devā adhārayan |
(18.4.25.c) te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutah ‖25‖
(18.4.26’) yās te dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrāḥ svadhāvatīḥ |
(18.4.26.c) tās te santūdbhvīḥ prabhvīs tās te yamo rājānu manyatām ‖26‖
(18.4.27’) akṣitiṃ bhūyasīm ‖27‖
(18.4.28’) drapsaś caskanda pṛthivīm anu dyām imaṃ ca yonim anu yaś ca pūrvaḥ |
(18.4.28.c) samānaṃ yonim anu samcarantaṃ drapsam juhomy anu sapta hotrāḥ ‖28‖
(18.4.29’) śatadhāraṃ vāyum arkaṃ svarvidaṃ nṛcakṣasas te abhi cakṣate rayim |
(18.4.29.c) ye pṛnanti pra ca yachanti sarvadā te duhrate dakṣiṇāṃ saptamātaram ‖29‖
(18.4.30’) kośaṃ duhanti kalaśaṃ caturbilam iḍāṃ dhenuṃ madhumatīṃ svastaye |
(18.4.30.c) ūrjaṃ madantīm aditiṃ janeṣv agne mā hiṃsīḥ parame vyoman ‖30‖ {22}
(18.4.31’) etat te devaḥ savitā vāso dadāti bhartave |
(18.4.31.c) tat tvaṃ yamasya rājye vasānas tārpyaṃ cara ‖31‖
(18.4.32’) dhānā dhenur abhavad vatso asyās tilo 'bhavat |
(18.4.32.c) tāṃ vai yamasya rājye akṣitām upa jīvati ‖32‖
(18.4.33’) etās te asau dhenavaḥ kāmadughā bhavantu |
(18.4.33.c) enīḥ śyenīḥ sarūpā virūpās tilavatsā upa tiṣṭhantu tvātra ‖33‖
(18.4.34’) enīr dhānā hariṇīḥ śyenīr asya kṛṣṇā dhānā rohiṇīr dhenavas te |
(18.4.34.c) tilavatsā ūrjam asmai duhānā viśvāhā santv anapasphurantīḥ ‖34‖
(18.4.35’) vaiśvānare havir idaṃ juhomi sāhasraṃ śatadhāram utsam |
(18.4.35.c) sa bibharti pitaraṃ pitāmahān prapitāmahān bibharti pinvamānaḥ ‖35‖
(18.4.36’) sahasradhāraṃ śatadhāram utsam akṣitaṃ vyacyamānaṃ salilasya pṛṣṭhe |
(18.4.36.c) ūrjaṃ duhānam anapasphurantam upāsate pitaraḥ svadhābhih ‖36‖
(18.4.37’) idaṃ kasāmbu cayanena citaṃ tat sajātā ava paśyateta |
(18.4.37.c) martyo 'yam amṛtatvam eti tasmai gṛhān kṛṇuta yāvatsabandhu ‖37‖
(18.4.38’) ihaivaidhi dhanasanir ihacitta ihakratuḥ |
(18.4.38.c) ihaidhi vīryavattaro vayodhā aparāhataḥ ‖38‖
(18.4.39’) putraṃ pautram abhitarpayantīr āpo madhumatīr imāḥ |
(18.4.39.c) svadhāṃ pitṛbhyo amṛtaṃ duhānā āpo devīr ubhayāṃs tarpayantu ‖39‖
(18.4.40’) āpo agniṃ pra hiṇuta pitṝ;mr upemaṃ yajñaṃ pitaro me juṣantām |
(18.4.40.c) āsīnām ūrjam upa ye sacante te no rayiṃ sarvavīraṃ ni yachān ‖40‖ {23}
(18.4.41’) sam indhate amartyaṃ havyavāhaṃ ghṛtapriyam |
(18.4.41.c) sa veda nihitān nidhīn pitṝn parāvato gatān ‖41‖
(18.4.42’) yaṃ te manthaṃ yam odananaṃ yan māṃsaṃ nipṛṇāmi te |
(18.4.42.c) te te santu svadhāvanto madhumanto ghṛtaścutaḥ ‖42‖
(18.4.43’) yās te dhānā anukirāmi tilamiśrāḥ svadhāvatīḥ |
(18.4.43.c) tās te santūdbhvīḥ prabhvīs tās te yamo rājānu manyatām ‖43‖
(18.4.44’) idaṃ pūrvam aparaṃ niyānaṃ yenā te pūrve pitaraḥ paretāḥ |
(18.4.44.c) purogavā ye abhisāco asya te tvā vahanti sukṛtām u lokam ‖44‖
(18.4.45’) sarasvatīṃ devayanto havante sarasvatīm adhvare tāyamāne |
(18.4.45.c) sarasvatīṃ sukṛto havante sarasvatī dāśuṣe vāryaṃ dāt ‖45‖
(18.4.46’) sarasvatīṃ pitaro havante dakṣiṇā yajñam abhinakṣamāṇāḥ |
(18.4.46.c) āsadyāsmin barhiṣi mādayadhvam anamīvā iṣa ā dhehy asme ‖46‖
(18.4.47’) sarasvati yā sarathaṃ yayāthokthaiḥ svadhābhir devi pitṛbhir madantī |
(18.4.47.c) sahasrārgham iḍo atra bhāgaṃ rāyas poṣaṃ yajamānāya dhehi ‖47‖
(18.4.48’) pṛthivīṃ tvā pṛthivyām ā veśayāmi devo no dhātā pra tirāty āyuḥ |
(18.4.48.c) parāparaitā vasuvid vo astv adhā mṛtāḥ pitṛṣu saṃ bhavantu ‖48‖
(18.4.49’) ā pra cyavethām apa tan mṛjethāṃ yad vām abhibhā atrocuḥ |
(18.4.49.c) asmād etam aghnyau tad vaśīyo dātuḥ pitṛṣv ihabhojanau mama ‖49‖
(18.4.50’) eyam agan dakṣiṇā bhadrato nā anena dattā sudughā vayodhāḥ |
(18.4.50.c) yauvane jīvān upapṛñcatī jarā pitṛbhya upasaṃparāṇayād imān ‖50‖ {24}
(18.4.51’) idaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ pra bharāmi barhir jīvaṃ devebhya uttaraṃ stṛṇāmi |
(18.4.51.c) tad ā roha puruṣa medhyo bhavan prati tvā jānantu pitarah paretam ‖51‖
(18.4.52’) edaṃ barhir asado medhyo 'bhūḥ prati tvā jānantu pitaraḥ paretam |
(18.4.52.c) yathāparu tanvaṃ saṃ bharasva gātrāṇi te brahmaṇā kalpayāmi ‖52‖
(18.4.53’) parṇo rājāpidhānaṃ carūṇām ūrjo balaṃ saha ojo na āgan |
(18.4.53.c) āyur jīvebhyo vidadhad dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ‖53‖
(18.4.54’) ūrjo bhāgo ya imaṃ jajānāśmānnānām ādhipatyaṃ jagāma |
(18.4.54.c) tam arcata viśvamitrā havirbhiḥ sa no yamaḥ prataraṃ jīvase dhāt ‖54‖
(18.4.55’) yathā yamāya harmyam avapan pañca mānavāḥ |
(18.4.55.c) evā vapāmi harmyaṃ yathā me bhūrayo 'sata ‖55‖
(18.4.56’) idaṃ hiraṇyaṃ bibhṛhi yat te pitābibhaḥ purā |
(18.4.56.c) svargaṃ yataḥ pitur hastaṃ nir mṛḍḍhi dakṣiṇam ‖56‖
(18.4.57’) ye ca jīvā ye ca mṛtā ye jātā ye ca yajñiyāḥ |
(18.4.57.c) tebhyo ghṛtasya kulyaitu madhudhārā vyundatī ‖57‖
(18.4.58’) vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavate vicakṣaṇaḥ sūro ahnāṃ pratarītoṣasāṃ divaḥ |
(18.4.58.c) prāṇaḥ sindhūnāṃ kalaśā;m acikradad indrasya hārdim āviśan manīṣayā ‖58‖
(18.4.59’) tveṣas te dhūma ūrṇotu divi ṣaṃ chukra ātataḥ |
(18.4.59.c) sūro na hi dyutā tvaṃ kṛpā pāvaka rocase ‖59‖
(18.4.60’) pra vā etīndur indrasya niṣkṛtiṃ sakhā sakhyur na pra mināti saṃgiraḥ |
(18.4.60.c) marya iva yoṣāḥ sam arṣase somaḥ kalaśe śatayāmanā pathā ‖60‖ {25}
(18.4.61’) akṣann amīmadanta hy ava priyā;m adhūṣata |
(18.4.61.c) astoṣata svabhānavo viprā yaviṣṭhā īmahe ‖61‖
(18.4.62’) ā yāta pitaraḥ somyāso gambhīraiḥ pathibhiḥ pitṛyāṇaiḥ |
(18.4.62.c) āyur asmabhyaṃ dadhataḥ prajāṃ ca rāyaś ca poṣair abhi naḥ sacadhvam ‖62‖
(18.4.63’) parā yāta pitaraḥ somyāso gambhīraiḥ pathibhiḥ pūryāṇaiḥ |
(18.4.63.c) adhā māsi punar ā yāta no gṛhān havir attuṃ suprajasaḥ suvīrāḥ ‖63‖
(18.4.64’) yad vo agnir ajahād ekam aṅgaṃ pitṛlokaṃ gamayaṃ jātavedāḥ |
(18.4.64.c) tad va etat punar ā pyāyayāmi sāṅgāḥ svarge pitaro mādayadhvam ‖64‖
(18.4.65’) abhūd dūtaḥ prahito jātavedāḥ sāyaṃ nyahna upavandyo nṛbhiḥ |
(18.4.65.c) prādāḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhayā te akṣann addhi tvaṃ deva prayatā havīṃṣi ‖65‖
(18.4.66’) asau hā iha te manaḥ kakutsalam iva jāmayaḥ |
(18.4.66.c) abhy enaṃ bhūma ūrṇuhi ‖66‖
(18.4.67’) śumbhantāṃ lokāḥ pitṛṣadanāḥ pitṛṣadane tvā loka ā sādayāmi ‖67‖
(18.4.68’) ye asmākaṃ pitaras teṣāṃ barhir asi ‖68‖
(18.4.69’) ud uttamaṃ varuṇa pāśam asmad avādhamaṃ śrathāya |
(18.4.69.c) adhā vayam āditya vrate tavānāgaso aditaye syāma ‖69‖
(18.4.70’) prāsmat pāśān varuṇa muñca sarvān yaih samāme badhyate yair vyāme |
(18.4.70.c) adhā jīvema śaradaṃ śatāni tvayā rājan gupitā rakṣamāṇāḥ ‖70‖ {26}
(18.4.71’) agnaye kavyavāhanāya svadhā namaḥ ‖71‖
(18.4.72’) somāya pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ ‖72‖
(18.4.73’) pitṛbhyaḥ somavadbhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ ‖73‖
(18.4.74’) yamāya pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ ‖74‖
(18.4.75’) etat te pratatāmaha svadhā ye ca tvām anu ‖75‖
(18.4.76’) etat te tatāmaha svadhā ye ca tvām anu ‖76‖
(18.4.77’) etat te tata svadhā ‖77‖
(18.4.78’) svadhā pitṛbhyaḥ pṛthiviṣadbhyaḥ ‖78‖
(18.4.79’) svadhā pitṛbhyo antarikṣasadbhyaḥ ‖79‖
(18.4.80’) svadhā pitṛbhyo diviṣadbhyaḥ ‖80‖ {27}
(18.4.81’) namo vaḥ pitara ūrje namo vaḥ pitaro rasāya ‖81‖
(18.4.82’) namo vaḥ pitaro bhāmāya namo vaḥ pitaro manyave ‖82‖
(18.4.83’) namo vaḥ pitaro yad ghoraṃ tasmai namo vaḥ pitaro yat krūraṃ tasmai ‖83‖
(18.4.84’) namo vaḥ pitaro yac chivaṃ tasmai namo vaḥ pitaro yat syonaṃ tasmai ‖84‖
(18.4.85’) namo vaḥ pitaraḥ svadhā vaḥ pitaraḥ ‖85‖
(18.4.86’) ye 'tra pitaraḥ pitaro ye 'tra yūyaṃ stha yuṣmāṃs te 'nu yūyaṃ teṣāṃ śreṣṭhā bhūyāstha ‖86‖
(18.4.87’) ya iha pitaro jīvā iha vayaṃ smaḥ |
(18.4.87.c) asmā;ms te 'nu vayaṃ teṣāṃ śreṣṭhā bhūyāsma ‖87‖
(18.4.88’) ā tvāgna idhīmahi dyumantaṃ devājaram |
(18.4.88.c) yad gha sā te panīyasī samid dīdayati dyavi |
(18.4.88.e) iṣaṃ stotṛbhya ā bhara ‖88‖
(18.4.89’) candramā apsv antar ā suparṇo dhāvate divi |
(18.4.89.c) na vo hiraṇyanemayaḥ padaṃ vindanti vidyuto vittaṃ me asya rodasī ‖89‖ {28}

(19.1.1’) saṃsaṃ sravantu nadyaḥ saṃ vātāḥ saṃ patatriṇaḥ |
(19.1.1.c) yajñam imaṃ vardhayatā giraḥ saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ‖1‖
(19.1.2’) imaṃ homā yajñam avatemaṃ saṃsrāvaṇā uta yajñam imaṃ vardhayatā giraḥ saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ‖2‖
(19.1.3’) rūpaṃrūpaṃ vayovayaḥ saṃrabhyainaṃ pari ṣvaje |
(19.1.3.c) yajñam imaṃ catasraḥ pradiśo vardhayantu saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ‖3‖

(19.2.1’) śaṃ ta āpo haimavatīḥ śam u te santūtsyāḥ |
(19.2.1.c) śaṃ te saniṣyadā āpaḥ śam u te santu varṣyāḥ ‖1‖
(19.2.2’) śaṃ ta āpo dhanvanyāḥ śaṃ te santv anūpyāḥ |
(19.2.2.c) śaṃ te khanitrimā āpaḥ śaṃ yāḥ kumbhebhir ābhṛtāḥ ‖2‖
(19.2.3’) anabhrayaḥ khanamānā viprā gambhīre apasaḥ |
(19.2.3.c) bhiṣagbhyo bhiṣaktarā āpo achā vadāmasi ‖3‖
(19.2.4’) apām aha divyānām apāṃ srotasyānām |
(19.2.4.c) apām aha praṇejane 'śvā bhavatha vājinaḥ ‖4‖
(19.2.5’) tā apaḥ śivā apo 'yakṣmaṃkaraṇīr apaḥ |
(19.2.5.c) yathaiva tṛpyate mayas tās ta ā datta bhesajīḥ ‖5‖

(19.3.1’) divas pṛthivyāḥ pary antarikṣād vanaspatibhyo adhy oṣadhībhyaḥ |
(19.3.1.c) yatrayatra vibhṛto jātavedās tata stuto juṣamāṇo na ehi ‖1‖
(19.3.2’) yas te apsu mahimā yo vaneṣu ya oṣadhīṣu paśuṣv apsv antaḥ |
(19.3.2.c) agne sarvās tanvaḥ saṃ rabhasva tābhir na ehi draviṇodā ajasraḥ ‖2‖
(19.3.3’) yas te deveṣu mahimā svargo yā te tanuḥ pitṛṣv āviveśa |
(19.3.3.c) puṣṭir yā te manuṣyeṣu paprathe 'gne tayā rayim asmāsu dhehi ‖3‖
(19.3.4’) śrutkarṇāya kavaye vedyāya vacobhir vākair upa yāmi rātim |
(19.3.4.c) yato bhayam abhayaṃ tan no astv ava devānāṃ yaja heḍo agne ‖4‖

(19.4.1’) yām āhutiṃ prathamām atharvā yā jātā yā havyam akṛṇoj jātavedāḥ |
(19.4.1.c) tāṃ ta etāṃ prathamo johavīmi tābhiṣ ṭupto vahatu havyam agnir agnaye svāha ‖1‖
(19.4.2’) ākūtiṃ devīṃ subhagāṃ puro dadhe cittasya mātā suhavā no astu |
(19.4.2.c) yām āśām emi kevalī sā me astu videyam enāṃ manasi praviṣṭām ‖2‖
(19.4.3’) ākūtyā no bṛhaspata ākūtyā na upā gahi |
(19.4.3.c) atho bhagasya no dhehy atho naḥ suhavo bhava ‖3‖
(19.4.4’) bṛhaspatir ma ākūtim āṅgirasaḥ prati jānātu vācam etām |
(19.4.4.c) yasya devā devatāḥ saṃbabhūvuḥ sa supraṇītāḥ kāmo anv etv asmān ‖4‖

(19.5.1’) indro rājā jagataś carṣaṇīnām adhi kṣami viṣurūpaṃ yad asti |
(19.5.1.c) tato dadāti dāśuṣe vasūni codad rādha upastutaś cid arvāk ‖1‖

(19.6.1’) sahasrabāhuḥ puruṣaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt |
(19.6.1.c) sa bhūmiṃ viśvato vṛtvāty atiṣṭhad daśāṅgulam ‖1‖
(19.6.2’) tribhiḥ padbhir dyām arohat pād asyehābhavat punaḥ |
(19.6.2.c) tathā vy akrāmad viṣvaṅ aśanānaśane anu ‖2‖
(19.6.3’) tāvanto asya mahimānas tato jyāyāṃś ca pūruṣaḥ |
(19.6.3.c) pādo 'sya viśvā bhūtāni tripād asyāmṛtaṃ divi ‖3‖
(19.6.4’) puruṣa evedaṃ sarvaṃ yad bhūtaṃ yac ca bhāvyam |
(19.6.4.c) utāmṛtatvasyeśvaro yad anyenābhavat saha ‖4‖
(19.6.5’) yat puruṣaṃ vy adadhuḥ katidhā vy akalpayan |
(19.6.5.c) mukhaṃ kim asya kim bāhū kim ūrū pādā ucyate ‖5‖
(19.6.6’) brāhmaṇo 'sya mukham āsīd bāhū rājanyo 'bhavat |
(19.6.6.c) madhyaṃ tad asya yad vaiśyaḥ padbhyāṃ śūdro ajāyata ‖6‖
(19.6.7’) candramā manaso jātaś cakṣoḥ sūryo ajāyata |
(19.6.7.c) mukhād indraś cāgniś ca prāṇād vāyur ajāyata ‖7‖
(19.6.8’) nābhyā āsīd antarikṣaṃ śīrṣṇo dyauḥ sam avartata |
(19.6.8.c) padbhyāṃ bhūmir diśaḥ śrotrāt tathā lokā;m akalpayan ‖8‖
(19.6.9’) virāḍ agre sam abhavad virājo adhi pūruṣaḥ |
(19.6.9.c) sa jāto aty aricyata paścād bhūmim atho puraḥ ‖9‖
(19.6.10’) yat puruṣeṇa haviṣā devā yajñam atanvata |
(19.6.10.c) vasanto asyāsīd ājyaṃ grīṣma idhmaḥ śarad dhaviḥ ‖10‖
(19.6.11’) taṃ yajñaṃ prāvṛṣā praukṣan puruṣaṃ jātam agraśaḥ |
(19.6.11.c) tena devā ayajanta sādhyā vasavaś ca ye ‖11‖
(19.6.12’) tasmād aśvā ajāyanta ye ca ke cobhayādataḥ |
(19.6.12.c) gāvo ha jajñire tasmāt tasmāj jātā ajāvayaḥ ‖12‖
(19.6.13’) tasmād yajñāt sarvahuta ṛcaḥ sāmāni jajñire |
(19.6.13.c) chando ha jajñire tasmād yajus tasmād ajāyata ‖13‖
(19.6.14’) tasmād yajñāt sarvahutaḥ saṃbhṛtaṃ pṛṣadājyam |
(19.6.14.c) paśūṃs tāṃś cakre vāyavyān āraṇyā grāmyāś ca ye ‖14‖
(19.6.15’) saptāsyāsan paridhayas triḥ sapta samidhaḥ kṛtāḥ |
(19.6.15.c) devā yad yajñaṃ tanvānā abadhnan puruṣaṃ paśum ‖15‖
(19.6.16’) mūrdhno devasya bṛhato aṃśavaḥ sapta saptatīḥ |
(19.6.16.c) rājñaḥ somasyājāyanta jātasya puruṣād adhi ‖16‖

(19.7.1’) citrāṇi sākaṃ divi rocanāni sarīsṛpāṇi bhuvane javāni |
(19.7.1.c) turmiśaṃ sumatim ichamāno ahāni gīrbhiḥ saparyāmi nākam ‖1‖
(19.7.2’) suhavam agne kṛttikā rohiṇī cāstu bhadraṃ mṛgaśiraḥ śam ārdrā |
(19.7.2.c) punarvasū sūnṛtā cāru puṣyo bhānur āśleṣā ayanaṃ maghā me ‖2‖
(19.7.3’) puṇyaṃ pūrvā phalgunyau cātra hastaś citrā śivā svāti sukho me astu |
(19.7.3.c) rādhe viśākhe suhavānurādhā jyeṣṭhā sunakṣatram ariṣṭa mūlam ‖3‖
(19.7.4’) annaṃ pūrvā rāsatāṃ me aṣādhā ūrjaṃ devy uttarā ā vahantu |
(19.7.4.c) abhijin me rāsatāṃ puṇyam eva śravaṇaḥ śraviṣṭhāḥ kurvatāṃ supuṣṭim ‖4‖
(19.7.5’) ā me mahac chatabhiṣag varīya ā me dvayā proṣṭhapadā suśarma |
(19.7.5.c) ā revatī cāśvayujau bhagaṃ ma ā me rayiṃ bharaṇya ā vahantu ‖5‖

(19.8.1’) yāni nakṣatrāṇi divy antarikṣe apsu bhūmau yāni nageṣu dikṣu |
(19.8.1.c) prakalpayaṃś candramā yāny eti sarvāṇi mamaitāni śivāni santu ‖1‖
(19.8.2’) aṣṭāviṃśāni śivāni śagmāni saha yogaṃ bhajantu me |
(19.8.2.c) yogaṃ pra padye kṣemaṃ ca kṣemaṃ pra padye yogaṃ ca namo 'horātrābhyām astu ‖2‖
(19.8.3’) svastitaṃ me suprātaḥ susāyaṃ sudivaṃ sumṛgaṃ suśakunaṃ me astu |
(19.8.3.c) suhavam agne svasty amartyaṃ gatvā punar āyābhinandan ‖3‖
(19.8.4’) anuhavaṃ parihavaṃ parivādaṃ parikṣavam |
(19.8.4.c) sarvair me riktakumbhān parā tānt savitaḥ suva ‖4‖
(19.8.5’) apapāpaṃ parikṣavaṃ puṇyaṃ bhakṣīmahi kṣavam |
(19.8.5.c) śivā te pāpa nāsikāṃ puṇyagaś cābhi mehatām ‖5‖
(19.8.6’) imā yā brahmaṇas pate viṣucīr vāta īrate |
(19.8.6.c) sadhrīcīr indra tāḥ kṛtvā mahyaṃ śivatamās kṛdhi ‖6‖
(19.8.7’) svasti no astv abhayaṃ no astu namo 'horatrābhyām astu ‖7‖

(19.9.1’) śāntā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntam idam urv antarikṣam |
(19.9.1.c) śāntā udanvatīr āpaḥ śāntā naḥ santv oṣadhīḥ ‖1‖
(19.9.2’) śāntāni pūrvarūpāṇi śāntaṃ no astu kṛtākṛtam |
(19.9.2.c) śāntaṃ bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca sarvam eva śam astu naḥ ‖2‖
(19.9.3’) iyaṃ yā parameṣṭhinī vāg devī brahmasaṃśitā |
(19.9.3.c) yayaiva sasṛje ghoraṃ tayaiva śāntir astu naḥ ‖3‖
(19.9.4’) idaṃ yat parameṣṭhinaṃ mano vāṃ brahmasaṃśitam |
(19.9.4.c) yenaiva sasṛje ghoraṃ tenaiva śāntir astu naḥ ‖4‖
(19.9.5’) imāni yāni pañcendriyāni manaḥṣaṣṭhāni me hṛdi brahmaṇā saṃśitāni |
(19.9.5.c) yair eva sasṛje ghoraṃ tair eva śāntir astu naḥ ‖5‖
(19.9.6’) śaṃ no mitraḥ śaṃ varuṇaḥ śaṃ viṣṇuḥ śaṃ prajāpatiḥ |
(19.9.6.c) śaṃ na indro bṛhaspatiḥ śaṃ no bhavatv aryamā ‖6‖
(19.9.7’) śaṃ no mitraḥ śaṃ varuṇaḥ śaṃ vivasvāṃ cham antakaḥ |
(19.9.7.c) utpātāḥ pārthivāntarikṣāḥ śaṃ no divicarā grahāḥ ‖7‖
(19.9.8’) śaṃ no bhūmir vepyamānā śam ulkā nirhataṃ ca yat |
(19.9.8.c) śaṃ gāvo lohitakṣīrāḥ śaṃ bhūmir ava tīryatīḥ ‖8‖
(19.9.9’) nakṣatram ulkābhihataṃ śam astu naḥ śaṃ no 'bhicārāḥ śam u santu kṛtyāḥ |
(19.9.9.c) śaṃ no nikhātā valgāḥ śam ulkā deśopasargāḥ śam u no bhavantu ‖9‖
(19.9.10’) śaṃ no grahāś cāndramasāḥ śam ādityaś ca rāhuṇā |
(19.9.10.c) śaṃ no mṛtyur dhūmaketuḥ śaṃ rudrās tigmatejasaḥ ‖10‖
(19.9.11’) śaṃ rudrāḥ śaṃ vasavaḥ śam ādityāḥ śam agnayaḥ |
(19.9.11.c) śaṃ no maharṣayo devāḥ śaṃ devāḥ śaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ ‖11‖
(19.9.12’) brahma prajāpatir dhātā lokā vedāḥ saptaṛṣayo 'gnayaḥ |
(19.9.12.c) tair me kṛtaṃ svastyayanam indro me śarma yachatu brahmā me śarma yachatu |
(19.9.12.e) viśve me devāḥ śarma yachantu sarve me devāḥ śarma yachantu ‖12‖
(19.9.13’) yāni kāni cic chāntāni loke saptaṛṣayo viduḥ |
(19.9.13.c) sarvāṇi śaṃ bhavantu me śaṃ me astv abhayaṃ me astu ‖13‖
(19.9.14’) pṛthivī śāntir antarikṣaṃ śāntir dyauḥ śāntir āpaḥ śāntir oṣadhayaḥ śāntir vanaspatayaḥ śānti
r viśve me devāḥ śāntiḥ sarve me devāḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntibhiḥ |
(19.9.14.c) yad iha ghoraṃ yad iha krūraṃ yad iha pāpaṃ tac chāntaṃ tac chivaṃ sarvam eva śam astu naḥ ‖14‖

(19.10.1’) śaṃ na indrāgnī bhavatām avobhiḥ śaṃ na indrāvaruṇā rātahavyā |
(19.10.1.c) śam indrāsomā suvitāya śaṃ yoḥ śaṃ na indrāpūṣaṇā vājasātau ‖1‖
(19.10.2’) śaṃ no bhagaḥ śam u naḥ śaṃso astu śaṃ naḥ puraṃdhiḥ śam u santu rāyaḥ |
(19.10.2.c) śaṃ naḥ satyasya suyamasya śaṃsaḥ śaṃ no aryamā purujāto astu ‖2‖
(19.10.3’) śaṃ no dhātā śam u dhartā no astu śaṃ na urūcī bhavatu svadhābhiḥ |
(19.10.3.c) śaṃ rodasī bṛhatī śaṃ no adriḥ śaṃ no devānāṃ suhavāni santu ‖3‖
(19.10.4’) śaṃ no agnir jyotiranīko astu śaṃ no mitrāvaruṇāv aśvinā śam |
(19.10.4.c) śaṃ naḥ sukṛtāṃ sukṛtāni santu śaṃ na iṣiro abhi vātu vātaḥ ‖4‖
(19.10.5’) śaṃ no dyāvāpṛthivī pūrvahūtau śam antarikṣaṃ dṛśaye no astu |
(19.10.5.c) śaṃ na oṣadhīr vanino bhavantu śaṃ no rajasas patir astu jiṣṇuḥ ‖5‖
(19.10.6’) śaṃ na indro vasubhir devo astu śam ādityebhir varuṇaḥ suśaṃsaḥ |
(19.10.6.c) śaṃ no rudro rudrebhir jalāṣaḥ śaṃ nas tvaṣṭā gnābhir iha śṛṇotu ‖6‖
(19.10.7’) śaṃ naḥ somo bhavatu brahma śaṃ naḥ śaṃ no grāvāṇaḥ śam u santu yajñāḥ |
(19.10.7.c) śaṃ naḥ svarūnāṃ mitayo bhavantu śaṃ naḥ prasvaḥ śam v astu vediḥ ‖7‖
(19.10.8’) śaṃ naḥ sūrya urucakṣā ud etu śaṃ no bhavantu pradiśaś catasraḥ |
(19.10.8.c) śaṃ naḥ parvatā dhruvayo bhavantu śaṃ naḥ sindhavaḥ śam u santv āpah ‖8‖
(19.10.9’) śaṃ no aditir bhavatu vratebhiḥ śaṃ no bhavantu marutaḥ svarkāḥ |
(19.10.9.c) śaṃ no viṣṇuḥ śam u pūṣā no astu śaṃ no bhavitraṃ śam v astu vāyuḥ ‖9‖
(19.10.10’) śaṃ no devaḥ savitā trāyamāṇaḥ śaṃ no bhavantūṣaso vibhātīḥ |
(19.10.10.c) śaṃ naḥ parjanyo bhavatu prajābhyaḥ śaṃ naḥ kṣetrasya patir astu śaṃbhuḥ ‖10‖

(19.11.1’) śaṃ naḥ satyasya patayo bhavantu śaṃ no arvantaḥ śam u santu gāvaḥ |
(19.11.1.c) śaṃ na ṛbhavaḥ sukṛtaḥ suhastāḥ śaṃ no bhavatu pitaro haveṣu ‖1‖
(19.11.2’) śaṃ no devā viśvadevā bhavantu śaṃ sarasvatī saha dhībhir astu |
(19.11.2.c) śam abhiṣācaḥ śam u rātiṣācaḥ śaṃ no divyāḥ pārthivāḥ śaṃ no apyāḥ ‖2‖
(19.11.3’) śaṃ no aja ekapād devo astu śam ahir budhnyaḥ śaṃ samudraḥ |
(19.11.3.c) śaṃ no apāṃ napāt perur astu śaṃ naḥ pṛṣṇir bhavatu devagopā ‖3‖
(19.11.4’) ādityā rudrā vasavo juṣantām idaṃ brahma kriyamāṇaṃ navīyaḥ |
(19.11.4.c) sṛṇvantu no divyāḥ pārthivāso gojātā uta ye yajñiyāsaḥ ‖4‖
(19.11.5’) ye devānām ṛtvijo yajñiyāso manor yajatrā amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ |
(19.11.5.c) te no rāsantām urugāyam adya yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ‖5‖
(19.11.6’) tad astu mitrāvaruṇā tad agne śaṃ yor asmabhyam idam astu śastam |
(19.11.6.c) aśīmahi gādham uta pratiṣṭhāṃ namo dive bṛhate sādanāya ‖6‖

(19.12.1’) uṣā apa svasus tamaḥ saṃ vartayati vartaniṃ sujātatā |
(19.12.1.c) ayā vājaṃ devahitaṃ sanema madema śatahimāḥ suvīrāḥ ‖1‖

(19.13.1’) indrasya bāhū sthavirau vṛṣāṇau citrā imā vṛṣabhau pārayiṣṇū |
(19.13.1.c) tau yokṣe prathamo yoga āgate yābhyāṃ jitam asurāṇāṃ svar yat ‖1‖
(19.13.2’) āśuḥ śiśāno vṛṣabho na bhīmo ghanāghanaḥ kṣobhaṇaś carṣanīnām |
(19.13.2.c) saṃkrandano 'nimiṣa ekavīraḥ śataṃ senā ajayat sākam indraḥ ‖2‖
(19.13.3’) saṃkrandanenānimiṣeṇa jiṣṇunāyodhyena duścyavanena dhṛṣṇunā |
(19.13.3.c) tad indreṇa jayata tat sahadhvaṃ yudho nara iṣuhastena vṛṣṇā ‖3‖
(19.13.4’) sa iṣuhastaiḥ sa niṣaṅgibhir vaśī saṃsraṣṭā sa yudha indro gaṇena |
(19.13.4.c) saṃsṛṣṭajit somapā bāhuśardhy ugradhanvā pratihitābhir astā ‖4‖
(19.13.5’) balavijñāyaḥ sthaviraḥ pravīraḥ sahasvān vājī sahamāna ugraḥ |
(19.13.5.c) abhivīro abhiṣatvā sahojij jaitram indra ratham ā tiṣṭha govidam ‖5‖
(19.13.6’) imaṃ vīram anu harṣadhvam ugram indraṃ sakhāyo anu saṃ rabhadhvam |
(19.13.6.c) grāmajitaṃ gojitaṃ vajrabāhuṃ jayantam ajma pramṛṇantam ojasā ‖6‖
(19.13.7’) abhi gotrāṇi sahasā gāhamāno 'dāya ugraḥ śatamanyur indraḥ |
(19.13.7.c) duścyavanaḥ pṛtanāṣāḍ ayodhyo 'smākaṃ senā avatu pra yutsu ‖7‖
(19.13.8’) bṛhaspate pari dīyā rathena rakṣohāmitrām apabādhamānaḥ |
(19.13.8.c) prabhañjaṃ chatrūn pramṛṇann amitrān asmākam edhy avitā tanūnām ‖8‖
(19.13.9’) indra eṣāṃ netā bṛhaspatir dakṣiṇā yajñaḥ pura etu somaḥ |
(19.13.9.c) devasenānām abhibhañjatīnāṃ jayantīnāṃ maruto yantu madhye ‖9‖
(19.13.10’) indrasya vṛṣṇo varuṇasya rājño ādityānāṃ marutāṃ śardha ugram |
(19.13.10.c) mahāmanasāṃ bhuvanacyavānāṃ ghoṣo devānāṃ jayatām ud asthāt ‖10‖
(19.13.11’) asmākam indraḥ samṛteṣu dhvajeṣv asmākaṣ yā iṣavas tā jayantu |
(19.13.11.c) asmākaṃ vīrā uttare bhavantv asmān devāso 'vatā haveṣu ‖11‖

(19.14.1’) idam ucchreyo 'vasānam āgāṃ śive me dyāvāpṛthivī abhūtām |
(19.14.1.c) asapatnāḥ pradiśo me bhavantu na vai tvā dviṣmo abhayaṃ no astu ‖1‖

(19.15.1’) yata indra bhayāmahe tato no abhayaṃ kṛdhi |
(19.15.1.c) maghavaṃ chagdhi tava tvaṃ na ūtibhir vi dviṣo vi mṛdho jahi ‖1‖
(19.15.2’) indraṃ vayam anūrādhaṃ havāmahe 'nu rādhyāsma dvipadā catuṣpadā |
(19.15.2.c) mā naḥ senā araruṣīr upa gur viṣūcir indra druho vi nāśaya ‖2‖
(19.15.3’) indras trātota vṛtrahā parasphāno vareṇyaḥ |
(19.15.3.c) sa rakṣitā caramataḥ sa madhyataḥ sa paścāt sa purastān no astu ‖3‖
(19.15.4’) uruṃ no lokam anu neṣi vidvānt svar yaj jyotir abhayaṃ svasti |
(19.15.4.c) ugrā ta indra sthavirasya bāhū upa kṣayema śaraṇā bṛhantā ‖4‖
(19.15.5’) abhayaṃ naḥ karaty antarikṣam abhayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime |
(19.15.5.c) abhayaṃ paścād abhayaṃ purastād uttarād adharād abhayaṃ no astu ‖5‖
(19.15.6’) abhayaṃ mitrād abhayam amitrād abhayaṃ jñātād abhayaṃ puro yaḥ |
(19.15.6.c) abhayaṃ naktam abhayaṃ divā naḥ sarvā āśā mama mitraṃ bhavantu ‖6‖

(19.16.1’) asapatnaṃ purastāt paścān no abhayaṃ kṛtam |
(19.16.1.c) savitā mā dakṣiṇata uttarān mā śacīpatiḥ ‖1‖
(19.16.2’) divo mādityā rakṣatu bhūmyā rakṣantv agnayaḥ |
(19.16.2.c) indrāgnī rakṣatāṃ mā purastād aśvināv abhitaḥ śarma yachatām |
(19.16.2.e) tiraścīn aghnyā rakṣatu jātavedā bhūtakṛto me sarvataḥ santu varma ‖2‖

(19.17.1’) agnir mā pātu vasubhiḥ purastāt tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.1.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖1‖
(19.17.2’) vāyur māntarikṣenaitasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.2.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖2‖
(19.17.3’) somo mā rudrair dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.3.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖3‖
(19.17.4’) varuṇo mādityair etasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.4.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānam pari dade svāha ‖4‖
(19.17.5’) sūryo mā dyāvāpṛthivībhyāṃ pratīcyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.5.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖5‖
(19.17.6’) āpo mauṣadhīmatīr etasyā diśaḥ pāntu tāsu krame tāsu śraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.6.c) tā mā rakṣantu tā mā gopāyantu tābhya ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖6‖
(19.17.7’) viśvakarmā mā saptaṛṣibhir udīcyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.7.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖7‖
(19.17.8’) indro mā marutvān etasyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.8.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖8‖
(19.17.9’) prajāpatir mā prajananavānt saha pratiṣṭhayā dhruvāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ p
raimi |
(19.17.9.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖9‖
(19.17.10’) bṛhaspatir mā viśvair devair ūrdhvāyā diśaḥ pātu tasmin krame tasmiṃ chraye tāṃ puraṃ praimi |
(19.17.10.c) sa mā rakṣatu sa mā gopāyatu tasmā ātmānaṃ pari dade svāha ‖10‖

(19.18.1’) agniṃ te vasuvantam ṛchantu |

(19.18.2.c) ye māghāyavaḥ etasyā diśo 'bhidāsān ‖2‖

(19.18.6.c) ye māghāyava etasyā diśo 'bhidāsān ‖6‖

(19.19.1’) mitraḥ pṛthivyod akrāmat tāṃ puraṃ pra ṇayāmi vaḥ |

(19.29.9’) jahi darbha sapatnān me jahi me pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19.29.9.c) jahi me sarvān durhārdo jahi me dviṣato maṇe ‖9‖

(19.30.1’) yat te darbha jarāmṛtyu śataṃ varmasu varma te |
(19.30.1.c) tenemaṃ varmiṇaṃ kṛtvā sapatnāṃ jahi vīryaiḥ ‖1‖
(19.30.2’) śataṃ te darbha varmāṇi sahasraṃ vīryāṇi te |
(19.30.2.c) tam asmai viśve tvāṃ devā jarase bhartavā aduḥ ‖2‖
(19.30.3’) tvām āhur devavarma tvāṃ darbha brahmaṇas patim |
(19.30.3.c) tvām indrasyāhur varma tvaṃ rāṣṭrāṇi rakṣasi ‖3‖
(19.30.4’) sapatnakṣayaṇaṃ darbha dviṣatas tapanaṃ hṛdah |
(19.30.4.c) maṇiṃ kṣatrasya vardhanaṃ tanūpānaṃ kṛṇomi te ‖4‖
(19.30.5’) yat samudro abhyakrandat parjanyo vidyutā saha |
(19.30.5.c) tato hiranyayo bindus tato darbho ajāyata ‖5‖

(19.31.1’) audumbareṇa maṇinā puṣṭikāmāya vedhasā |
(19.31.1.c) paśūṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ sphātiṃ goṣṭhe me savitā karat ‖1‖
(19.31.2’) yo no agnir gārhapatyaḥ paśūnām adhipā asat |
(19.31.2.c) audumbaro vṛso maṇiḥ saṃ mā sṛjatu puṣṭyā ‖2‖
(19.31.3’) karīṣiṇīṃ phalavatīṃ svadhām irāṃ ca no gṛhe |
(19.31.3.c) audumbarasya tejasā dhātā puṣṭiṃ dadhātu me ‖3‖
(19.31.4’) yad dvipāc ca catuṣpāc ca yāny annāni ye rasāḥ |
(19.31.4.c) gṛhṇe 'haṃ tv eṣāṃ bhūmānaṃ bibhrad audumbaraṃ maṇim ‖4‖
(19.31.5’) puṣṭiṃ paśūnām pari jagrabhāhaṃ catuṣpadāṃ dvipadāṃ yac ca dhānyam |
(19.31.5.c) payaḥ paśūnāṃ rasam oṣadhīnāṃ bṛhaspatiḥ savitā me ni yachāt ‖5‖
(19.31.6’) ahaṃ paśūnām adhipā asāni mayi puṣṭaṃ puṣṭapatir dadhātu |
(19.31.6.c) mahyam audumbaro maṇir draviṇāni ni yachatu ‖6‖
(19.31.7’) upa maudumbaro maṇiḥ prajayā ca dhanena ca |
(19.31.7.c) indreṇa jinvito maṇir ā māgant saha varcasā ‖7‖
(19.31.8’) devo maṇiḥ sapatnahā dhanasā dhanasātaye |
(19.31.8.c) paśor annasya bhūmānaṃ gavāṃ sphātiṃ ni yachatu ‖8‖
(19.31.9’) yathāgre tvaṃ vanaspate puṣṭhyā saha jajñiṣe |
(19.31.9.c) evā dhanasya me sphātim ā dadhātu sarasvatī ‖9‖
(19.31.10’) ā me dhanaṃ sarasvatī payasphātiṃ ca dhānyam |
(19.31.10.c) sinīvāly upā vahād ayaṃ caudumbaro maṇiḥ ‖10‖
(19.31.11’) tvaṃ maṇīṇām adhipā vṛṣāsi tvayi puṣṭaṃ puṣṭapatir jajāna |
(19.31.11.c) tvayīme vājā draviṇāni sarvaudumbaraḥ sa tvam asmat sahasvārād ārād arātim amatiṃ kṣudhaṃ ca ‖11‖
(19.31.12’) grāmaṇīr asi grāmaṇīr utthāya abhiṣikto 'bhi mā siñca varcasā |
(19.31.12.c) tejo 'si tejo mayi dhārayādhi rayir asi rayiṃ me dhehi ‖12‖
(19.31.13’) puṣṭir asi puṣṭyā mā sam aṅgdhi gṛhamedhī gṛhapatiṃ mā kṛṇu |
(19.31.13.c) audumbaraḥ sa tvam asmāsu dhehi rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ ni yacha rāyas poṣāya prati muñce ahaṃ tvām ‖1
3‖
(19.31.14’) ayam audumbaro maṇir vīro vīrāya badhyate |
(19.31.14.c) sa naḥ saniṃ madhumatīṃ kṛṇotu rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīram ni yachāt ‖14‖

(19.32.1’) śatakāṇḍo duścyavanaḥ sahasraparṇa uttiraḥ |
(19.32.1.c) darbho ya ugra oṣadhis taṃ te badhnāmy āyuṣe ‖1‖
(19.32.2’) nāsya keśān pra vapanti norasi tāḍam ā ghnate |
(19.32.2.c) yasmā achinnaparṇena darbhena śarma yachati ‖2‖
(19.32.3’) divi te tūlam oṣadhe pṛthivyām asi niṣṭhitaḥ |
(19.32.3.c) tvayā sahasrakāṇḍenāyuḥ pra vardhayāmahe ‖3‖
(19.32.4’) tisro divo aty atṛṇat tisra imāḥ pṛthivīr uta |
(19.32.4.c) tvayāhaṃ durhārdo jihvāṃ ni tṛṇadmi vacāṃsi ‖4‖
(19.32.5’) tvam asi sahamāno 'ham asmi sahasvān |
(19.32.5.c) ubhau sahasvantau bhūtvā sapatnān sahiṣīvahi ‖5‖
(19.32.6’) sahasva no abhimātiṃ sahasva pṛtanāyataḥ |
(19.32.6.c) sahasva sarvān durhārdaḥ suhārdo me bahūn kṛdhi ‖6‖
(19.32.7’) darbheṇa devajātena divi ṣṭambhena śaśvad it |
(19.32.7.c) tenāhaṃ śaśvato janā;m asanaṃ sanavāni ca ‖7‖
(19.32.8’) priyaṃ mā darbha kṛṇu brahmarājanyābhyām śūdrāya cāryāya ca |
(19.32.8.c) yasmai ca kāmayāmahe sarvasmai ca vipaśyate ‖8‖
(19.32.9’) yo jāyamānaḥ pṛthivīm adṛṃhad yo astabhnād antarikṣaṃ divaṃ ca |
(19.32.9.c) yaṃ bibhrataṃ nanu pāpmā viveda sa no 'yaṃ darbho varuṇo divā kaḥ ‖9‖
(19.32.10’) sapatnahā śatakāṇḍaḥ sahasvān oṣadhīnāṃ prathamaḥ saṃ babhūva |
(19.32.10.c) sa no 'yaṃ darbhaḥ pari pātu viśvatas tena sākṣīya pṛtanāḥ pṛtanyataḥ ‖10‖

(19.33.1’) sahasrārghaḥ śatakāṇḍaḥ payasvān apām agnir vīrudhāṃ rājasūyam |
(19.33.1.c) sa no 'yaṃ darbhaḥ pari pātu viśvato devo maṇir āyuṣā saṃ sṛjāti naḥ ‖1‖
(19.33.2’) ghṛtād ullupto madhumān payasvān bhūmidṛṃho 'cyutaś cyāvayiṣṇuḥ |
(19.33.2.c) nudant sapatnān adharāṃś ca kṛṇvan darbhā roha mahatām indriyeṇa ‖2‖
(19.33.3’) tvaṃ bhūmim aty eṣy ojasā tvaṃ vedyāṃ sīdasi cārur adhvare |
(19.33.3.c) tvāṃ pavitram ṛṣayo 'bharanta tvaṃ punīhi duritāny asmat ‖3‖
(19.33.4’) tīkṣṇo rājā viṣāsahī rakṣohā viśvacarṣaṇiḥ |
(19.33.4.c) ojo devānāṃ balam ugram etat taṃ te badhnāmi jarase svastaye ‖4‖
(19.33.5’) darbheṇa tvaṃ kṛṇavad vīryāṇi darbhaṃ bibhrad ātmanā mā vyathiṣṭhāḥ |
(19.33.5.c) atiṣṭhāya varcasādhānyānt sūrya ivā bhāhi pradiśaś catasraḥ ‖5‖

(19.34.1’) jāṅgiḍo 'si jaṅgiḍo rakṣitāsi jaṅgidaḥ |
(19.34.1.c) dvipāc catuṣpād asmākaṃ sarvaṃ rakṣatu jaṅgidaḥ ‖1‖
(19.34.2’) yā gṛtsyas tripañcāśīḥ śataṃ kṛtyākṛtaś ca ye |
(19.34.2.c) sarvān vinaktu tejaso 'rasāṃ jaṅgidas karat ‖2‖
(19.34.3’) arasaṃ kṛtrimaṃ nādam arasāḥ sapta visrasaḥ |
(19.34.3.c) apeto jaṅgiḍāmatim iṣum asteva śātaya ‖3‖
(19.34.4’) kṛtyādūṣaṇa evāyam atho arātidūṣaṇah |
(19.34.4.c) atho sahasvāñ jaṅgiḍaḥ pra na āyumṣi tāriṣat ‖4‖
(19.34.5’) sa jaṅgiḍasya mahimā pari ṇaḥ pātu viśvataḥ |
(19.34.5.c) viṣkandhaṃ yena sāsaha saṃskandham oja ojasā ‖5‖
(19.34.6’) triṣ ṭvā devā ajanayan niṣṭhitaṃ bhūmyām adhi |
(19.34.6.c) tam u tvāṅgirā iti brāhmaṇāḥ pūrvyā viduḥ ‖6‖
(19.34.7’) na tvā pūrvā oṣadhayo na tvā taranti yā navāḥ |
(19.34.7.c) vibādha ugro jaṅgiḍaḥ paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ ‖7‖
(19.34.8’) athopadāna bhagavo jāṅgiḍāmitavīrya |
(19.34.8.c) purā ta ugrā grasata upendro vīryaṃ dadau ‖8‖
(19.34.9’) ugra it te vanaspata indra ojmānam ā dadhau |
(19.34.9.c) amīvāḥ sarvāś cātayaṃ jahi rakṣāṃsy oṣadhe ‖9‖
(19.34.10’) āśarīkaṃ viśarīkaṃ balāsaṃ pṛṣṭyāmayam |
(19.34.10.c) takmānaṃ viśvaśāradam arasāṃ jaṅgiḍas karat ‖10‖

(19.35.1’) indrasya nāma gṛhṇanta ṛsayo jaṅgidaṃ daduḥ |
(19.35.1.c) devā yaṃ cakrur bheṣajam agre viṣkandhadūṣaṇam ‖1‖
(19.35.2’) sa no rakṣatu jaṅgiḍo dhanapālo dhaneva |
(19.35.2.c) devā yaṃ cakrur brāhmaṇāḥ paripāṇam arātiham ‖2‖
(19.35.3’) durhārdaḥ saṃghoraṃ cakṣuḥ pāpakṛtvānam āgamam |
(19.35.3.c) tāṃs tvaṃ sahasracakṣo pratībodhena nāśaya paripāṇo 'si jaṅgiḍaḥ ‖3‖
(19.35.4’) pari mā divaḥ pari mā pṛthivyāḥ pary antarikṣāt pari mā vīrudbhyaḥ |
(19.35.4.c) pari mā bhūtāt pari mota bhavyād diśodiśo jaṅgiḍaḥ pātv asmān ‖4‖
(19.35.5’) ya ṛṣṇavo devakṛtā ya uto vavṛte 'nyaḥ |
(19.35.5.c) sarvāṃ stān viśvabheṣajo 'rasāṃ jaṅgiḍas karat ‖5‖

(19.36.1’) śatavāro anīnaśad yakṣmān rakṣāṃsi tejasā |
(19.36.1.c) ārohan varcasā saha maṇir durṇāmacātanaḥ ‖1‖
(19.36.2’) śṛṅgābhyāṃ rakṣo nudate mūlena yātudhānyaḥ |
(19.36.2.c) madhyena yakṣmaṃ bādhate nainaṃ pāpmāti tatrati ‖2‖
(19.36.3’) ye yakṣmāso arbhakā mahānto ye ca śabdinaḥ |
(19.36.3.c) sarvāṃ durṇāmahā maṇiḥ śatavāro anīnaśat ‖3‖
(19.36.4’) śataṃ vīrān ajanayac chataṃ yakṣmān apāvapat |
(19.36.4.c) durṇāmnaḥ sarvān hatvāva rakṣāṃsi dhūnute ‖4‖
(19.36.5’) hiraṇyaśṛṅga ṛṣabhaḥ śātavāro ayaṃ maṇiḥ |
(19.36.5.c) durṇāmnaḥ sarvāṃs tṛdhvāva rakṣāṃsy akramīt ‖5‖
(19.36.6’) śatam ahaṃ durṇāmnīnāṃ gandharvāpsarasāṃ śatam |
(19.36.6.c) śatam śaśvanvatīnāṃ śatavāreṇa vāraye ‖6‖

(19.37.1’) idaṃ varco agninā dattam āgan bhargo yaśaḥ saha ojo vayo balam |
(19.37.1.c) trayastriṃśad yāni ca vīryāṇi tāny agniḥ pra dadātu me ‖1‖
(19.37.2’) varca ā dhehi me tanvāṃ saha ojo vayo balam |
(19.37.2.c) indriyāya tvā karmaṇe vīryāya prati gṛhṇāmi śataśāradāya ‖2‖
(19.37.3’) ūrje tvā balāya tvaujase sahase tvā |
(19.37.3.c) abhibhūyāya tvā rāṣṭrabhṛtyāya pary ūhāmi śataśāradāya ‖3‖
(19.37.4’) ṛtubhyaṣ ṭvārtavebhyo mādbhyaḥ saṃvatsarebhyaḥ |
(19.37.4.c) dhātre vidhātre samṛdhe bhūtasya pataye yaje ‖4‖

(19.38.1’) na taṃ yakṣmā arundhate nainaṃ śapatho aśnute |
(19.38.1.c) yaṃ bheṣajasya gulguloḥ surabhir gandho aśnute ‖1‖
(19.38.2’) viṣvañcas tasmād yakṣmā mṛgā aśvā iverate |
(19.38.2.c) yad gulgulu saindhavaṃ yad vāpy asi samudriyam ‖2‖
(19.38.3’) ubhayor agrabhaṃ nāmāsmā ariṣṭatātaye ‖3‖

(19.39.1’) aitu devas trāyamāṇaḥ kuṣṭho himavatas pari |
(19.39.1.c) takmānaṃ sarvaṃ nāśaya sarvāś ca yātudhānyah ‖1‖
(19.39.2’) trīṇi te kuṣṭha nāmāni nadyamāro nadyāriṣaḥ |
(19.39.2.c) nadyāyaṃ puruso riṣat |
(19.39.2.e) yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyaṃprātar atho divā ‖2‖
(19.39.3’) jīvalā nāma te mātā jīvanto nāma te pitā |
(19.39.3.c) nadyāyaṃ puruṣo riṣat |
(19.39.3.e) yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyaṃprātar atho divā ‖3‖
(19.39.4’) uttamo asy oṣadhīnām anaḍvān jagatām iva vyāghraḥ śvapadām iva |
(19.39.4.c) nadyāyaṃ puruṣo riṣat |
(19.39.4.e) yasmai paribravīmi tvā sāyaṃprātar atho divā ‖4‖
(19.39.5’) triḥ śāmbubhyo aṅgirebhyas trir ādityebhyas pari |
(19.39.5.c) trir jāto viśvadevebhyaḥ |
(19.39.5.e) sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākaṃ somena tiṣṭhati |
(19.39.6’) aśvattho devasadanas tṛtīyasyām ito divi |
(19.39.6.c) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata |
(19.39.6.e) sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākaṃ somena tiṣṭhati |
(19.39.7’) hiraṇyayī naur acarad dhiraṇyabandhanā divi |
(19.39.7.c) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata |
(19.39.7.e) sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajah sākaṃ somena tiṣṭhati |
(19.39.8’) yatra nāvaprabhraṃśanaṃ yatra himavataḥ śiraḥ |
(19.39.8.c) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ tataḥ kuṣṭho ajāyata |
(19.39.8.e) sa kuṣṭho viśvabheṣajaḥ sākaṃ somena tiṣṭhati |
(19.39.9’) yaṃ tvā veda pūrva ikṣvāko yaṃ vā tvā kuṣṭha kāmyaḥ |
(19.39.9.c) yaṃ vā vaso yam ātsyas tenāsi viśvabheṣajaḥ ‖9‖
(19.39.10’) śīrṣaśokaṃ tṛtīyakaṃ sadaṃdir yaś ca hāyanaḥ |
(19.39.10.c) takmānaṃ viśvadhāvīryādharāñcaṃ parā suva ‖10‖

(19.40.1’) yan me chidraṃ manaso yac ca vācaḥ sarasvatī manyumantaṃ jagāma |
(19.40.1.c) viśvais tad devaiḥ saha saṃvidānaḥ saṃ dadhātu bṛhaspatih ‖1‖
(19.40.2’) mā na āpo medhāṃ mā brahma pra mathiṣṭana |
(19.40.2.c) suṣyadā yūyaṃ syandadhvam upahūto 'haṃ sumedhā varcasvī ‖2‖
(19.40.3’) mā no medhāṃ mā no dīkṣāṃ mā no hiṃsiṣṭaṃ yat tapaḥ |
(19.40.3.c) śivā naḥ śaṃ santv āyuṣe śivā bhavantu mātaraḥ ‖3‖
(19.40.4’) yā naḥ pīparad aśvinā jyotiṣmatī tamas tiraḥ |
(19.40.4.c) tām asme rāsatām iṣam ‖4‖

(19.41.1’) bhadram ichanta ṛṣayaḥ svarvidas tapo dīkṣām upaniṣedur agre |
(19.41.1.c) tato rāṣṭraṃ balam ojaś ca jātaṃ tad asmai devā upasaṃnamantu ‖1‖

(19.42.1’) brahma hotā brahma yajñā brahmaṇā svaravo mitāḥ |
(19.42.1.c) adhvaryur brahmaṇo jāto brahmaṇo 'ntarhitaṃ haviḥ ‖1‖
(19.42.2’) brahma sruco ghṛtavatīr brahmaṇā vedir uddhitā |
(19.42.2.c) brahma yajñasya tattvaṃ ca ṛtvijo ye haviṣkṛtaḥ |
(19.42.2.e) śamitāya svāhā ‖2‖
(19.42.3’) aṃhomuce pra bhare manīṣām ā sutrāvṇe sumatim āvṛṇānaḥ |
(19.42.3.c) imam indra prati havyaṃ gṛbhāya satyāḥ santu yajamānasya kāmāḥ ‖3‖
(19.42.4’) amhomucaṃ vrṣabhaṃ yajñiyānāṃ virājantaṃ prathamam adhvarāṇam |
(19.42.4.c) apāṃ napātam aśvinā huve dhiya indriyeṇa ta indriyaṃ dattam ojaḥ ‖4‖

(19.43.1’) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19.43.1.c) agnir mā tatra nayatv agnir medhā dadhātu me |
(19.43.1.e) agnaye svāhā ‖1‖
(19.43.2’) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19.43.2.c) vāyur mā tatra nayatu vāyuḥ praṇān dadhātu me vāyave svāhā ‖2‖
(19.43.3’) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19.43.3.c) sūryo mā tatra nayatu cakṣuḥ sūryo dadhātu me |
(19.43.3.e) sūryāya svāhā ‖3‖
(19.43.4’) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19.43.4.c) candro mā tatra nayatu manaś candro dadhātu me |
(19.43.4.e) candrāya svāhā ‖4‖
(19.43.5’) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19.43.5.c) somo mā tatra nayatu payaḥ somo dadhātu me |
(19.43.5.e) somāya svāhā ‖5‖
(19.43.6’) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19.43.6.c) indro mā tatra nayatu balam indro dadhātu me |
(19.43.6.e) indrāya svāhā ‖6‖
(19.43.7’) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19.43.7.c) āpo mā tatra nayatv amṛtam mopa tiṣṭhatu |
(19.43.7.e) adbhyaḥ svāhā ‖7‖
(19.43.8’) yatra brahmavido yānti dīkṣayā tapasā saha |
(19.43.8.c) brahmā mā tatra nayatu brahmā brahma dadhātu me |
(19.43.8.e) brahmaṇe svāhā ‖8‖

(19.44.1’) āyuṣo 'si prataraṇaṃ vipraṃ bheṣajam ucyase |
(19.44.1.c) tad āñjana tvaṃ śaṃtāte śam āpo abhayaṃ kṛtam ‖1‖
(19.44.2’) yo harimā jāyānyo 'ṅgabhedo viṣalpakaḥ |
(19.44.2.c) sarvaṃ te yakṣmam aṅgebhyo bahir nir hantv āñjanam ‖2‖
(19.44.3’) āñjanaṃ pṛthivyāṃ jātaṃ bhadraṃ puruṣajīvanam |
(19.44.3.c) kṛṇotv apramāyukaṃ rathajūtim anāgasam ‖3‖
(19.44.4’) prāṇa prāṇaṃ trāyasvāso asave mṛḍa |
(19.44.4.c) nirṛte nirṛtyā naḥ pāśebhyo muñca ‖4‖
(19.44.5’) sindhor garbho 'si vidyutāṃ puṣpam |
(19.44.5.c) vātaḥ prāṇaḥ sūryaś cakṣur divas payaḥ ‖5‖
(19.44.6’) devāñjana traikakuda pari mā pāhi viśvataḥ |
(19.44.6.c) na tvā taranty oṣadhayo bāhyāḥ parvatīyā uta ‖6‖
(19.44.7’) vīdaṃ madhyam avāsṛpad rakṣohāmīvacātanaḥ |
(19.44.7.c) amīvāḥ sarvāś cātayan nāśayad abhibhā itaḥ ‖7‖
(19.44.8’) bahv idaṃ rājan varuṇānṛtam āha pūruṣaḥ |
(19.44.8.c) tasmāt sahasravīrya muñca naḥ pary aṃhasaḥ ‖8‖
(19.44.9’) yad āpo aghnyā iti varuṇeti yad ūcima |
(19.44.9.c) tasmāt sahasravīrya muñca naḥ pary aṃhasaḥ ‖9‖
(19.44.10’) mitraś ca tvā varuṇaś cānupreyatur āñjana |
(19.44.10.c) tau tvānugatya dūraṃ bhogāya punar ohatuḥ ‖10‖

(19.45.1’) ṛṇād ṛṇam iva saṃ naya kṛtyāṃ kṛtyākṛto gṛham |
(19.45.1.c) cakṣurmantrasya durhārdaḥ pṛṣṭīr api śṛṇāñjana ‖1‖
(19.45.2’) yad asmāsu duṣvapnyaṃ yad goṣu yac ca no gṛhe |
(19.45.2.c) anāmagas taṃ ca durhārdaḥ priyaḥ prati muñcatām ‖2‖
(19.45.3’) apām ūrja ojaso vāvṛdhānam agner jātam adhi jātavedasaḥ |
(19.45.3.c) caturvīraṃ parvatīyaṃ yad āñjanaṃ diśaḥ pradiśaḥ karad ic chivās te ‖3‖
(19.45.4’) caturvīraṃ badhyata āñjanaṃ te sarvā diśo abhayās te bhavantu |
(19.45.4.c) dhruvas tiṣṭhāsi saviteva cārya imā viśo abhi harantu te balim ‖4‖
(19.45.5’) ākṣvaikaṃ maṇim ekaṃ krṇuṣva snāhy ekenā pibaikam eṣām |
(19.45.5.c) caturvīraṃ nairṛtebhyaś caturbhyo grāhyā bandhebhyaḥ pari pātv asmān ‖5‖
(19.45.6’) agnir māgnināvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase |
(19.45.6.c) tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ‖6‖
(19.45.7’) indro mendriyeṇāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase |
(19.45.7.c) tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ‖7‖
(19.45.8’) somo mā saumyenāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase |
(19.45.8.c) tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ‖8‖
(19.45.9’) bhago ma bhagenāvatu prāṇāyāpānāyāyuṣe varcasa ojase |
(19.45.9.c) tejase svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ‖9‖
(19.45.10’) maruto mā gaṇair avantu prāṇāyāpānāyuṣe varcasa ojase tejase |
(19.45.10.c) svastaye subhūtaye svāhā ‖10‖

(19.46.1’) prajāpatiṣ ṭvā badhnāt prathamam astṛtaṃ vīryāya kam |
(19.46.1.c) tat te badhnāmy āyuṣe varcasa ojase ca balāya cāstṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖1‖
(19.46.2’) ūrdhvas tiṣṭhatu rakṣann apramādam astṛtemam mā tvā dabhan paṇayo yātudhānāḥ |
(19.46.2.c) indra iva dasyūn ava dhūnuṣva pṛtanyataḥ sarvāṃ chatrūn vi ṣahasvāstṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖2‖
(19.46.3’) śataṃ ca na praharanto nighnanto na tastire |
(19.46.3.c) tasminn indraḥ pary adatta cakṣuḥ prāṇam atho balam astṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖3‖
(19.46.4’) indrasya tvā varmaṇā pari dhāpayāmo yo devānām adhirājo babhūva |
(19.46.4.c) punas tvā devāḥ pra ṇayantu sarve 'stṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖4‖
(19.46.5’) asmin maṇāv ekaśataṃ vīryāṇi sahasraṃ prāṇā asminn astṛte |
(19.46.5.c) vyāghraḥ śatrūn abhi tiṣṭha sarvān yas tvā pṛtanyād adharaḥ so astv astṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖5‖
(19.46.6’) ghṛtād ullupto madhumān payasvānt sahasraprāṇaḥ śatayonir vayodhāḥ |
(19.46.6.c) śambhūś ca mayobhūś corjasvāṃś ca payasvāṃś cāstṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖6‖
(19.46.7’) yathā tvam uttaro 'so asapatnaḥ sapatnahā |
(19.46.7.c) sajātānām asad vaśī tathā tvā savitā karad astṛtas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖7‖

(19.47.1’) ā rātri pārthivaṃ rajaḥ pitur aprāyi dhāmabhiḥ |
(19.47.1.c) divaḥ sadāṃsi bṛhatī vi tiṣṭhasa ā tveṣaṃ vartate tamaḥ ‖1‖
(19.47.2’) na yasyāḥ pāraṃ dadṛśe na yoyuvad viśvam asyāṃ ni viśate yad ejati |
(19.47.2.c) ariṣṭāsas ta urvi tamasvati rātri pāram aśīmahi bhadre pāram aśīmahi ‖2‖
(19.47.3’) ye te rātri nṛcakṣaso draṣṭāro navatir nava |
(19.47.3.c) aśītiḥ santy aṣṭā uto te sapta saptatiḥ ‖3‖
(19.47.4’) ṣaṣṭiś ca ṣaṭ ca revati pañcāśat pañca sumnayi |
(19.47.4.c) catvāraś catvāriṃśac ca trayas triṃśac ca vājini ‖4‖
(19.47.5’) dvau ca te viṃśatiś ca te rātry ekādaśāvamāḥ |
(19.47.5.c) tebhir no adya pāyubhir nu pāhi duhitar divaḥ ‖5‖
(19.47.6’) rakṣā mākir no aghaśaṃsa īśata mā no duḥśaṃsa īśata |
(19.47.6.c) mā no adya gavāṃ steno māvīnāṃ vṛka īśata ‖6‖
(19.47.7’) māśvānāṃ bhadre taskaro mā nṛṇāṃ yātudhānyaḥ |
(19.47.7.c) paramebhiḥ pathibhi steno dhāvatu taskaraḥ |
(19.47.7.e) pareṇa datvatī rajjuḥ pareṇāghāyur arṣatu ‖7‖
(19.47.8’) adha rātri tṛṣṭadhūmam aśīrṣāṇam ahiṃ kṛṇu |
(19.47.8.c) hanū vṛkasya jambhayā stenaṃ drupade jahi ‖8‖
(19.47.9’) tvayi rātri vasāmasi svapiṣyāmasi jāgṛhi |
(19.47.9.c) gobhyo naḥ śarma yachāśvebhyaḥ puruṣebhyaḥ ‖9‖

(19.48.1’) atho yāni ca yasmā ha yāni cāntaḥ parīṇahi |
(19.48.1.c) tāni te pari dadmasi ‖1‖
(19.48.2’) rātri mātar uṣase naḥ pari dehi |
(19.48.2.c) uṣā no ahne pari dadātv ahas tubhyaṃ vibhāvari ‖2‖
(19.48.3’) yat kiṃ cedaṃ patayati yat kiṃ cedaṃ sarīsṛpam |
(19.48.3.c) yat kiṃ ca parvatāyāsatvaṃ tasmāt tvaṃ rātri pāhi naḥ ‖3‖
(19.48.4’) sā paścāt pāhi sā puraḥ sottarād adharād uta |
(19.48.4.c) gopāya no vibhāvari stotāras ta iha smasi ‖4‖
(19.48.5’) ye rātrim anutiṣṭhanti ye ca bhūteṣu jāgrati |
(19.48.5.c) paśūn ye sarvān rakṣanti te na ātmasu jāgrati te naḥ paśuṣu jāgrati ‖5‖
(19.48.6’) veda vai rātri te nāma ghṛtācī nāma vā asi |
(19.48.6.c) tāṃ tvāṃ bharadvājo veda sā no vitte 'dhi jāgrati ‖6‖

(19.49.1’) iṣirā yoṣā yuvatir damūnā rātrī devasya savitur bhagasya |
(19.49.1.c) aśvakṣabhā suhavā saṃbhṛtaśrīr ā paprau dyāvāpṛthivī mahitvā ‖1‖
(19.49.2’) ati viśvāny aruhad gambhiro varṣiṣṭham aruhanta śraviṣṭhāḥ |
(19.49.2.c) uśatī rātry anu sā bhadrābhi tiṣṭhate mitra iva svadhābhiḥ ‖2‖
(19.49.3’) varye vande subhage sujāta ājagan rātri sumanā iha syām |
(19.49.3.c) asmāṃs trāyasva naryāṇi jātā atho yāni gavyāni puṣṭyā ‖3‖
(19.49.4’) siṃhasya rātry uśatī pīṃṣasya vyāghrasya dvīpino varca ā dade |
(19.49.4.c) aśvasya bradhnaṃ puruṣasya māyuṃ puru rūpāṇi kṛṇuṣe vibhātī ‖4‖
(19.49.5’) śivāṃ rātrim anusūryaṃ ca himasya mātā suhavā no astu |
(19.49.5.c) asya stomasya subhage ni bodha yena tvā vande viśvāsu dikṣu ‖5‖
(19.49.6’) stomasya no vibhāvari rātri rājeva joṣase |
(19.49.6.c) asāma sarvavīrā bhavāma sarvavedaso vyuchantīr anūṣasaḥ ‖6‖
(19.49.7’) śamyā ha nāma dadhiṣe mama dipsanti ye dhanā |
(19.49.7.c) rātrīhi tān asutapā ya steno na vidyate yat punar na vidyate ‖7‖
(19.49.8’) bhadrāsi rātri camaso na viṣṭo viṣvaṅ gorūpaṃ yuvatir bibharṣi |
(19.49.8.c) cakṣuṣmatī me uśatī vapūmṣi prati tvaṃ divyā na kṣām amukthāḥ ‖8‖
(19.49.9’) yo adya stena āyaty aghāyur martyo ripuḥ |
(19.49.9.c) rātrī tasya pratītya pra grīvāḥ pra śiro hanat ‖9‖
(19.49.10’) pra pādau na yathāyati pra hastau na yathāśiṣat |
(19.49.10.c) yo malimlur upāyati sa saṃpiṣṭo apāyati |
(19.49.10.e) apāyati svapāyati śuṣke sthāṇāv apāyati ‖10‖

(19.50.1’) adha rātri tṛṣṭadhūmam aśīrṣāṇam ahiṃ kṛṇu |
(19.50.1.c) akṣau vṛkasya nir jahyās tena taṃ drupade jahi ‖1‖
(19.50.2’) ye te rātry anaḍvāhas tīkṣṇaśṛṅgāḥ svāśavaḥ |
(19.50.2.c) tebhir no adya pārayāti durgāṇi viśvahā ‖2‖
(19.50.3’) rātriṃrātrim ariṣyantas tarema tanvā vayam |
(19.50.3.c) gambhīram aplavā iva na tareyur arātayaḥ ‖3‖
(19.50.4’) yathā śāmyākaḥ prapatann apavān nānuvidyate |
(19.50.4.c) evā rātri pra pātaya yo asmā;m abhyaghāyati ‖4‖
(19.50.5’) apa stenaṃ vāsayo goajam uta taskaram |
(19.50.5.c) atho yo arvataḥ śiro 'bhidhāya ninīṣati ‖5‖
(19.50.6’) yad adya rātri subhage vibhajanty ayo vasu |
(19.50.6.c) yad etad asmān bhojaya yathed anyān upāyasi ‖6‖
(19.50.7’) uṣase naḥ pari dehi sarvān rātry anāgasaḥ |
(19.50.7.c) uṣā no ahne ā bhajād ahas tubhyaṃ vibhāvari ‖7‖

(19.51.1’) ayuto 'ham ayuto ma ātmāyutaṃ me cakṣur ayutaṃ me śrotram |
(19.51.1.c) ayuto me prāṇo 'yuto me 'pāno 'yuto me vyāno 'yuto 'haṃ sarvaḥ ‖1‖
(19.51.2’) devasya tvā savituḥ prasave 'śvinor bāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāṃ prasūta ā rabhe ‖2‖

(19.52.1’) kāmas tad agre sam avartata manaso retaḥ prathamaṃ yad āsīt |
(19.52.1.c) sa kāma kāmena bṛhatā sayonī rāyas poṣaṃ yajamānāya dhehi ‖1‖
(19.52.2’) tvaṃ kāma sahasāsi pratiṣṭhito vibhur vibhāvā sakha ā sakhīyate |
(19.52.2.c) tvam ugraḥ pṛtanāsu sasahiḥ saha ojo yajamānāya dhehi ‖2‖
(19.52.3’) dūrāc cakamānāya pratipāṇāyākṣaye |
(19.52.3.c) āsmā aśṛṇvann āśāḥ kāmenājanayant svaḥ ‖3‖
(19.52.4’) kāmena mā kāma āgan hṛdayād dhṛdayaṃ pari |
(19.52.4.c) yad amīṣām ado manas tad aitūpa mām iha ‖4‖
(19.52.5’) yat kāma kāmayamānā idaṃ kṛṇmasi te haviḥ |
(19.52.5.c) tan naḥ sarvaṃ sam ṛdhyatām athaitasya haviṣo vīhi svāhā ‖5‖

(19.53.1’) kālo aśvo vahati saptaraśmiḥ sahasrākṣo ajaro bhūriretāḥ |
(19.53.1.c) tam ā rohanti kavayo vipaścitas tasya cakrā bhuvanāni viśvā ‖1‖
(19.53.2’) sapta cakrān vahati kāla eṣa saptāsya nābhīr amṛtaṃ nv akṣaḥ |
(19.53.2.c) sa imā viśvā bhuvanāny añjat kālaḥ sa īyate prathamo nu devaḥ ‖2‖
(19.53.3’) pūrṇaḥ kumbho 'dhi kāla āhitas taṃ vai paśyāmo bahudhā nu santam |
(19.53.3.c) sa imā viśvā bhuvanāni pratyaṅ kālaṃ tam āhuḥ parame vyoman ‖3‖
(19.53.4’) sa eva saṃ bhuvanāny ābharat sa eva saṃ bhuvanāni pary ait |
(19.53.4.c) pitā sann abhavat putra eṣāṃ tasmād vai nānyat param asti tejaḥ ‖4‖
(19.53.5’) kālo 'mūṃ divam ajanayat kāla imāḥ pṛthivīr uta |
(19.53.5.c) kāle ha bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ceṣitaṃ ha vi tiṣṭhate ‖5‖
(19.53.6’) kālo bhūtim asṛjata kāle tapati sūryaḥ |
(19.53.6.c) kāle ha viśvā bhūtāni kāle cakṣur vi paśyati ‖6‖
(19.53.7’) kāle manaḥ kāle prāṇaḥ kāle nāma samāhitam |
(19.53.7.c) kālena sarvā nandanty āgatena prajā imāḥ ‖7‖
(19.53.8’) kāle tapaḥ kāle jyeṣṭham kāle brahma samāhitam |
(19.53.8.c) kālo ha sarvasyeśvaro yaḥ pitāsīt prajāpateḥ ‖8‖
(19.53.9’) teneṣitaṃ tena jātaṃ tad u tasmin pratiṣṭhitam |
(19.53.9.c) kālo ha brahma bhūtvā bibharti parameṣṭhinam ‖9‖
(19.53.10’) kālah prajā asṛjata kālo agre prajāpatim |
(19.53.10.c) svayaṃbhūḥ kaśyapaḥ kālāt tapaḥ kālād ajāyata ‖10‖

(19.54.1’) kālād āpaḥ sam abhavan kālād brahma tapo diśaḥ |
(19.54.1.c) kālenod eti sūryaḥ kāle ni viśate punaḥ ‖1‖
(19.54.2’) kālena vātaḥ pavate kālena pṛthivī mahī |
(19.54.2.c) dyaur mahī kāla āhitā ‖2‖
(19.54.3’) kālo ha bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ ca putro ajanayat purā |
(19.54.3.c) kālād ṛcaḥ sam abhavan yajuḥ kālād ajāyata ‖3‖
(19.54.4’) kālo yajñaṃ sam airayad devebhyo bhāgam akṣitam |
(19.54.4.c) kāle gandharvāpsarasaḥ kāle lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ ‖4‖
(19.54.5’) kāle 'yam aṅgirā devo 'tharvā cādhi tiṣṭhataḥ |
(19.54.5.c) imaṃ ca lokaṃ paramaṃ ca lokaṃ puṇyāṃś ca lokān vidhṛtīś ca puṇyāḥ |
(19.54.5.e) sarvāṃl lokān abhijitya brahmaṇā kālaḥ sa īyate paramo nu devaḥ ‖5‖

(19.55.1’) rātriṃrātrim aprayātaṃ bharanto 'śvāyeva tiṣṭhate ghāsam asmai |
(19.55.1.c) rāyas poṣeṇa sam iṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma ‖1‖
(19.55.2’) yā te vasor vāta iṣuḥ sā ta eṣā tayā no mṛḍa |
(19.55.2.c) rāyas poṣeṇa sam iṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma ‖2‖
(19.55.3’) sāyaṃsāyaṃ gṛhapatir no agniḥ prātaḥprātaḥ saumanasasya dātā |
(19.55.3.c) vasorvasor vasudāna edhi vayaṃ tvendhānās tanvaṃ puṣema ‖3‖
(19.55.4’) prātaḥprātar gṛhapatir no agniḥ sāyaṃsāyaṃ saumanasasya dātā |
(19.55.4.c) vasorvasor vasudāna edhīndhānās tvā śataṃhimā ṛdhema ‖4‖
(19.55.5’) apaścā dagdhānnasya bhūyāsam |
(19.55.5.c) annādāyānnapataye rudrāya namo agnaye |
(19.55.5.e) sabhyaḥ sabhāṃ me pāhi ye ca sabhyāḥ sabhāsadaḥ ‖5‖
(19.55.6’) tvām indrā puruhūta viśvam āyur vy aśnavan |
(19.55.6.c) aharahar balim it te haranto 'śvāyeva tiṣṭhate ghāsam agne ‖6‖

(19.56.1’) yamasya lokād adhy ā babhūvitha pramadā martyān pra yunakṣi dhīraḥ |
(19.56.1.c) ekākinā sarathaṃ yāsi vidvānt svapnaṃ mimāno asurasya yonau ‖1‖
(19.56.2’) bandhas tvāgre viśvacayā apaśyat purā rātryā janitor eke ahni |
(19.56.2.c) tataḥ svapnedam adhy ā babhūvitha bhiṣagbhyo rūpam apagūhamānaḥ ‖2‖
(19.56.3’) bṛhadgāvāsurebhyo 'dhi devān upāvartata mahimānam ichan |
(19.56.3.c) tasmai svapnāya dadhur ādhipatyaṃ trayastriṃśāsaḥ svar ānaśānāḥ ‖3‖
(19.56.4’) naitāṃ viduḥ pitaro nota devā yeṣāṃ jalpiś caraty antaredam |
(19.56.4.c) trite svapnam adadhur āptye nara ādityāso varuṇenānuśiṣṭāḥ ‖4‖
(19.56.5’) yasya krūram abhajanta duṣkṛto 'svapnena sukṛtaḥ puṇyam āyuḥ |
(19.56.5.c) svar madasi parameṇa bandhunā tapyamānasya manaso 'dhi jajñiṣe ‖5‖
(19.56.6’) vidma te sarvāḥ parijāḥ purastād vidma svapna yo adhipā ihā te |
(19.56.6.c) yaśaśvino no yaśaseha pāhy ārād dviṣebhir apa yāhi dūram ‖6‖

(19.57.1’) yathā kalāṃ yathā śaphaṃ yathā rṇaṃ samnayanti |
(19.57.1.c) evā duṣvapnyaṃ sarvam apriye saṃ nayāmasi ‖1‖
(19.57.2’) saṃ rājāno aguḥ sam ṛṇāmy aguḥ saṃ kuṣṭhā aguḥ saṃ kalā aguḥ |
(19.57.2.c) sam asmāsu yad duṣvapnyaṃ nir dviṣate duṣvapnyaṃ suvāma ‖2‖
(19.57.3’) devānāṃ patnīnāṃ garbha yamasya kara yo bhadraḥ svapna |
(19.57.3.c) sa mama yaḥ pāpas tad dviṣate pra hiṇmaḥ |
(19.57.3.e) mā tṛṣṭānām asi kṛṣṇaśakuner mukham ‖3‖
(19.57.4’) taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa tvaṃ svapnāśva iva kāyam aśva iva nīnāham |
(19.57.4.c) anāsmākaṃ devapīyuṃ piyāruṃ vapa yad asmāsu duṣvapnyaṃ yad goṣu yac ca no gṛhe ‖4‖
(19.57.5’) anāsmākas tad devapīyuḥ piyārur niṣkam iva prati muñcatām |
(19.57.5.c) navāratnīn apamayā asmākaṃ tataḥ pari |
(19.57.5.e) duṣvapnyaṃ sarvaṃ dviṣate nir dayāmasi ‖5‖

(19.58.1’) ghṛtasya jūtiḥ samanā sadevā saṃvatsaraṃ haviṣā vardhayantī |
(19.58.1.c) śrotraṃ cakṣuḥ prāṇo 'chinno no astv achinnā vayam āyuṣo varcasaḥ ‖1‖
(19.58.2’) upāsmān prāṇo hvayatām upa prāṇaṃ havāmahe |
(19.58.2.c) varco jagrāha pṛthivy antarikṣaṃ varcaḥ somo bṛhaspatir vidhattā ‖2‖
(19.58.3’) varcaso dyāvāpṛthivī saṃgrahaṇī babhūvathur varco gṛhītvā pṛthivīm anu saṃ carema |
(19.58.3.c) yaśasam gāvo gopatim upa tiṣṭhanty āyatīr yaśo gṛhītvā pṛthivīm anu saṃ carema ‖3‖
(19.58.4’) vrajaṃ kṛṇudhvaṃ sa hi vo nṛpāṇo varmā sīvyadhvaṃ bahulā pṛthūni |
(19.58.4.c) puraḥ kṛṇudhvam āyasīr adhṛṣṭā mā vaḥ susroc camaso dṛṃhata tam ‖4‖
(19.58.5’) yajñasya cakṣuḥ prabhṛtir mukhaṃ ca vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi |
(19.58.5.c) imaṃ yajñaṃ vitataṃ viśvakarmaṇā devā yantu sumanasyamānāḥ ‖5‖
(19.58.6’) ye devānām ṛtvijo ye ca yajñiyā yebhyo havyaṃ kriyate bhāgadheyam |
(19.58.6.c) imaṃ yajñaṃ saha patnībhir etya yāvanto devās taviṣā mādayantām ‖6‖

(19.59.1’) tvam agne vratapā asi deva ā martyeṣv ā |
(19.59.1.c) tvaṃ yajñeṣv īḍyaḥ ‖1‖
(19.59.2’) yad vo vayaṃ pramināma vratāni viduṣāṃ devā aviduṣṭarāsaḥ |
(19.59.2.c) agniṣ ṭad viśvād ā pṛṇātu vidvānt somasya yo brāhmaṇā;m āviveśa ‖2‖
(19.59.3’) ā devānām api panthām aganma yac chaknavāma tad anupravoḍhum |
(19.59.3.c) agnir vidvānt sa yajāt sa id dhotā so 'dhvarānt sa ṛtūn kalpayāti ‖3‖

(19.60.1’) vāṅ ma āsan nasoḥ prāṇaś cakṣur akṣṇoḥ śrotraṃ karṇayoḥ |

(19.60.2’) ūrvor ojo jaṅghayor javaḥ pādayoḥ |

(19.61.1’) tanūs tanvā me sahe dataḥ sarvam āyur aśīya |
(19.61.1.c) syonaṃ me sīda puruḥ pṛṇasva pavamānaḥ svarge ‖1‖

(19.62.1’) priyaṃ mā kṛṇu deveṣu priyaṃ rājasu mā kṛṇu |
(19.62.1.c) priyaṃ sarvasya paśyata uta śūdra utārye ‖1‖

(19.63.1’) ut tiṣṭha brahmaṇas pate devān yajñena bodhaya |

(19.64.1’) agne samidham āhārṣaṃ bṛhate jātavedase |

(19.64.2’) idhmena tvā jātavedaḥ samidhā vardhayāmasi |
(19.64.2.c) tathā tvam asmān vardhaya prajayā ca dhanena ca ‖2‖
(19.64.3’) yad agne yāni kāni cid ā te dārūṇi dadhmasi |
(19.64.3.c) sarvaṃ tad astu me śivaṃ taj juṣasva yaviṣṭhya ‖3‖
(19.64.4’) etās te agne samidhas tvam iddhaḥ samid bhava |
(19.64.4.c) āyur asmāsu dhehy amṛtatvam ācāryāya ‖4‖

(19.65.1’) hariḥ suparṇo divam āruho 'rciṣā ye tvā dipsanti divam utpatantam |

(19.66.1’) ayojālā asurā māyino 'yasmayaiḥ pāśair aṅkino ye caranti |

(19.72.1’) yasmāt kośād udabharāma vedaṃ tasminn antar ava dadhma enam |

(20.1.1’) indra tvā vṛṣabhaṃ vayaṃ sute some havāmahe |

(20.1.3.c) stomair vidhemāgnaye ‖3‖

(20.15.1’) pra maṃhiṣṭhāya bṛhate bṛhadraye satyaśuṣmāya tavase matiṃ bhare |

(20.16.1’) udapruto na vayo rakṣamāṇā vāvadato abhriyasyeva ghoṣāḥ |

(20.16.2’) saṃ gobhir aṅgiraso nakṣamāṇo bhaga ived aryamaṇaṃ nināya |

(20.17.1’) achā ma indraṃ matayaḥ svarvidaḥ sadhrīcīr viśvā uśatīr anūṣata |

(20.30.1’) pra te mahe vidathe śaṃsiṣaṃ harī pra te vanve vanuṣo haryataṃ madam |

(20.34.1’) yo jāta eva prathamo manasvān devo devān kratunā paryamūṣat |

(20.39.1’) indraṃ vo viśvatas pari havāmahe janebhyaḥ |

(20.137.14’) girā vajro na saṃbhṛtaḥ sabalo anapacyutaḥ |
(20.137.14.c) vavakṣa ṛṣvo astṛtaḥ ‖14‖

(20.138.1’) mahā;m indro ya ojasā parjanyo vṛṣṭimā;m iva |
(20.138.1.c) stomair vatsasya vāvṛdhe ‖1‖
(20.138.2’) prajām ṛtasya piprataḥ pra yad bharanta vahnayaḥ |
(20.138.2.c) viprā ṛtasya vāhasā ‖2‖
(20.138.3’) kaṇvāḥ indraṃ yad akrata stomair yajñasya sādhanam |
(20.138.3.c) jāmi bruvata āyudham ‖3‖

(20.139.1’) ā nūnam aśvinā yuvaṃ vatsasya gantam avase |
(20.139.1.c) prāsmai yachatam avṛkam pṛthu chardir yuyutaṃ yā arātayaḥ ‖1‖
(20.139.2’) yad antarikṣe yad divi yat pañca mānuṣā;m anu |
(20.139.2.c) nṛmnaṃ tad dhattam aśvinā ‖2‖
(20.139.3’) ye vām daṃsāṃsy aśvinā viprāsaḥ parimāmṛśuḥ |
(20.139.3.c) evet kāṇvasya bodhatam ‖3‖
(20.139.4’) ayaṃ vāṃ gharmo aśvinā stomena pari ṣicyate |
(20.139.4.c) ayaṃ somo madhumān vājinīvasū yena vṛtraṃ ciketathaḥ ‖4‖
(20.139.5’) yad apsu yad vanaspatau yad oṣadhīṣu purudaṃsasā kṛtam |
(20.139.5.c) tena māviṣṭam aśvinā ‖5‖

(20.140.1’) yan nāsatyā bhuraṇyatho yad vā deva bhiṣajyathaḥ |
(20.140.1.c) ayaṃ vāṃ vatso matibhir na vindhate haviṣmantaṃ hi gachathaḥ ‖1‖
(20.140.2’) ā nūnam aśvinor ṛṣi stomaṃ ciketa vāmayā |
(20.140.2.c) ā somaṃ madhumattamaṃ gharmaṃ siñcād atharvaṇi ‖2‖
(20.140.3’) ā nūnaṃ raghuvartaniṃ rathaṃ tiṣṭhātho aśvinā |
(20.140.3.c) ā vāṃ stomā ime mama nabho na cucyavīrata ‖3‖
(20.140.4’) yad adya vāṃ nāsatyokthair ācucyuvīmahi |
(20.140.4.c) yad vā vāṇībhir aśvinevet kaṇvasya bodhatam ‖4‖
(20.140.5’) yad vāṃ kakṣīvā;m uta yad vyaśva ṛṣir yad vāṃ dīrghatamā juhāva |
(20.140.5.c) pṛthī yad vāṃ vainyaḥ sādaneṣv eved ato aśvinā cetayethām ‖5‖

(20.141.1’) yātaṃ chardiṣpā uta paraspā bhūtaṃ jagatpā uta nas tanūpā |
(20.141.1.c) vartis tokāya tanayāya yātam ‖1‖
(20.141.2’) yad indreṇa sarathaṃ yātho aśvinā yad vā vāyunā bhavathaḥ samokasā |
(20.141.2.c) yad ādityebhir ṛbhubhiḥ sajoṣasā yad vā viṣṇor vikramaṇeṣu tiṣṭhathaḥ ‖2‖
(20.141.3’) yad adyāśvināv ahaṃ huveya vājasātaye |
(20.141.3.c) yat pṛtsu turvaṇe sanas tac chreṣṭham aśvinor avaḥ ‖3‖
(20.141.4’) ā nūnaṃ yātam aśvinemā havyāni vāṃ hitā |
(20.141.4.c) ime somāso adhi turvaśe yadāv ime kaṇveṣu vām atha ‖4‖
(20.141.5’) yan nāsatyā parāke arvāke asti bheṣajam |
(20.141.5.c) tena nūnaṃ vimadāya pracetasā chardir vatsāya yachatam ‖5‖

(20.142.1’) abhutsy u pra devyā sākaṃ vācāham aśvinoh |
(20.142.1.c) vy āvar devy ā matiṃ vi rātiṃ martyebhyaḥ ‖1‖
(20.142.2’) pra bodhayoṣo aśvinā pra devi sūnṛte mahi |
(20.142.2.c) pra yajñahotar ānuṣak pra madāya śravo bṛhat ‖2‖
(20.142.3’) yad uṣo yāsi bhānunā saṃ sūryeṇa rocase |
(20.142.3.c) ā hāyam aśvino ratho vartir yāti nṛpāyyam ‖3‖
(20.142.4’) yad āpītāso aṃśavo gāvo na duhra ūdhabhiḥ |
(20.142.4.c) yad vā vāṇīr anuṣata pra devayanto aśvinā ‖4‖
(20.142.5’) pra dyumnāya pra śavase pra nṛṣāhyāya śarmaṇe |
(20.142.5.c) pra dakṣāya pracetasā ‖5‖
(20.142.6’) yan nūnaṃ dhībhir aśvinā pitur yonā niṣīdathaḥ |
(20.142.6.c) yad vā sumnebhir ukthyā ‖6‖

(20.143.1’) taṃ vāṃ rathaṃ vayam adyā huvema pṛthujrayam aśvinā saṃgatiṃ goḥ |
(20.143.1.c) yaḥ sūryāṃ vahati vandhurāyur girvāhasaṃ purutamaṃ vasūyum ‖1‖
(20.143.2’) yuvaṃ śriyam aśvinā devatā tāṃ divo napātā vanathaḥ śacībhiḥ |
(20.143.2.c) yuvor vapur abhi pṛkṣaḥ sacante vahanti yat kakuhāso rathe vām ‖2‖
(20.143.3’) ko vām adyā karate rātahavya ūtaye vā sutapeyāya vārkaiḥ |
(20.143.3.c) ṛtasya vā vanuṣe pūrvyāya namo yemāno aśvinā vavartat ‖3‖
(20.143.4’) hiraṇyayena purubhū rathenemaṃ yajñaṃ nāsatyopa yātam |
(20.143.4.c) pibātha in madhunaḥ somyasya dadhatho ratnaṃ vidhate janāya ‖4‖
(20.143.5’) ā no yātaṃ divo acha pṛthivyā hiraṇyayena suvṛtā rathena |
(20.143.5.c) mā vām anye ni yaman devayantaḥ saṃ yad dade nābhiḥ pūrvyā vām ‖5‖
(20.143.6’) nū no rayiṃ puruvīraṃ bṛhantaṃ dasrā mimāthām ubhayeṣv asme |
(20.143.6.c) naro yad vām aśvinā stomam āvant sadhastutim ājamīlhāso agman ‖6‖
(20.143.7’) iheha yad vāṃ samanā papṛkṣe seyam asme sumatir vājaratnā |
(20.143.7.c) uruṣyataṃ jaritāraṃ yuvaṃ ha śritaḥ kāmo nāsatyā yuvadrik ‖7‖
(20.143.8’) madhumatīr oṣadhīr dyāva āpo madhuman no bhavatv antarikṣam |
(20.143.8.c) kṣetrasya patir madhumān no astv ariṣyanto anv enaṃ carema ‖8‖
(20.143.9’) panāyyaṃ tad aśvinā kṛtaṃ vāṃ vṛṣabho divo rajasaḥ pṛthivyāḥ |
(20.143.9.c) sahasraṃ śaṃsā uta ye gaviṣṭau sarvā;m it tā;m upa yātā pibadhyai ‖9‖
